[No.48] Probabilistic Conformal Prediction with Approximate Conditional Validity
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 近似条件有效性概率自举预测
类别: probabilistic methods (Bayesian methods, variational inference, sampling, UQ, etc.)
作者: Vincent Plassier;Alexander Fishkov;Mohsen Guizani;Maxim Panov;Eric Moulines;
机构: Ecole Polytechnique;Skolkovo Institute of Science and Technology;Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/29865
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01794
英摘: We develop a new method for generating prediction sets that combines the flexibility of conformal methods with an estimate of the conditional distribution $P_{Y \mid X}$. Existing methods, such as conformalized quantile regression and probabilistic conformal prediction, usually provide only a marginal coverage guarantee. In contrast, our approach extends these frameworks to achieve approximately conditional coverage, which is crucial for many practical applications. Our prediction sets adapt to the behavior of the predictive distribution, making them effective even under high heteroscedasticity. While exact conditional guarantees are infeasible without assumptions on the underlying data distribution, we derive non-asymptotic bounds that depend on the total variation distance of the conditional distribution and its estimate. Using extensive simulations, we show that our method consistently outperforms existing approaches in terms of conditional coverage, leading to more reliable statistical inference in a variety of applications.
中摘: 我们开发了一种新的方法来生成预测集，结合了容错方法的灵活性以及条件分布$P_{Y \mid X}$的估计。现有的方法，如容错分位回归和概率性容错预测，通常只能提供边际覆盖保证。相比之下，我们的方法扩展了这些框架，以实现大约条件覆盖，这对于许多实际应用至关重要。我们的预测集能够适应预测分布的行为，即使在高异方差性的情况下也能保持有效。虽然在没有假设底层数据分布的前提下无法获得精确的条件保证，但我们推导出了依赖于条件分布及其估计的非渐近界。通过广泛的模拟，我们展示了我们的方法在条件覆盖方面始终优于现有方法，从而在各种应用中提供了更可靠的统计推断。

[No.49] VEDIT: Latent Prediction Architecture For Procedural Video Representation Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: prediction
标题: VEDIT：潜在预测架构用于过程视频表示学习
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Han Lin;Tushar Nagarajan;Nicolas Ballas;Mido Assran;Mojtaba Komeili;Mohit Bansal;Koustuv Sinha;
机构: University of North Carolina at Chapel Hill;Meta;University of North Carolina;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30010
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03478
英摘: Procedural video representation learning is an active research area where the objective is to learn an agent which can anticipate and forecast the future given the present video input, typically in conjunction with textual annotations. Prior works often rely on large-scale pretraining of visual encoders and prediction models with language supervision. However, the necessity and effectiveness of extending compute intensive pretraining to learn video clip sequences with noisy text supervision have not yet been fully validated by previous works. In this work, we show that a strong off-the-shelf frozen pretrained visual encoder, along with a well designed prediction model, can achieve state-of-the-art (SoTA) performance in forecasting and procedural planning without the need for pretraining the prediction model, nor requiring additional supervision from language or ASR. Instead of learning representations from pixel space, our method utilizes the latent embedding space of publicly available vision encoders. By conditioning on frozen clip-level embeddings from observed steps to predict the actions of unseen steps, our prediction model is able to learn robust representations for forecasting through iterative denoising - leveraging the recent advances in diffusion transformers (Peebles & Xie, 2023). Empirical studies over a total of five procedural learning tasks across four datasets (NIV, CrossTask, COIN and Ego4D-v2) show that our model advances the strong baselines in long-horizon action anticipation (+2.6% in Verb ED@20, +3.1% in Noun ED@20), and significantly improves the SoTA in step forecasting (+5.0%), task classification (+3.8%), and procedure planning tasks (up to +2.28% in success rate, +3.39% in mAcc, and +0.90% in mIoU).
中摘: 程序化视频表示学习是一个活跃的研究领域，目标是训练一个代理能够在给定当前视频输入的情况下预测和预见未来，通常与文本注释相结合。以往的工作往往依赖于大规模视觉编码器和预测模型的语言监督预训练。然而，将计算密集型的预训练扩展到通过嘈杂的文本监督学习视频片段序列的必要性和有效性尚未得到先前工作的充分验证。在本工作中，我们展示了使用一个现成的强大冻结预训练视觉编码器，以及一个精心设计的预测模型，在不需要预训练预测模型或额外的语言或ASR监督的情况下，可以实现最先进的（SoTA）预测和程序化规划性能。我们的方法不是从像素空间学习表示，而是利用公开可用的视觉编码器的潜在嵌入空间。通过条件化已观察步骤的冻结片段级嵌入来预测未见步骤的动作，我们的预测模型能够通过迭代去噪学习稳健的表示，利用了扩散变换器（Peebles & Xie, 2023）的近期进展来进行预测。在四个数据集（NIV、CrossTask、COIN和Ego4D-v2）上的五个程序化学习任务的实证研究显示，我们的模型不仅在长时距动作预见方面改进了强大的基线（动词ED@20提高了2.6%，名词ED@20提高了3.1%），还在步骤预测方面显著提升了SoTA性能（提高了5.0%）、任务分类方面提高了SoTA性能（提高了3.8%），以及程序规划任务方面也有所提升（成功率最多提高了2.28%，mAcc提高了3.39%，mIoU提高了0.90%）。

[No.50] CBMA: Improving Conformal Prediction through Bayesian Model Averaging
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: prediction
标题: CBMA：通过贝叶斯模型平均提高同态预测性能
类别: probabilistic methods (Bayesian methods, variational inference, sampling, UQ, etc.)
作者: Pankaj Bhagwat;Linglong Kong;Bei Jiang;
机构: University of Alberta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30578
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16924
英摘: Conformal prediction has emerged as a popular technique for facilitating valid predictive inference across a spectrum of machine learning models, under minimal assumption of exchangeability. Recently, Hoff (2023) showed that full conformal Bayes provides the most efficient prediction sets (smallest by expected volume) among all prediction sets that are valid at the $(1 - α)$ level if the model is correctly specified. However, a critical issue arises when the Bayesian model itself may be mis-specified, resulting in prediction set that might be suboptimal, even though it still enjoys the frequentist coverage guarantee. To address this limitation, we propose an innovative solution that combines Bayesian model averaging (BMA) with conformal prediction. This hybrid not only leverages the strengths of Bayesian conformal prediction but also introduces a layer of robustness through model averaging. Theoretically, we prove that the resulting prediction set will converge to the optimal level of efficiency, if the true model is included among the candidate models. This assurance of optimality, even under potential model uncertainty, provides a significant improvement over existing methods, ensuring more reliable and precise uncertainty quantification.
中摘: 自适应预测作为一种在最小交换性假设下促进各种机器学习模型有效预测推断的技术而崭露头角。最近，霍夫（2023）证明，在模型正确指定的情况下，全自适应贝叶斯方法提供了在$(1 - \alpha)$水平上所有有效预测集中最高效的预测集（预期体积最小）。然而，当贝叶斯模型本身可能被误指定时，一个关键问题出现了，这可能导致预测集变得次优，尽管它仍然享有频繁主义覆盖率保证。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了一种创新解决方案，将贝叶斯模型平均（BMA）与自适应预测相结合。这种混合方法不仅利用了贝叶斯自适应预测的优势，还通过模型平均引入了一层稳健性。理论上，我们证明，如果真实模型包含在候选模型之中，所得到的预测集将收敛到最优效率水平。即使在潜在的模型不确定性情况下，这种优化性的保证也提供了一种相对于现有方法的重要改进，确保了更可靠和精确的不确定性量化。

[No.51] Any-step Dynamics Model Improves Future Predictions for Online and Offline Reinforcement Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 任意步动态模型提高在线和离线强化学习的未来预测能力
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Haoxin Lin;Yu-Yan Xu;Yihao Sun;Zhilong Zhang;Yi-Chen Li;Chengxing Jia;Junyin Ye;Jiaji Zhang;Yang Yu;
机构: Nanjing University;;Quebec Artificial Intelligence Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30099
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17031
英摘: Model-based methods in reinforcement learning offer a promising approach to enhance data efficiency by facilitating policy exploration within a dynamics model. However, accurately predicting sequential steps in the dynamics model remains a challenge due to the bootstrapping prediction, which attributes the next state to the prediction of the current state. This leads to accumulated errors during model roll-out. In this paper, we propose the Any-step Dynamics Model (ADM) to mitigate the compounding error by reducing bootstrapping prediction to direct prediction. ADM allows for the use of variable-length plans as inputs for predicting future states without frequent bootstrapping. We design two algorithms, ADMPO-ON and ADMPO-OFF, which apply ADM in online and offline model-based frameworks, respectively. In the online setting, ADMPO-ON demonstrates improved sample efficiency compared to previous state-of-the-art methods. In the offline setting, ADMPO-OFF not only demonstrates superior performance compared to recent state-of-the-art offline approaches but also offers better quantification of model uncertainty using only a single ADM.
中摘: 基于模型的方法在强化学习中提供了一种增强数据效率的有前景的途径，通过在动力学模型中促进策略探索。然而，由于动力学模型中的递归预测方式（即下一状态依赖于当前状态的预测），准确预测序列步骤仍然是一项挑战，这会导致在模型展开过程中累积误差。本文提出了一种任意步长动力学模型（Any-step Dynamics Model，ADM），通过减少递归预测转而采用直接预测来减轻累积误差。ADM允许使用不同长度的计划作为输入来预测未来状态，而无需频繁进行递归预测。我们设计了两种算法——ADMPO-ON和ADMPO-OFF，分别应用于在线和离线的基于模型框架中。在在线设置下，ADMPO-ON相比之前最先进的方法展示了更好的样本效率。在离线设置下，ADMPO-OFF不仅展示了优于最近先进离线方法的性能，还仅使用单一ADM就能更好地量化模型不确定性。

[No.53] A Multi-Power Law for Loss Curve Prediction Across Learning Rate Schedules
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 跨学习率调度的损失曲线预测多重幂律法则
类别: foundation or frontier models, including LLMs
作者: Kairong Luo;Haodong Wen;Shengding Hu;Zhenbo Sun;Zhiyuan Liu;Maosong Sun;Kaifeng Lyu;Wenguang Chen;
机构: ;Xi'an Jiao Tong University;Tsinghua University;University of California, Berkeley;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30030
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12811
英摘: Training large models is both resource-intensive and time-consuming, making it crucial to understand the quantitative relationship between model performance and hyperparameters. In this paper, we present an empirical law that describes how the pretraining loss of large language models evolves under different learning rate schedules, such as constant, cosine, and step decay schedules. Our proposed law takes a multi-power form, combining a power law based on the sum of learning rates and additional power laws to account for a loss reduction effect induced by learning rate decay. We extensively validate this law on various model sizes and architectures, and demonstrate that after fitting on a few learning rate schedules, the law accurately predicts the loss curves for unseen schedules of different shapes and horizons. Moreover, by minimizing the predicted final pretraining loss across learning rate schedules, we are able to find a schedule that outperforms the widely used cosine learning rate schedule. Interestingly, this automatically discovered schedule bears some resemblance to the recently proposed Warmup-Stable-Decay (WSD) schedule (Hu et al, 2024) but achieves a slightly lower final loss. We believe these results could offer valuable insights for understanding the dynamics of pretraining and designing learning rate schedules to improve efficiency.
中摘: 训练大型模型既耗费资源又耗时，因此理解模型性能与超参数之间的定量关系至关重要。在本文中，我们提出了一条经验定律，描述了在不同的学习率调度下（如恒定、余弦和步衰减调度）大型语言模型预训练损失的变化规律。我们提出的这条定律采用多幂形式，基于学习率之和的幂律，并结合了额外的幂律来解释由学习率衰减引起的损失减少效应。我们在各种模型规模和架构上广泛验证了这条定律，并证明在拟合少量的学习率调度后，该定律能够准确预测不同形状和时间范围的未见过的学习率调度下的损失曲线。此外，通过最小化预测的最终预训练损失来跨学习率调度进行优化，我们能够找到一个优于广泛使用的余弦学习率调度的调度方案。有趣的是，这个自动发现的调度方案与最近提出的Warmup-Stable-Decay（WSD）调度方案（Hu等人，2024）有些相似，但在最终损失方面略低一些。我们相信这些结果可以为理解预训练的动力学过程和设计提高效率的学习率调度提供有价值的见解。

[No.57] Kernel-based Optimally Weighted Conformal Time-Series Prediction
来源: ICLR 2025

英摘: In this work, we present a novel conformal prediction method for time-series, which we call Kernel-based Optimally Weighted Conformal Prediction Intervals (KOWCPI). Specifically, KOWCPI adapts the classic Reweighted Nadaraya-Watson (RNW) estimator for quantile regression on dependent data and learns optimal data-adaptive weights. Theoretically, we tackle the challenge of establishing a conditional coverage guarantee for non-exchangeable data under strong mixing conditions on the non-conformity scores. We demonstrate the superior performance of KOWCPI on real and synthetic time-series data against state-of-the-art methods, where KOWCPI achieves narrower confidence intervals without losing coverage.
中摘: 在本文中，我们提出了一种用于时间序列的新颖的齐性预测方法，称为核基于优化加权齐性预测区间（KOWCPI）。具体而言，KOWCPI 调整了经典的重加权 Nadaraya-Watson （RNW）估计器以适应依赖数据上的分位数回归，并学习最优的数据自适应权重。理论上，我们在强混合条件下处理了非可交换数据下建立条件覆盖保证的挑战。我们展示了在真实和合成的时间序列数据上，KOWCPI 在不损失覆盖的情况下比最先进的方法能够获得更窄的置信区间。

[No.58] WardropNet: Traffic Flow Predictions via Equilibrium-Augmented Learning
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: prediction
标题: WARDROPNet：通过均衡增强学习进行交通流量预测
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Kai Jungel;Dario Paccagnan;Axel Parmentier;Maximilian Schiffer;
机构: Technische Universität München;Imperial College London;Ecole Nationale des Ponts et Chaussees;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/30834
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06656
英摘: When optimizing transportation systems, anticipating traffic flows is a central element. Yet, computing such traffic equilibria remains computationally expensive. Against this background, we introduce a novel combinatorial optimization augmented neural network architecture that allows for fast and accurate traffic flow predictions. We propose WardropNet, a neural network that combines classical layers with a subsequent equilibrium layer: the first ones inform the latter by predicting the parameterization of the equilibrium problem's latency functions. Using supervised learning we minimize the difference between the actual traffic flow and the predicted output. We show how to leverage a Bregman divergence fitting the geometry of the equilibria, which allows for end-to-end learning. WardropNet outperforms pure learning-based approaches in predicting traffic equilibria for realistic and stylized traffic scenarios. On realistic scenarios, WardropNet improves on average for time-invariant predictions by up to 72% and for time-variant predictions by up to 23% over pure learning-based approaches.
中摘: 在优化运输系统时，预测交通流量是一个核心要素。然而，计算这样的交通均衡仍然非常耗费计算资源。在此背景下，我们提出了一种新颖的组合优化增强神经网络架构，能够快速且准确地进行交通流量预测。我们提出了WardropNet，这是一种结合了经典层和后续均衡层的神经网络：前者的预测信息用于指导后者解决均衡问题中的延时函数参数化问题。通过监督学习，我们最小化实际交通流量与预测输出之间的差异。我们展示了如何利用适合均衡几何结构的Bregman发散进行学习，这使得端到端的学习成为可能。在现实和简化交通场景中，WardropNet 在预测交通均衡方面优于纯粹基于学习的方法。在现实场景中，WardropNet 在时间不变预测方面的平均改进幅度高达72%，在时间变预测方面的改进幅度高达23%。

[No.59] Towards Auto-Regressive Next-Token Prediction: In-context Learning Emerges from Generalization
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 自回归下一个词预测：内省学习源自泛化能力
类别: learning theory
作者: Zixuan Gong;Xiaolin Hu;Huayi Tang;Yong Liu;
机构: Renmin University of China;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28825
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17024
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) abilities. However, existing theoretical analysis of ICL primarily exhibits two limitations: (a) Limited i.i.d. Setting. Most studies focus on supervised function learning tasks where prompts are constructed with i.i.d. input-label pairs. This i.i.d. assumption diverges significantly from real language learning scenarios where prompt tokens are interdependent. (b) Lack of Emergence Explanation. Most literature answers what ICL does from an implicit optimization perspective but falls short in elucidating how ICL emerges and the impact of pre-training phase on ICL. In our paper, to extend (a), we adopt a more practical paradigm, auto-regressive next-token prediction (AR-NTP), which closely aligns with the actual training of language models. Specifically, within AR-NTP, we emphasize prompt token-dependency, which involves predicting each subsequent token based on the preceding sequence. To address (b), we formalize a systematic pre-training and ICL framework, highlighting the layer-wise structure of sequences and topics, alongside a two-level expectation. In conclusion, we present data-dependent, topic-dependent and optimization-dependent PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds for pre-trained LLMs, investigating that ICL emerges from the generalization of sequences and topics. Our theory is supported by experiments on numerical linear dynamic systems, synthetic GINC and real-world language datasets.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）展示了显著的上下文内学习（ICL）能力。然而，现有的ICL理论分析主要存在两个局限性：(a) 有限的独立同分布（i.i.d.）设置。大多数研究集中在监督函数学习任务上，其中提示由独立同分布的输入-标签对构建。这种i.i.d.假设与实际的语言学习场景有很大偏差，在实际场景中，提示中的令牌是相互依赖的。(b) 缺乏涌现解释。大多数文献从隐式优化的角度回答了ICL做了什么，但在解释ICL是如何涌现出来的以及预训练阶段对ICL的影响方面做得不够。在我们的论文中，为了扩展(a)，我们采用了一种更实用的范式——自回归下一个令牌预测（AR-NTP），这与语言模型的实际训练更为接近。具体而言，在AR-NTP中，我们强调了提示令牌间的依赖性，即基于前一个序列来预测每个后续令牌。为了解决(b)，我们正式化了一个系统化的预训练和ICL框架，突出了序列和主题的逐层结构，并提出了两层期望。总之，我们为预训练LLMs提供了数据依赖、主题依赖和优化依赖的PAC-Bayesian泛化界，探讨了ICL如何源自序列和主题的泛化。我们的理论通过在数值线性动态系统、合成GINGC和真实世界语言数据集上的实验得到了支持。

[No.60] MeToken: Uniform Micro-environment Token Boosts Post-Translational Modification Prediction
来源: ICLR 2025
命中: prediction
标题: MeToken：均匀微环境增强翻译后修饰预测
类别: applications to physical sciences (physics, chemistry, biology, etc.)
作者: Cheng Tan;Zhenxiao Cao;Zhangyang Gao;Lirong Wu;Siyuan Li;Yufei Huang;Jun Xia;Bozhen Hu;Stan Z. Li;
机构: Zhejiang University;Xi'an Jiao Tong University;Westlake University;BioMap Research;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2025/poster/28395
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01856
英摘: Post-translational modifications (PTMs) profoundly expand the complexity and functionality of the proteome, regulating protein attributes and interactions that are crucial for biological processes. Accurately predicting PTM sites and their specific types is therefore essential for elucidating protein function and understanding disease mechanisms. Existing computational approaches predominantly focus on protein sequences to predict PTM sites, driven by the recognition of sequence-dependent motifs. However, these approaches often overlook protein structural contexts. In this work, we first compile a large-scale sequence-structure PTM dataset, which serves as the foundation for fair comparison. We introduce the MeToken model, which tokenizes the micro-environment of each amino acid, integrating both sequence and structural information into unified discrete tokens. This model not only captures the typical sequence motifs associated with PTMs but also leverages the spatial arrangements dictated by protein tertiary structures, thus providing a holistic view of the factors influencing PTM sites. Designed to address the long-tail distribution of PTM types, MeToken employs uniform sub-codebooks that ensure even the rarest PTMs are adequately represented and distinguished. We validate the effectiveness and generalizability of MeToken across multiple datasets, demonstrating its superior performance in accurately identifying PTM types. The results underscore the importance of incorporating structural data and highlight MeToken's potential in facilitating accurate and comprehensive PTM predictions, which could significantly impact proteomics research. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/A4Bio/MeToken.

[No.80] Mask-Enhanced Autoregressive Prediction: Pay Less Attention to Learn More
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 掩码增强自回归预测：少关注更多
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Xialie Zhuang;Zhikai Jia;Jianjin Li;Zhenyu Zhang;Li Shen;Zheng Cao;Shiwei Liu;
机构: University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Ubiquant;scitix;;University of Texas at Austin;Sun Yat-sen University;SCITIX (SGP) TECH PTE. LTD.;University of Oxford;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46344
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07490
英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) are discovered to suffer from accurately retrieving key information. To address this, we propose Mask-Enhanced Autoregressive Prediction (MEAP), a simple yet effective training paradigm that seamlessly integrates Masked Language Modeling (MLM) into Next-Token Prediction (NTP) to enhance the latter's in-context retrieval capabilities. Specifically, MEAP first randomly masks a small fraction of input tokens and then directly performs the standard next-token prediction autoregressive using a decoder-only Transformer. MEAP eliminates the need for bidirectional attention or encoder-decoder architectures for MLM, incurring no additional computational overhead during pre-training or inference. Intensive experiments demonstrate that MEAP substantially outperforms NTP on key information retrieval and long-context reasoning tasks, while performing on par or better on commonsense reasoning tasks. The benefits of MEAP also extend to supervised fine-tuning, where it shows remarkable advantages in lost-in-the-middle scenarios, outperforming NTP by 11.77 percentage points. Our analysis indicates that MEAP's effectiveness arises from its ability to promote more distinguishable attention scores by concentrating on a reduced set of non-masked tokens. This mechanism improves the model's focus on task-relevant signals while mitigating the influence of peripheral context. These findings position MEAP as a promising training paradigm for large language models.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）被发现存在准确检索关键信息的问题。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为Mask-Enhanced Autoregressive Prediction（MEAP）的简单而有效的训练范式，该范式将Masked Language Modeling（MLM）无缝集成到Next-Token Prediction（NTP）中，以增强其上下文检索能力。具体来说，MEAP首先随机掩蔽输入token的一小部分，然后直接使用解码器型Transformer进行标准的下一个token预测自回归过程。MEAP消除了在MLM中需要双向注意或编码器-解码器架构的需求，在预训练和推理过程中不增加额外的计算开销。密集的实验表明，MEAP在关键信息检索和长上下文推理任务上显著优于NTP，而在常识推理任务上表现相当甚至更好。MEAP的优势还延伸到监督微调场景，在“中间迷失”情况下显示出显著优势，比NTP高出11.77个百分点。我们的分析表明，MEAP的有效性来源于其通过集中关注较少的非掩蔽token来促进更具区分性的注意力分数的能力。这种机制提高了模型对任务相关信号的关注度，同时减轻了外围上下文的影响。这些发现使MEAP成为大型语言模型有前途的训练范式。

[No.94] Prediction models that learn to avoid missing values
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 预测模型学会避免缺失值
类别: general machine learning->supervised learning
作者: Lena Stempfle;Anton Matsson;Newton Mwai;Fredrik D. Johansson;
机构: Chalmers University of Technology;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43980
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03393
英摘: Handling missing values at test time is challenging for machine learning models, especially when aiming for both high accuracy and interpretability. Established approaches often add bias through imputation or excessive model complexity via missingness indicators. Moreover, either method can obscure interpretability, making it harder to understand how the model utilizes the observed variables in predictions. We propose missingness-avoiding (MA) machine learning, a general framework for training models to rarely require the values of missing (or imputed) features at test time. We create tailored MA learning algorithms for decision trees, tree ensembles, and sparse linear models by incorporating classifier-specific regularization terms in their learning objectives. The tree-based models leverage contextual missingness by reducing reliance on missing values based on the observed context. Experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that MA-DT, MA-LASSO, MA-RF, and MA-GBT effectively reduce the reliance on features with missing values while maintaining predictive performance competitive with their unregularized counterparts. This shows that our framework gives practitioners a powerful tool to maintain interpretability in predictions with test-time missing values.
中摘: 在测试时处理缺失值对机器学习模型来说是一个挑战，尤其是在追求高准确性和可解释性的同时。已有的方法往往通过插补或使用缺失性指示器增加模型复杂度，从而引入偏差。这两种方法都可能损害可解释性，使得理解模型如何利用观测变量进行预测变得更加困难。我们提出了缺失性避免（MA）机器学习，这是一种通用框架，用于训练模型在测试时很少需要缺失（或插补）特征的值。我们为决策树、树集合、稀疏线性模型创建了定制化的MA学习算法，在其学习目标中引入了特定分类器的正则化项。基于树的模型通过减少对缺失值的依赖来利用上下文中的缺失性。实验表明，MA-DT、MA-LASSO、MA-RF 和 MA-GBT 在减少对缺失特征的依赖性方面表现出色，同时保持与未正则化的对应模型相当的预测性能。这表明我们的框架为实践者提供了一个强大的工具，能够在测试时存在缺失值的情况下保持预测的可解释性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.106] Efficient and Scalable Density Functional Theory Hamiltonian Prediction through Adaptive Sparsity
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 通过自适应稀疏性实现的高效可扩展密度泛函理论哈密顿量预测
类别: applications->chemistry physics and earth sciences
作者: Erpai Luo;Xinran Wei;Lin Huang;Yunyang Li;Han Yang;Zaishuo Xia;Zun Wang;Chang Liu;Bin Shao;Jia Zhang;
机构: ;Microsoft;Yale University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45656
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01171
英摘: Hamiltonian matrix prediction is pivotal in computational chemistry, serving as the foundation for determining a wide range of molecular properties. While SE(3) equivariant graph neural networks have achieved remarkable success in this domain, their substantial computational cost--driven by high-order tensor product (TP) operations--restricts their scalability to large molecular systems with extensive basis sets. To address this challenge, we introduce SPHNet, an efficient and scalable equivariant network, that incorporates adaptive SParsity into Hamiltonian prediction. SPHNet employs two innovative sparse gates to selectively constrain non-critical interaction combinations, significantly reducing tensor product computations while maintaining accuracy. To optimize the sparse representation, we develop a Three-phase Sparsity Scheduler, ensuring stable convergence and achieving high performance at sparsity rates of up to 70%. Extensive evaluations on QH9 and PubchemQH datasets demonstrate that SPHNet achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while providing up to a 7x speedup over existing models. Beyond Hamiltonian prediction, the proposed sparsification techniques also hold significant potential for improving the efficiency and scalability of other SE(3) equivariant networks, further broadening their applicability and impact. Our code can be found at https://github.com/microsoft/SPHNet.
中摘: 哈密尔顿矩阵预测在计算化学中至关重要，它是确定分子广泛性质的基础。虽然SE(3)对称图神经网络在这一领域取得了显著的成功，但其高昂的计算成本——由高阶张量积（TP）操作驱动——限制了它们在具有大量基组的大分子系统中的可扩展性。为了解决这一挑战，我们引入了SPHNet，这是一种高效且可扩展的对称网络，它将自适应稀疏性融入到哈密尔顿预测中。SPHNet采用两种创新的稀疏门控来选择性地约束非关键相互作用组合，大幅减少了张量积计算量，同时保持了准确性。为了优化稀疏表示，我们开发了一种三阶段稀疏调度器，确保稳定收敛，并在高达70%的稀疏率下实现高性能。在QH9和PubchemQH数据集上的广泛评估表明，SPHNet在提供最高精度的同时，比现有模型快至7倍的速度。除了哈密尔顿预测之外，所提出的稀疏化技术还有望提高其他SE(3)对称网络的效率和可扩展性，进一步扩大它们的应用范围和影响。我们的代码可以在https://github.com/microsoft/SPHNet找到。

[No.107] UP-VLA: A Unified Understanding and Prediction Model for Embodied Agent
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: UP-VLA：统一的具身智能体理解与预测模型
类别: applications->robotics
作者: Jianke Zhang;Yanjiang Guo;Yucheng Hu;Xiaoyu Chen;Xiang Zhu;Jianyu Chen;
机构: Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45080
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18867
英摘: Recent advancements in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have leveraged pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to improve the generalization capabilities. VLMs, typically pre-trained on vision-language understanding tasks, provide rich semantic knowledge and reasoning abilities. However, prior research has shown that VLMs often focus on high-level semantic content and neglect low-level features, limiting their ability to capture detailed spatial information and understand physical dynamics. These aspects, which are crucial for embodied control tasks, remain underexplored in existing pre-training paradigms. In this paper, we investigate the training paradigm for VLAs, and introduce \textbf{UP-VLA}, a \textbf{U}nified VLA model training with both multi-modal \textbf{U}nderstanding and future \textbf{P}rediction objectives, enhancing both high-level semantic comprehension and low-level spatial understanding. Experimental results show that UP-VLA achieves a 33% improvement on the Calvin ABC-D benchmark compared to the previous state-of-the-art method. Additionally, UP-VLA demonstrates improved success rates in real-world manipulation tasks, particularly those requiring precise spatial information.
中摘: 近期在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型方面的进展利用了预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）来提高泛化能力。VLMs通常在视觉-语言理解任务上进行预训练，提供了丰富的语义知识和推理能力。然而，先前的研究表明，VLMs往往关注高层语义内容而忽视低级特征，限制了它们捕捉详细空间信息和理解物理动态的能力。这些方面对于体现控制任务至关重要，但在现有的预训练范式中仍然未被充分探索。在本文中，我们研究了VLA的训练范式，并引入了**UP-VLA**这一统一的VLA模型，该模型结合了多模态理解和未来预测目标，增强了高层语义理解和低级空间理解。实验结果显示，UP-VLA在Calvin ABC-D基准测试上的表现比之前的最佳方法提高了33%。此外，UP-VLA在现实世界的操作任务中表现出更高的成功率，特别是在需要精确空间信息的任务中。

[No.108] SPHINX: Structural Prediction using Hypergraph Inference Network
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: SPHINX：基于超图推理网络的结构预测
类别: deep learning->graph neural networks
作者: Iulia Duta;Pietro Lio;
机构: University of Cambridge;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43825
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03208
英摘: The importance of higher-order relations is widely recognized in a large number of real-world systems. However, annotating them is a tedious and sometimes impossible task. Consequently, current approaches for data modelling either ignore the higher-order interactions altogether or simplify them into pairwise connections. In order to facilitate higher-order processing, even when a hypergraph structure is not available, we introduce Structural Prediction using Hypergraph Inference Network (SPHINX), a model that learns to infer a latent hypergraph structure in an unsupervised way, solely from the final node-level signal. The model consists of a soft, differentiable clustering method used to sequentially predict, for each hyperedge, the probability distribution over the nodes and a sampling algorithm that converts them into an explicit hypergraph structure. We show that the recent advancement in $k$-subset sampling represents a suitable tool for producing discrete hypergraph structures, addressing some of the training instabilities exhibited by prior works. The resulting model can generate the higher-order structure necessary for any modern hypergraph neural network, facilitating the capture of higher-order interaction in domains where annotating them is difficult. Through extensive ablation studies and experiments conducted on two challenging datasets for trajectory prediction, we demonstrate that our model is capable of inferring suitable latent hypergraphs, that are interpretable and enhance the final performance.
中摘: 在许多实际系统中，高阶关系的重要性得到了广泛认可。然而，标注这些高阶关系是一项繁琐且有时不可能完成的任务。因此，当前的数据建模方法要么完全忽略高阶交互作用，要么将其简化为二元连接。为了促进即使在没有超图结构的情况下也能进行高阶处理，我们引入了超图推理网络（SPHINX）结构预测模型，该模型能够在未监督的情况下学习从最终节点级信号推断潜在的超图结构。该模型由一种软聚类方法组成，用于顺序预测每个超边的概率分布，并通过采样算法将这些概率分布转换为显式的超图结构。我们展示了最近在$k$子集采样方面的进展提供了一种合适的工具来生成离散的超图结构，解决了先前工作中的一些训练不稳定性问题。由此产生的模型能够生成任何现代超图神经网络所需的高阶结构，从而在难以标注高阶交互作用的领域中捕捉高阶交互作用。通过广泛的消融研究和在两个具有挑战性的轨迹预测数据集上的实验，我们证明我们的模型能够推断出合适的潜在超图结构，这些结构可解释性强，能提升最终性能。

[No.110] Volume Optimality in Conformal Prediction with Structured Prediction Sets
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于体积最优性的结构预测集配准预测Volume Optimality in Conformal Prediction with Structured Prediction Sets
类别: general machine learning
作者: Chao Gao;Liren Shan;Vaidehi Srinivas;Aravindan Vijayaraghavan;
机构: University of Chicago;Toyota Technological Institute at Chicago;Northwestern University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44066
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16658
英摘: Conformal Prediction is a widely studied technique to construct prediction sets of future observations. Most conformal prediction methods focus on achieving the necessary coverage guarantees, but do not provide formal guarantees on the size (volume) of the prediction sets. We first prove an impossibility of volume optimality where any distribution-free method can only find a trivial solution. We then introduce a new notion of volume optimality by restricting the prediction sets to belong to a set family (of finite VC-dimension), specifically a union of $k$-intervals. Our main contribution is an efficient distribution-free algorithm based on dynamic programming (DP) to find a union of $k$-intervals that is guaranteed for any distribution to have near-optimal volume among all unions of $k$-intervals satisfying the desired coverage property. By adopting the framework of distributional conformal prediction (Chernozhukov et al., 2021), the new DP based conformity score can also be applied to achieve approximate conditional coverage and conditional restricted volume optimality, as long as a reasonable estimator of the conditional CDF is available. While the theoretical results already establish volume-optimality guarantees, they are complemented by experiments that demonstrate that our method can significantly outperform existing methods in many settings.
中摘:  conformal预测是一种广泛研究的技术，用于构建未来观测值的预测集。大多数conformal预测方法专注于实现必要的覆盖保证，但并不提供关于预测集大小（体积）的正式保证。我们首先证明了体积优化的不可能性，即任何无分布的方法只能找到一个平凡的解决方案。然后，我们引入了一个新的体积优化概念，通过将预测集限制在有限VC维数的集合族内，特别是$k$-区间并集。我们的主要贡献是一个基于动态规划(DP)的有效无分布算法，该算法可以确保对于任何分布，所找到的$k$-区间并集在满足所需覆盖性质的所有$k$-区间并集中具有接近最优的体积。通过采用分布一致conformal预测的框架（Chernozhukov等人，2021），新的基于DP的conformity评分也可以应用于实现近似的条件覆盖和条件受限体积优化，只要有条件CDF的合理估计器可用。尽管理论结果已经建立了体积优化的保证，但实验结果进一步表明，在许多情况下，我们的方法可以显著优于现有方法。

[No.111] Provable Length Generalization in Sequence Prediction via Spectral Filtering
来源: ICML 2025

作者: Annie Marsden;Evan Dogariu;Naman Agarwal;Xinyi Chen;Daniel Suo;Elad Hazan;

英摘: We consider the problem of length generalization in sequence prediction. We continue by studying this concept through the lens of the spectral filtering algorithm. We present a gradient-based learning algorithm that provably achieves length generalization for linear dynamical systems. We conclude with proof-of-concept experiments which are consistent with our theory.
中摘: 我们考虑在序列预测中的长度泛化问题。在这个背景下，我们定义了一个新的性能度量标准——非对称后悔度（Asymmetric-Regret），它衡量的是模型相对于基准预测器的后悔度，而该基准预测器具有比学习者可用的上下文长度更长的上下文长度。随后，我们通过光谱滤波算法这一视角来研究这一概念。我们提出了一种基于梯度的学习算法，可以证明该算法能够实现长度泛化，适用于线性动力系统。最后，我们通过概念验证实验来支持我们的理论。

[No.112] Kandinsky Conformal Prediction: Beyond Class- and Covariate-Conditional Coverage
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 康定斯基自适应预测：超越类条件和协变量条件覆盖
类别: social aspects->fairness
作者: Konstantina Bairaktari;Jiayun Wu;Steven Wu;
机构: ;Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45742
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17264
英摘: Conformal prediction is a powerful distribution-free framework for constructing prediction sets with coverage guarantees. Classical methods, such as split conformal prediction, provide marginal coverage, ensuring that the prediction set contains the label of a random test point with a target probability. However, these guarantees may not hold uniformly across different subpopulations, leading to disparities in coverage. Prior work has explored coverage guarantees conditioned on events related to the covariates and label of the test point. We present Kandinsky conformal prediction, a framework that significantly expands the scope of conditional coverage guarantees. In contrast to Mondrian conformal prediction, which restricts its coverage guarantees to disjoint groups -- reminiscent of the rigid, structured grids of Piet Mondrian's art -- our framework flexibly handles overlapping and fractional group memberships defined jointly on covariates and labels, reflecting the layered, intersecting forms in Wassily Kandinsky's compositions. Our algorithm unifies and extends existing methods, encompassing covariate-based group conditional, class conditional, and Mondrian conformal prediction as special cases, while achieving a minimax-optimal high-probability conditional coverage bound. Finally, we demonstrate the practicality of our approach through empirical evaluation on real-world datasets.
中摘: 自适应预测是一种强大的无分布框架，用于构建具有覆盖保证的预测集。经典的自适应预测方法，如分割自适应预测，提供边际覆盖保证，确保预测集包含随机测试点的标签的概率达到目标值。然而，这些保证可能不适用于不同的子人群，导致覆盖差异。先前的工作探索了基于测试点协变量和标签相关事件的覆盖保证。我们提出了Kandinsky自适应预测框架，该框架显著扩展了条件覆盖保证的范围。与Mondrian自适应预测不同，后者将其覆盖保证限制在不相交的组别上——类似于皮特·蒙德里安艺术中的刚性、结构化网格——我们的框架灵活地处理协变量和标签上定义的重叠和分数组别成员身份，反映了瓦西里·康定斯基作品中的分层、交叠形式。我们的算法统一并扩展了现有方法，包括基于协变量的组条件、类别条件以及Mondrian自适应预测作为特殊情况，同时实现了最小最大最优的高概率条件覆盖界。最后，我们通过在实际数据集上的实证评估展示了我们方法的实用性。

[No.114] Distributed Conformal Prediction via Message Passing
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 分布式一致性预测通过消息传递
类别: theory->probabilistic methods
作者: Haifeng Wen;Hong Xing;Osvaldo Simeone;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;King's College London;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45295
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.14544
英摘: Post-hoc calibration of pre-trained models is critical for ensuring reliable inference, especially in safety-critical domains such as healthcare. Conformal Prediction (CP) offers a robust post-hoc calibration framework, providing distribution-free statistical coverage guarantees for prediction sets by leveraging held-out datasets. In this work, we address a decentralized setting where each device has limited calibration data and can communicate only with its neighbors over an arbitrary graph topology. We propose two message-passing-based approaches for achieving reliable inference via CP: quantile-based distributed conformal prediction (Q-DCP) and histogram-based distributed conformal prediction (H-DCP). Q-DCP employs distributed quantile regression enhanced with tailored smoothing and regularization terms to accelerate convergence, while H-DCP uses a consensus-based histogram estimation approach. Through extensive experiments, we investigate the trade-offs between hyperparameter tuning requirements, communication overhead, coverage guarantees, and prediction set sizes across different network topologies. The code of our work is released on: https://github.com/HaifengWen/Distributed-Conformal-Prediction.
中摘: 后验校准预训练模型对于确保可靠的推理至关重要，尤其是在如医疗保健这样的安全关键领域。条件一致性预测（Conformal Prediction, CP）提供了一种稳健的后验校准框架，通过利用保留的数据集来为预测集提供无分布统计覆盖保证。在本研究中，我们探讨了一个去中心化的设置，在这个设置中，每个设备拥有限的校准数据，并且只能与其邻居通过任意图拓扑进行通信。我们提出了两种基于消息传递的方法，以通过CP实现可靠的推理：基于分位数的分布式条件一致性预测（Q-DCP）和基于直方图的分布式条件一致性预测（H-DCP）。Q-DCP采用增强有定制平滑和正则化项的分布式分位数回归，以加速收敛；而H-DCP使用基于共识的直方图估计方法。通过广泛的实验，我们在不同的网络拓扑结构下调查了超参数调整需求、通信开销、覆盖保证以及预测集大小之间的权衡。我们的工作代码已发布在：https://github.com/HaifengWen/Distributed-Conformal-Prediction。

[No.115] Prediction-Powered E-Values
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 预测驱动的E值
类别: probabilistic methods->everything else
作者: Daniel Csillag;Claudio Jose Struchiner;Guilherme Tegoni Goedert;
机构: Fundação Getúlio Vargas;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43872
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04294
英摘: Quality statistical inference requires a sufficient amount of data, which can be missing or hard to obtain. To this end, prediction-powered inference has risen as a promising methodology, but existing approaches are largely limited to Z-estimation problems such as inference of means and quantiles. In this paper, we apply ideas of prediction-powered inference to e-values. By doing so, we inherit all the usual benefits of e-values -- such as anytime-validity, post-hoc validity and versatile sequential inference -- as well as greatly expand the set of inferences achievable in a prediction-powered manner. In particular, we show that every inference procedure that can be framed in terms of e-values has a prediction-powered counterpart, given by our method. We showcase the effectiveness of our framework across a wide range of inference tasks, from simple hypothesis testing and confidence intervals to more involved procedures for change-point detection and causal discovery, which were out of reach of previous techniques. Our approach is modular and easily integrable into existing algorithms, making it a compelling choice for practical applications.
中摘: 高质量的统计推断需要足够的数据，而这些数据可能缺失或难以获取。为此，预测驱动的推断已经作为一种有前景的方法出现，但现有的方法主要局限于Z-估计问题，例如均值和分位数的推断。在本文中，我们将预测驱动的推断思想应用于e值。通过这种方式，我们继承了e值的所有常规优势——如任意时有效性、事后有效性以及多功能的序贯推断——同时极大地扩展了可以通过预测驱动方式实现的推断范围。特别是，我们证明了每一个可以用e值表述的推断过程都有一个由我们的方法提供的预测驱动的对应过程。我们展示了我们的框架在广泛的各种推断任务中的有效性，从简单的假设检验和置信区间到更复杂的变点检测和因果发现程序，这些都是之前的技术无法触及的。我们的方法是模块化的，并且易于集成到现有算法中，使其成为实际应用中的一个极具吸引力的选择。

[No.116] Latent Imputation before Prediction: A New Computational Paradigm for De Novo Peptide Sequencing
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 潜在于预测之前的插补：一种新的计算范式用于从头肽序列分析
类别: applications->chemistry physics and earth sciences
作者: Ye Du;Chen Yang;Nanxi Yu;Wanyu Lin;Qian Zhao;Shujun Wang;
机构: Hong Kong Polytechnic University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43921
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17524
英摘: De novo peptide sequencing is a fundamental computational technique for ascertaining amino acid sequences of peptides directly from tandem mass spectrometry data, eliminating the need for reference databases. Cutting-edge models usually encode the observed mass spectra into latent representations from which peptides are predicted autoregressively. However, the issue of missing fragmentation, attributable to factors such as suboptimal fragmentation efficiency and instrumental constraints, presents a formidable challenge in practical applications. To tackle this obstacle, we propose a novel computational paradigm called \underline{\textbf{L}}atent \underline{\textbf{I}}mputation before \underline{\textbf{P}}rediction (LIPNovo). LIPNovo is devised to compensate for missing fragmentation information within observed spectra before executing the final peptide prediction. Rather than generating raw missing data, LIPNovo performs imputation in the latent space, guided by the theoretical peak profile of the target peptide sequence. The imputation process is conceptualized as a set-prediction problem, utilizing a set of learnable peak queries to reason about the relationships among observed peaks and directly generate the latent representations of theoretical peaks through optimal bipartite matching. In this way, LIPNovo manages to supplement missing information during inference and thus boosts performance. Despite its simplicity, experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that LIPNovo outperforms state-of-the-art methods by large margins. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/usr922/LIPNovo}{https://github.com/usr922/LIPNovo}.
中摘: 从头肽测序是一种基本的计算技术，可以直接从串联质谱数据中确定肽的氨基酸序列，无需参考数据库。最先进的模型通常将观测到的质谱编码为潜在表示，从中预测肽。然而，由于诸如亚优化裂解效率和仪器限制等因素导致的缺失裂解问题，在实际应用中构成了一个严峻挑战。为了解决这一障碍，我们提出了一种新的计算范式，称为潜在插补预测（LIPNovo）。LIPNovo旨在在执行最终肽预测之前，在观测到的光谱中补偿缺失的裂解信息。与生成原始缺失数据不同，LIPNovo在潜在空间中进行插补，并由目标肽序列的理论峰型指导。插补过程被构想为一个集合预测问题，利用一组可学习的峰查询来推理观测到的峰之间的关系，并通过最优二分匹配直接生成理论峰的潜在表示。这样，LIPNovo能够在推断过程中补充缺失的信息，从而提升性能。尽管其结构相对简单，但在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，LIPNovo在很大程度上优于现有方法。代码可在[https://github.com/usr922/LIPNovo](https://github.com/usr922/LIPNovo) 获取。

[No.118] NTPP: Generative Speech Language Modeling for Dual-Channel Spoken Dialogue via Next-Token-Pair Prediction
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: NTPP：通过下一个词对预测实现的双通道口语对话生成语言建模
类别: applications->language speech and dialog
作者: Qichao Wang;Ziqiao Meng;Wenqian Cui;Yifei Zhang;Pengcheng Wu;Bingzhe Wu;Irwin King;Liang Chen;Peilin Zhao;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;National University of Singapore;Chinese University of Hong Kong;Nanyang Technological University;Shenzhen University;Tencent;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46439
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00975
英摘: Inspired by the impressive capabilities of GPT-4o, there is growing interest in enabling speech language models (SLMs) to engage in natural, fluid spoken interactions with humans. Recent advancements have led to the development of several SLMs that demonstrate promising results in this area. However, current approaches have yet to fully exploit dual-channel speech data, which inherently captures the structure and dynamics of human conversation. In this work, we systematically explore the use of dual-channel speech data in the context of modern large language models, and introduce a novel generative modeling paradigm, Next-Token-Pair Prediction (NTPP), to enable speaker-independent dual-channel spoken dialogue learning using decoder-only architectures for the first time. We evaluate our approach on standard benchmarks, and empirical results show that our proposed method, NTPP, significantly improves the conversational abilities of SLMs in terms of turn-taking prediction, response coherence, and naturalness. Moreover, compared to existing methods, NTPP achieves substantially lower inference latency, highlighting its practical efficiency for real-time applications.
中摘: 受GPT-4o卓越能力的启发，人们越来越关注如何使语音语言模型（SLMs）能够与人类进行自然流畅的口语互动。近期的进展催生了多种在这一领域表现出色的SLMs。然而，当前的方法尚未充分利用双通道语音数据，这种数据本质上可以捕捉人类对话的结构和动态。在这项工作中，我们系统地探讨了在现代大型语言模型背景下使用双通道语音数据的可能性，并首次引入了一种新颖的生成建模范式——Next-Token-Pair Prediction (NTPP)，以实现基于解码器架构的独立说话人双通道口语对话学习。我们在标准基准上评估了我们的方法，实验结果表明，我们提出的方法NTPP在换言互动态预测、响应连贯性和自然度方面显著提升了SLMs的对话能力。此外，与现有方法相比，NTPP实现了显著更低的推理延迟，突显了其在实时应用中的实际效率。

[No.119] Time-Aware World Model for Adaptive Prediction and Control
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 时aware世界模型用于自适应预测与控制
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Anh N Nhu;Sanghyun Son;Ming Lin;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08441
英摘: In this work, we introduce the Time-Aware World Model (TAWM), a model-based approach that explicitly incorporates temporal dynamics. By conditioning on the time-step size, Δt, and training over a diverse range of Δt values -- rather than sampling at a fixed time-step -- TAWM learns both high- and low-frequency task dynamics across diverse control problems. Grounded in the information-theoretic insight that the optimal sampling rate depends on a system's underlying dynamics, this time-aware formulation improves both performance and data efficiency. Empirical evaluations show that TAWM consistently outperforms conventional models across varying observation rates in a variety of control tasks, using the same number of training samples and iterations. Our code can be found online at: github.com/anh-nn01/Time-Aware-World-Model.
中摘: 在本工作中，我们引入了时间意识世界模型（TAWM），这是一种基于模型的方法，明确地融入了时间动态。通过条件设置时间步长Δt，并在一系列不同的Δt值上进行训练——而不是固定时间步长采样——TAWM能够学习各种控制问题中的高低频任务动态。基于信息论的见解，即最优采样率取决于系统的基本动态，这种时间意识的表述形式在提高性能和数据效率方面表现出色。实证评估表明，在多种控制任务中，使用相同的训练样本数量和迭代次数时，TAWM在不同观测率下的表现始终优于传统模型。我们的代码可以在以下网址找到：github.com/anh-nn01/Time-Aware-World-Model。

[No.120] Representations Shape Weak-to-Strong Generalization: Theoretical Insights and Empirical Predictions
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 表征塑造从弱到强的泛化能力：理论洞察与实证预测
类别: theory->deep learning
作者: Yihao Xue;Jiping Li;Baharan Mirzasoleiman;
机构: University of California, Los Angeles;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43511
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00620
英摘: Weak-to-Strong Generalization (W2SG), where a weak model supervises a stronger one, serves as an important analogy for understanding how humans might guide superhuman intelligence in the future. Promising empirical results revealed that a strong model can surpass its weak supervisor. While recent work has offered theoretical insights into this phenomenon, a clear understanding of the interactions between weak and strong models that drive W2SG remains elusive. We investigate W2SG through a theoretical lens and show that it can be characterized using kernels derived from the principal components of weak and strong models' internal representations. These kernels can be used to define a space that, at a high level, captures what the weak model is unable to learn but is learnable by the strong model. The projection of labels onto this space quantifies how much the strong model falls short of its full potential due to weak supervision. This characterization also provides insights into how certain errors in weak supervision can be corrected by the strong model, regardless of overfitting. Our theory has significant practical implications, providing a representation-based metric that predicts W2SG performance trends without requiring labels, as shown in experiments on molecular predictions with transformers and 5 NLP tasks involving 52 LLMs.
中摘: 弱到强泛化（Weak-to-Strong Generalization, W2SG），即一个较弱模型监督一个更强的模型，为理解人类在未来如何引导超人类智能提供了重要的类比。近期的一些实证研究表明，更强的模型可以超越其较弱的监督者。尽管最近的工作已经提供了关于这一现象的理论见解，但弱模型和强模型之间交互作用的具体机制仍不清楚。我们从理论角度研究W2SG，并表明它可以使用源自弱模型和强模型内部表示的主要成分的核来加以表征。这些核可用于定义一个空间，在高层次上捕捉弱模型无法学习而强模型可以学习的内容。将标签投影到该空间可以量化由于弱监督而导致强模型未能充分发挥其潜力的程度。这种表征还揭示了即使在过拟合的情况下，某些弱监督中的错误也可以被强模型纠正。我们的理论具有重要的实际意义，提供了一种基于表示的度量标准，能够在不需要标签的情况下预测W2SG性能趋势，如在分子预测任务中使用变换器以及涉及52个大型语言模型的5项NLP任务所证明的那样。

[No.121] TraceGrad: a Framework Learning Expressive SO(3)-equivariant Non-linear Representations for Electronic-Structure Hamiltonian Prediction
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: TraceGrad：一个学习表达性SO(3)齐变非线性表示的框架，用于电子结构哈密顿量预测
类别: applications->chemistry physics and earth sciences
作者: Shi Yin;Xinyang Pan;Fengyan Wang;Lixin He;
机构: Hefei Comprehensive National Science Center;University of Science and Technology of China;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05722
英摘: We propose a framework to combine strong non-linear expressiveness with strict SO(3)-equivariance in prediction of the electronic-structure Hamiltonian, by exploring the mathematical relationships between SO(3)-invariant and SO(3)-equivariant quantities and their representations. The proposed framework, called TraceGrad, first constructs theoretical SO(3)-invariant trace quantities derived from the Hamiltonian targets, and use these invariant quantities as supervisory labels to guide the learning of high-quality SO(3)-invariant features. Given that SO(3)-invariance is preserved under non-linear operations, the learning of invariant features can extensively utilize non-linear mappings, thereby fully capturing the non-linear patterns inherent in physical systems. Building on this, we propose a gradient-based mechanism to induce SO(3)-equivariant encodings of various degrees from the learned SO(3)-invariant features. This mechanism can incorporate powerful non-linear expressive capabilities into SO(3)-equivariant features with consistency of physical dimensions to the regression targets, while theoretically preserving equivariant properties, establishing a strong foundation for predicting Hamiltonian. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in prediction accuracy across eight challenging benchmark databases on Hamiltonian prediction. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach not only improves the accuracy of Hamiltonian prediction but also significantly enhances the prediction for downstream physical quantities, and also markedly improves the acceleration performance for the traditional Density Functional Theory algorithms.
中摘: 我们提出了一种框架，旨在结合强非线性表达能力和严格的SO(3)-等变性，在电子结构哈密顿量预测中进行融合。通过探索SO(3)-不变量和SO(3)-等变量之间的数学关系及其表示，我们研究了这些关系在预测中的应用。所提出的框架称为TraceGrad，首先从哈密顿量目标构建理论上的SO(3)-不变量迹量，并使用这些不变量作为监督标签来引导高质量SO(3)-不变特征的学习。由于非线性操作可以保持SO(3)-不变性，因此不变特征的学习能够广泛利用非线性映射，从而全面捕捉物理系统中存在的非线性模式。在此基础上，我们提出了一种基于梯度的机制，用于从学习到的SO(3)-不变特征中诱导出各种程度的SO(3)-等变编码。该机制能够在保持物理维度一致性的同时，将强大的非线性表达能力整合到SO(3)-等变特征中，从而为预测哈密顿量奠定坚实的基础。我们的方法在八个具有挑战性的基准数据库上实现了哈密顿量预测的最新准确率。实验结果表明，这种方法不仅提高了哈密顿量预测的准确性，还显著增强了对下游物理量的预测能力，并且明显改善了传统密度泛函理论算法的加速性能。

[No.124] CMoS: Rethinking Time Series Prediction Through the Lens of Chunk-wise Spatial Correlations

英摘: Recent advances in lightweight time series forecasting models suggest the inherent simplicity of time series forecasting tasks. [No.125] Implicit degree bias in the link prediction task
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 隐含层级偏见在链接预测任务中的影响
类别: deep learning->graph neural networks
作者: Rachith Aiyappa;Xin Wang;Munjung Kim;Ozgur Can Seckin;Yong-Yeol Ahn;Sadamori Kojaku;
机构: Indiana University;;University of Virginia;State University of New York at Binghamton;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44481
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14985
英摘: Link prediction -- a task of distinguishing actual hidden edges from random unconnected node pairs -- is one of the quintessential tasks in graph machine learning. Despite being widely accepted as a universal benchmark and a downstream task for representation learning, the validity of the link prediction benchmark itself has been rarely questioned. Here, we show that the common edge sampling procedure in the link prediction task has an implicit bias toward high-degree nodes and produces a highly skewed evaluation that favors methods overly dependent on node degree, to the extent that a ``null'' link prediction method based solely on node degree can yield nearly optimal performance. We propose a degree-corrected link prediction task that offers a more reasonable assessment that aligns better with the performance in the recommendation task. Finally, we demonstrate that the degree-corrected benchmark can more effectively train graph machine-learning models by reducing overfitting to node degrees and facilitating the learning of relevant structures in graphs.
中摘: 链接预测——一项区分实际隐藏边与随机未连接节点对的任务——是图机器学习中的核心任务之一。尽管它被广泛接受为表示学习的通用基准和下游任务，但链接预测基准的有效性本身很少受到质疑。在这里，我们展示了链接预测任务中的常见边采样过程隐含地偏向高度节点，并产生了一种高度偏斜的评估，这种评估过于倾向于依赖节点度的方法，以至于一个仅基于节点度的“空”链接预测方法可以几乎达到最优性能。我们提出了一种修正后的链接预测任务，它能提供一个更为合理的评估，更符合推荐任务中的性能表现。最后，我们证明了修正后的基准可以通过减少对节点度的过拟合以及促进图中相关结构的学习，更有效地训练图机器学习模型。

[No.126] Measuring In-Context Computation Complexity via Hidden State Prediction
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 通过隐藏状态预测衡量上下文相关计算复杂度
类别: deep learning->sequential models time series
作者: Vincent Herrmann;Róbert Csordás;Jürgen Schmidhuber;
机构: IDSIA;Stanford University;King Abdullah University of Science and Technology;Universita della Svizzera Italiana;NNAISENSE;Institute of Digital Technologies;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44985
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13431
英摘: Detecting when a neural sequence model does "interesting" computation is an open problem. The next token prediction loss is a poor indicator: Low loss can stem from trivially predictable sequences that are uninteresting, while high loss may reflect unpredictable but also irrelevant information that can be ignored by the model. We propose a better metric: measuring the model's ability to predict its own future hidden states. We show empirically that this metric -- in contrast to the next token prediction loss -- correlates with the intuitive interestingness of the task. To measure predictability, we introduce the architecture-agnostic "prediction of hidden states" (PHi) layer that serves as an information bottleneck on the main pathway of the network (e.g., the residual stream in Transformers). We propose a novel learned predictive prior that enables us to measure the novel information gained in each computation step, which serves as our metric. We show empirically that our metric predicts the description length of formal languages learned in-context, the complexity of mathematical reasoning problems, and the correctness of self-generated reasoning chains.
中摘: 检测神经序列模型进行“有趣”计算的时机是一个开放性问题。下一个标记预测损失并不是一个好的指标：低损失可能来自容易预测但无趣的序列，而高损失则可能反映不可预测但同样无关紧要的信息，这些信息可以被模型忽略。我们提出一个更好的度量标准：衡量模型预测其未来隐藏状态的能力。实验结果显示，这一度量标准与任务的直观有趣性相关，而不同于下一个标记预测损失。为了测量可预测性，我们引入了一个架构无关的“隐藏状态预测”（PHi）层，作为网络主要路径上的信息瓶颈（例如，在Transformer中的残差流）。我们提出了一个新的学习预测先验，使我们能够测量每一步计算中获得的新信息，这作为我们的度量标准。实验结果表明，我们的度量标准可以预测上下文中学到的形式语言的描述长度、数学推理问题的复杂性以及自动生成的推理链的正确性。

[No.127] Conformal Prediction with Cellwise Outliers: A Detect-then-Impute Approach
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于单元离群值的 conformal 预测：一种先检测后填充的方法
类别: general machine learning
作者: Qian Peng;Yajie Bao;Haojie Ren;Zhaojun Wang;Changliang Zou;
机构: Nankai University;Shanghai Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43829
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04986
英摘: Conformal prediction is a powerful tool for constructing prediction intervals for black-box models, providing a finite sample coverage guarantee for exchangeable data. However, this exchangeability is compromised when some entries of the test feature are contaminated, such as in the case of cellwise outliers. To address this issue, this paper introduces a novel framework called detect-then-impute conformal prediction. This framework first employs an outlier detection procedure on the test feature and then utilizes an imputation method to fill in those cells identified as outliers. To quantify the uncertainty in the processed test feature, we adaptively apply the detection and imputation procedures to the calibration set, thereby constructing exchangeable features for the conformal prediction interval of the test label. We develop two practical algorithms, PDI-CP and JDI-CP, and provide a distribution-free coverage analysis under some commonly used detection and imputation procedures. Notably, JDI-CP achieves a finite sample $1-2α$ coverage guarantee. Numerical experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that our proposed algorithms exhibit robust coverage properties and comparable efficiency to the oracle baseline.
中摘: 自适应预测区间是一种强大的工具，用于构建黑盒模型的预测区间，能够为可交换数据提供有限样本覆盖保证。然而，当测试特征的一些条目受到污染，例如单元异常值的情况下，这种可交换性会受到影响。为了应对这一问题，本文引入了一种名为“检测-然后-填补”的新颖框架。该框架首先在测试特征上应用异常检测程序，然后使用填补方法来替换那些被识别为异常值的单元格。为了量化处理后的测试特征中的不确定性，我们通过自适应地将检测和填补程序应用于校准集，从而构建出可交换的特征，用于构造测试标签的预测区间。我们开发了两个实用算法：PDI-CP 和 JDI-CP，并在一些常用检测和填补程序下提供了无分布假设的覆盖分析。值得注意的是，JDI-CP 能够实现有限样本下的 $1-2\alpha$ 覆盖保证。我们在合成数据集和真实数据集上的数值实验表明，我们提出的算法具有稳健的覆盖性能，并且与最优基准相当的效率。

[No.128] Direct Prediction Set Minimization via Bilevel Conformal Classifier Training
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 直接预测集最小化通过双层一致分类器训练
类别: general machine learning
作者: Yuanjie Shi;Hooman Shahrokhi;Xuesong Jia;Xiongzhi Chen;Jana Doppa;Yan Yan;
机构: Washington State University;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45700
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06599
英摘: Conformal prediction (CP) is a promising uncertainty quantification framework which works as a wrapper around a black-box classifier to construct prediction sets (i.e., subset of candidate classes) with provable guarantees. However, standard calibration methods for CP tend to produce large prediction sets which makes them less useful in practice. This paper considers the problem of integrating conformal principles into the training process of deep classifiers to directly minimize the size of prediction sets. We formulate conformal training as a bilevel optimization problem and propose the {\em Direct Prediction Set Minimization (DPSM)} algorithm to solve it. The key insight behind DPSM is to minimize a measure of the prediction set size (upper level) that is conditioned on the learned quantile of conformity scores (lower level). We analyze that DPSM has a learning bound of $O(1/\sqrt{n})$ (with $n$ training samples), while prior conformal training methods based on stochastic approximation for the quantile has a bound of $Ω(1/s)$ (with batch size $s$ and typically $s \ll \sqrt{n}$). Experiments on various benchmark datasets and deep models show that DPSM significantly outperforms the best prior conformal training baseline with $20.46\%\downarrow$ in the prediction set size and validates our theory.
中摘: 自适应预测集最小化（DPSM）算法是一种有前途的不确定性量化框架，它作为黑盒分类器的包装器，构建具有可证明保证的预测集（即候选类别的子集）。然而，标准的自适应预测集校准方法往往会生成较大的预测集，这使得它们在实际应用中不够有用。本文考虑了将自适应原则整合到深度分类器的训练过程中的问题，以直接最小化预测集的大小。我们将自适应训练形式化为一个双层优化问题，并提出了DPSM算法来解决这个问题。DPSM的核心洞察是通过最小化预测集大小的度量（上层），该度量依赖于学习到的自适应分数一致性得分的分位数（下层），从而最小化预测集的大小。我们分析表明，DPSM的学习界为$O(1/\sqrt{n})$（其中$n$为训练样本数量），而基于随机近似计算分位数的先前自适应训练方法的学习界为$Ω(1/s)$（其中$s$为批次大小，通常$s \ll \sqrt{n}$）。在各种基准数据集和深度模型上的实验显示，DPSM显著优于之前的最佳自适应预测集训练基线，预测集大小减少了$20.46\%$，并验证了我们的理论。

链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44237
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05676
英摘: Ensuring model calibration is critical for reliable prediction, yet popular distribution-free methods such as histogram binning and isotonic regression offer only asymptotic guarantees. We introduce a unified framework for Venn and Venn-Abers calibration that extends Vovk's approach beyond binary classification to a broad class of prediction problems defined by generic loss functions. Our method transforms any perfectly in-sample calibrated predictor into a set-valued predictor that, in finite samples, outputs at least one marginally calibrated point prediction. These set predictions shrink asymptotically and converge to a single conditionally calibrated prediction, capturing epistemic uncertainty. We further propose Venn multicalibration, a new approach for achieving finite-sample calibration across subpopulations. For quantile loss, our framework recovers group-conditional and multicalibrated conformal prediction as special cases and yields novel prediction intervals with quantile-conditional coverage.
中摘: 确保模型校准对于可靠预测至关重要，然而诸如直方图分箱和等温回归等流行的非参数方法只能提供渐进保证。我们提出了一种统一框架，用于Venn校准和Venn-Abers校准，将Vovk的方法从二元分类扩展到由通用损失函数定义的一系列广泛的预测问题。我们的方法将任何完美内样本校准的预测器转换为集合值预测器，在有限样本中，它会输出至少一个边缘校准的预测值。这些集合预测随着样本量的增加而收缩，并最终收敛到单个条件校准的预测值，从而捕捉到认知不确定性。此外，我们还提出了Venn多校准这一新方法，以实现子人群中的有限样本校准。对于分位数损失，我们的框架可以恢复群体条件性和多校准的符合性预测，并产生具有分位数条件覆盖的新预测区间。

[No.132] Lightweight Dataset Pruning without Full Training via Example Difficulty and Prediction Uncertainty
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 轻量级数据集剪枝无需完整训练通过示例难度和预测不确定性
类别: general machine learning->supervised learning
作者: Yeseul Cho;Baekrok Shin;Changmin Kang;Chulhee Yun;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46202
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06905
英摘: Recent advances in deep learning rely heavily on massive datasets, leading to substantial storage and training costs. Dataset pruning aims to alleviate this demand by discarding redundant examples. However, many existing methods require training a model with a full dataset over a large number of epochs before being able to prune the dataset, which ironically makes the pruning process more expensive than just training the model on the entire dataset. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a Difficulty and Uncertainty-Aware Lightweight (DUAL) score, which aims to identify important samples from the early training stage by considering both example difficulty and prediction uncertainty. To address a catastrophic accuracy drop at an extreme pruning, we further propose a ratio-adaptive sampling using Beta distribution. Experiments on various datasets and learning scenarios such as image classification with label noise and image corruption, and model architecture generalization demonstrate the superiority of our method over previous state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches. Specifically, on ImageNet-1k, our method reduces the time cost for pruning to 66% compared to previous methods while achieving a SOTA, specifically 60% test accuracy at a 90% pruning ratio. On CIFAR datasets, the time cost is reduced to just 15% while maintaining SOTA performance.
中摘: 近年来，深度学习的进步很大程度上依赖于庞大的数据集，这导致了存储和训练成本的显著增加。数据集修剪旨在通过丢弃冗余样本来缓解这一需求。然而，许多现有方法在能够修剪数据集之前需要使用完整数据集进行大量epoch的训练，这反而使得修剪过程的成本高于直接在完整数据集上训练模型的成本。为克服这一局限性，我们引入了一种“难易度和不确定性感知轻量级”（DUAL）得分，该得分旨在通过同时考虑示例难度和预测不确定性，在早期训练阶段识别出重要的样本。为了应对极端修剪可能导致的灾难性准确率下降，我们进一步提出了一种基于Beta分布的比率自适应采样方法。实验表明，我们的方法在各种数据集和学习场景（如带有标签噪声和图像腐蚀的图像分类，以及模型架构泛化）中优于先前的最佳方法。具体而言，与以前的方法相比，我们在ImageNet-1k上将修剪时间成本降低了66%，同时实现了最佳测试准确率60%，修剪比率为90%。在CIFAR数据集上，我们的时间成本降低了仅为15%，同时保持了最佳性能。

[No.134] From Pixels to Perception: Interpretable Predictions via Instance-wise Grouped Feature Selection
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 从像素到感知：基于实例分组特征选择的可解释预测

英摘: Understanding the decision-making process of machine learning models provides valuable insights into the task, the data, and the reasons behind a model's failures. [No.135] Disparate Conditional Prediction in Multiclass Classifiers
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 多类分类器中的异质条件预测
类别: social aspects->fairness
作者: Sivan Sabato;Eran Treister;Elad Yom-Tov;
机构: McMaster University;Ben-Gurion University of the Negev;Bar-Ilan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45683
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2206.03234
英摘: We propose methods for auditing multiclass classifiers for fairness under multiclass equalized odds,by estimating the deviation from equalized odds when the classifier is not completely fair. We generalize to multiclass classifiers the measure of Disparate Conditional Prediction (DCP), originally suggested by Sabato & Yom-Tov (2020) for binary classifiers. DCP is defined as the fraction of the population for which the classifier predicts with conditional prediction probabilities that differ from the closest common baseline. We provide new local-optimization methods for estimating the multiclass DCPunder two different regimes,one in which the conditional confusion matrices for each protected sub-population are known, and one in which these cannot be estimated, for instance, because the classifier is inaccessible or because good-quality individual-level data is not available. These methods can be used to detect classifiers that likely treat a significant fraction of the population unfairly. Experiments demonstrate the accuracy of the methods. Code is provided at https://github.com/sivansabato/DCPmulticlass.
中摘: 我们提出了一种在多类别分类器公平性审计方面的方法，通过估计当分类器不完全公平时与平等机会偏差之间的差异。我们将不平等条件预测（DCP）这一度量推广到了多类别分类器，最初由Sabato & Yom-Tov（2020）为二分类器建议。DCP定义为那些分类器预测条件概率与其最接近的通用基线概率不同的人群比例。我们提供了两种新的局部优化方法来估计多类别DCP，在两种不同的情况下分别适用：一种情况是每个受保护子人群的条件混淆矩阵已知；另一种情况是这些条件混淆矩阵无法被估计，例如因为分类器不可访问或者高质量的个体级数据不可用。这些方法可以用来检测可能不公平对待了显著部分人群的分类器。实验表明了这些方法的准确性。代码可以在https://github.com/sivansabato/DCPmulticlass获取。

[No.136] Prune 'n Predict: Optimizing LLM Decision-making with Conformal Prediction
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 剪枝与预测：利用一致预测优化大语言模型决策making
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Harit Vishwakarma;Alan Mishler;Thomas Cook;Niccolo Dalmasso;Natraj Raman;Sumitra Ganesh;
机构: University of Wisconsin;JPMorgan Chase & Co.;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46415
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00555
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) are empowering decision-making in several applications, including tool or API usage and answering multiple-choice questions (MCQs). However, incorrect outputs pose significant risks in high-stakes domains like healthcare and finance. To quantify LLM uncertainty and thereby mitigate these risks, recent works employ conformal prediction (CP), a model- and distribution-agnostic framework that uses LLM outputs to generate a \emph{prediction set} containing the true answer with high probability. Leveraging CP, we propose \emph{conformal revision of questions} (CROQ), which revises the question by narrowing down the available choices to those in the prediction set and asking the LLM the revised question. We expect LLMs to be more accurate on revised questions with fewer choices. Furthermore, we expect CROQ to be effective when the prediction sets from CP are small. Commonly used logit scores often lead to large sets, diminishing CROQ's effectiveness. To overcome this, we propose CP-OPT, an optimization framework to learn scores that minimize set sizes while maintaining coverage. Our extensive experiments on MMLU, ToolAlpaca, and TruthfulQA datasets with multiple LLMs show that CROQ improves accuracy over the standard inference, with more pronounced gains when paired with CP-OPT.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）正在为工具或API使用以及回答多项选择题（MCQs）等多种应用赋能决策。然而，在如医疗和金融等高风险领域，错误输出会带来重大风险。为了量化LLM的不确定性并从而减轻这些风险，近期的研究采用了容错预测（CP），这是一种不依赖于特定模型和分布的框架，通过利用LLM的输出生成包含正确答案的概率较高的“预测集”。借助CP，我们提出了一种称为“容错问题修订”（CROQ）的方法，该方法通过缩小可用选项范围至预测集中的一部分，并让LLM回答修订后的问题。我们预期，在较少的选择项下，LLM能够更准确地回答问题。此外，当CP生成的预测集较小时，我们预计CROQ将更为有效。常用的logit分数往往会导致较大的集合，从而削弱CROQ的效果。为此，我们提出了CP-OPT，一种优化框架，用于学习最小化集合大小同时保持覆盖范围的评分方法。我们在MMLU、ToolAlpaca和TruthfulQA数据集上对多个LLM进行的广泛实验表明，CROQ在准确性上优于标准推理，特别是在与CP-OPT结合使用时，效果更为显著。

[No.137] Relational Conformal Prediction for Correlated Time Series
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 相关性核验预测在相关时间序列中的应用
类别: general machine learning->sequential network and time series modeling
作者: Andrea Cini;Alexander Jenkins;Danilo Mandic;Cesare Alippi;Filippo Maria Bianchi;

链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43601
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09443
英摘: We address the problem of uncertainty quantification in time series forecasting by exploiting observations at correlated sequences. Relational deep learning methods leveraging graph representations are among the most effective tools for obtaining point estimates from spatiotemporal data and correlated time series. However, the problem of exploiting relational structures to estimate the uncertainty of such predictions has been largely overlooked in the same context. To this end, we propose a novel distribution-free approach based on the conformal prediction framework and quantile regression. Despite the recent applications of conformal prediction to sequential data, existing methods operate independently on each target time series and do not account for relationships among them when constructing the prediction interval. We fill this void by introducing a novel conformal prediction method based on graph deep learning operators. Our approach, named Conformal Relational Prediction (CoRel), does not require the relational structure (graph) to be known a priori and can be applied on top of any pre-trained predictor. Additionally, CoRel includes an adaptive component to handle non-exchangeable data and changes in the input time series. Our approach provides accurate coverage and achieves state-of-the-art uncertainty quantification in relevant benchmarks.
中摘: 我们通过利用相关序列中的观测值来解决时间序列预测中的不确定性量化问题。基于图表示的关联深度学习方法是获得时空数据和相关时间序列点估计最有效的工具之一。然而，在相同背景下，利用关联结构来估计此类预测的不确定性的问题已被很大程度上忽视。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种基于符合性预测框架和分位数回归的新颖无分布方法。尽管最近将符合性预测应用于序列数据，但现有方法独立地对每个目标时间序列进行操作，并在构建预测区间时未考虑它们之间的关系。我们通过引入一种基于图深度学习算子的新型符合性预测方法填补了这一空白。我们的方法名为Conformal Relational Prediction（CoRel），不需要事先知道关联结构（图），并且可以在任何预训练预测器之上应用。此外，CoRel 包含一个自适应组件以处理非可交换数据以及输入时间序列的变化。我们的方法提供了准确的覆盖范围，并在相关基准测试中实现了最先进的不确定性量化。

[No.139] From Feature Interaction to Feature Generation: A Generative Paradigm of CTR Prediction Models
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 从特征交互到特征生成：一种生成范式的点击率预测模型
类别: applications
作者: Mingjia Yin;Junwei Pan;Hao Wang;Ximei Wang;Shangyu Zhang;Jie Jiang;Defu Lian;Enhong Chen;
机构: University of Science and Technology of China;Tencent;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45999
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14041
英摘: Click-Through Rate (CTR) prediction, a core task in recommendation systems, aims to estimate the probability of users clicking on items. Existing models predominantly follow a discriminative paradigm, which relies heavily on explicit interactions between raw ID embeddings. However, this paradigm inherently renders them susceptible to two critical issues: embedding dimensional collapse and information redundancy, stemming from the over-reliance on feature interactions \emph{over raw ID embeddings}. To address these limitations, we propose a novel \emph{Supervised Feature Generation (SFG)} framework, \emph{shifting the paradigm from discriminative ``feature interaction" to generative ``feature generation"}. Specifically, SFG comprises two key components: an \emph{Encoder} that constructs hidden embeddings for each feature, and a \emph{Decoder} tasked with regenerating the feature embeddings of all features from these hidden representations. Unlike existing generative approaches that adopt self-supervised losses, we introduce a supervised loss to utilize the supervised signal, \ie, click or not, in the CTR prediction task. This framework exhibits strong generalizability: it can be seamlessly integrated with most existing CTR models, reformulating them under the generative paradigm. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SFG consistently mitigates embedding collapse and reduces information redundancy, while yielding substantial performance gains across various datasets and base models. The code is available at https://github.com/USTC-StarTeam/GE4Rec.
中摘: 点击率（CTR）预测是推荐系统中的一个核心任务，旨在估计用户点击项目的概率。现有模型主要遵循判别式范式，这依赖于原始ID嵌入之间的显式交互。然而，这种范式本质上使它们容易受到两个关键问题的影响：嵌入维度坍缩和信息冗余，这些问题源于过度依赖特征交互而非原始ID嵌入。为了解决这些限制，我们提出了一种新颖的监督特征生成（SFG）框架，将范式从判别式的“特征交互”转变为生成式的“特征生成”。具体而言，SFG 包含两个关键组件：一个编码器，用于为每个特征构建隐藏嵌入；以及一个解码器，负责从这些隐藏表示再生所有特征的嵌入。与现有的生成方法采用自监督损失不同，我们引入了监督损失来利用CTR预测任务中的监督信号，即点击或不点击。该框架表现出强大的泛化能力：它可以无缝地与大多数现有的CTR模型集成，并在生成范式下重新定义它们。广泛的实验表明，SFG 一致地缓解了嵌入维度坍缩并减少了信息冗余，同时在各种数据集和基础模型上取得了显著的性能提升。相关代码可在 https://github.com/USTC-StarTeam/GE4Rec 获取。

[No.140] Topology-aware Neural Flux Prediction Guided by Physics

[No.141] WGFormer: An SE(3)-Transformer Driven by Wasserstein Gradient Flows for Molecular Ground-State Conformation Prediction
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: WGFormer：一种由 Wasserstein 梯度流驱动的 SE(3) 变换器，用于分子基态构象预测
类别: applications->chemistry physics and earth sciences
作者: Fanmeng Wang;Minjie Cheng;Hongteng Xu;
机构: Renmin University of China;BioMap Research;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46561
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09795
英摘: Predicting molecular ground-state conformation (i.e., energy-minimized conformation) is crucial for many chemical applications such as molecular docking and property prediction. Classic energy-based simulation is time-consuming when solving this problem, while existing learning-based methods have advantages in computational efficiency but sacrifice accuracy and interpretability. In this work, we propose a novel and effective method to bridge the energy-based simulation and the learning-based strategy, which designs and learns a Wasserstein gradient flow-driven SE(3)-Transformer, called WGFormer, for ground-state conformation prediction. Specifically, our method tackles this task within an auto-encoding framework, which encodes low-quality conformations by the proposed WGFormer and decodes corresponding ground-state conformations by an MLP. The architecture of WGFormer corresponds to Wasserstein gradient flows -- it optimizes conformations by minimizing an energy function defined on the latent mixture models of atoms, thereby significantly improving performance and interpretability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art competitors, providing a new and insightful paradigm to predict ground-state conformation.

[No.142] BoxLM: Unifying Structures and Semantics of Medical Concepts for Diagnosis Prediction in Healthcare

[No.145] PEAKS: Selecting Key Training Examples Incrementally via Prediction Error Anchored by Kernel Similarity
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: PEAKS：通过核相似性锚定预测误差增量选择关键训练示例
类别: deep learning->algorithms
作者: Mustafa Burak Gurbuz;Xingyu Zheng;Constantine Dovrolis;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;Cold Spring Harbor Laboratory;Cyprus Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45861
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05250
英摘: As deep learning continues to be driven by ever-larger datasets, understanding which examples are most important for generalization has become a critical question. While progress in data selection continues, emerging applications require studying this problem in dynamic contexts. To bridge this gap, we pose the Incremental Data Selection (IDS) problem, where examples arrive as a continuous stream, and need to be selected without access to the full data source. In this setting, the learner must incrementally build a training dataset of predefined size while simultaneously learning the underlying task. We find that in IDS, the impact of a new sample on the model state depends fundamentally on both its geometric relationship in the feature space and its prediction error. Leveraging this insight, we propose PEAKS (Prediction Error Anchored by Kernel Similarity), an efficient data selection method tailored for IDS. Our comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that PEAKS consistently outperforms existing selection strategies. Furthermore, PEAKS yields increasingly better performance returns than random selection as training data size grows on real-world datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/BurakGurbuz97/PEAKS.
中摘: 随着深度学习不断依赖于越来越大的数据集，理解哪些示例对泛化最为重要已成为一个关键问题。尽管在数据选择方面的进展继续进行，但新兴应用需要在这种动态背景下研究这个问题。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了增量数据选择（IDS）问题，其中示例以连续流的形式到来，并且需要在无法访问完整数据源的情况下进行选择。在这种情况下，学习者必须逐步构建一个预定义大小的训练数据集，同时学习底层任务。我们发现，在IDS中，新样本对模型状态的影响从根本上取决于其在特征空间中的几何关系及其预测误差。利用这一洞察，我们提出了一种名为PEAKS（基于核相似性的预测误差锚定）的有效数据选择方法，该方法专门针对IDS。我们的全面评估表明，PEAKS始终优于现有的选择策略。此外，在真实世界的数据集上，随着训练数据量的增加，PEAKS的表现逐渐优于随机选择。代码可在https://github.com/BurakGurbuz97/PEAKS获取。

[No.146] On Temperature Scaling and Conformal Prediction of Deep Classifiers
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 温度归一化与深度分类器的齐性预测
类别: deep learning->everything else
作者: Lahav Dabah;Tom Tirer;
机构: Bar-Ilan University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44049
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.05806
英摘: In many classification applications, the prediction of a deep neural network (DNN) based classifier needs to be accompanied by some confidence indication. Two popular approaches for that aim are: 1) Calibration: modifies the classifier's softmax values such that the maximal value better estimates the correctness probability; and 2) Conformal Prediction (CP): produces a prediction set of candidate labels that contains the true label with a user-specified probability, guaranteeing marginal coverage but not, e.g., per class coverage. In practice, both types of indications are desirable, yet, so far the interplay between them has not been investigated. Focusing on the ubiquitous Temperature Scaling (TS) calibration, we start this paper with an extensive empirical study of its effect on prominent CP methods. We show that while TS calibration improves the class-conditional coverage of adaptive CP methods, surprisingly, it negatively affects their prediction set sizes. Motivated by this behavior, we explore the effect of TS on CP beyond its calibration application and reveal an intriguing trend under which it allows to trade prediction set size and conditional coverage of adaptive CP methods. Then, we establish a mathematical theory that explains the entire non-monotonic trend. Finally, based on our experiments and theory, we offer simple guidelines for practitioners to effectively combine adaptive CP with calibration, aligned with user-defined goals.
中摘: 在许多分类应用中，深度神经网络（DNN）分类器的预测需要伴随一定的置信度指示。实现这一目标的两种流行方法是：1）校准：修改分类器的softmax值，使得最大值更好地估计正确性概率；和2）置信预测（Conformal Prediction，CP）：生成一个包含真实标签的候选标签集合，该集合以用户指定的概率包含真实标签，保证边际覆盖，但不保证类别的覆盖。实践中，这两种类型的指示都是需要的，然而到目前为止，它们之间的相互作用尚未被研究。本文聚焦于普遍使用的温度校准（Temperature Scaling，TS），首先通过广泛的实证研究探讨了其对主流CP方法的影响。我们发现虽然TS校准可以提高自适应CP方法的条件覆盖，但令人惊讶的是，它会负面影响预测集的大小。受此行为启发，我们进一步探索了TS对CP的影响，揭示了一种有趣的趋势，在这种趋势下，TS允许在自适应CP方法的预测集大小和条件覆盖之间进行权衡。最后，我们建立了一个数学理论来解释整个非单调趋势。基于我们的实验和理论，我们为实践者提供了简单的指导方针，以有效地将自适应CP与校准结合使用，从而符合用户定义的目标。

[No.147] Learnable Spatial-Temporal Positional Encoding for Link Prediction
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 可学习的空间-时间位置编码用于链接预测
类别: deep learning->graph neural networks
作者: Katherine Tieu;Dongqi Fu;Zihao Li;Ross Maciejewski;Jingrui He;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;Meta;Arizona State University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45924
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08309
英摘: Accurate predictions rely on the expressiveness power of graph deep learning frameworks like graph neural networks and graph transformers, where a positional encoding mechanism has become much more indispensable in recent state-of-the-art works to record the canonical position information. However, the current positional encoding is limited in three aspects: (1) most positional encoding methods use pre-defined, and fixed functions, which are inadequate to adapt to the complex attributed graphs; (2) a few pioneering works proposed the learnable positional encoding but are still limited to the structural information, not considering the real-world time-evolving topological and feature information; (3) most positional encoding methods are equipped with transformers' attention mechanism to fully leverage their capabilities, where the dense or relational attention is often unaffordable on large-scale structured data. Hence, we aim to develop Learnable Spatial-Temporal Positional Encoding in an effective and efficient manner and propose a simple temporal link prediction model named L-STEP. Briefly, for L-STEP, we (1) prove the proposed positional learning scheme can preserve the graph property from the spatial-temporal spectral viewpoint, (2) verify that MLPs can fully exploit the expressiveness and reach transformers' performance on that encoding, (3) change different initial positional encoding inputs to show robustness, (4) analyze the theoretical complexity and obtain less empirical running time than SOTA, and (5) demonstrate its temporal link prediction out-performance on 13 classic datasets and with 10 algorithms in both transductive and inductive settings using 3 different sampling strategies. Also, L-STEP obtains the leading performance in the newest large-scale TGB benchmark. Our code is available at https://github.com/kthrn22/L-STEP.
中摘: 准确的预测依赖于图深度学习框架（如图神经网络和图变换器）的表达能力，其中位置编码机制在最近的前沿工作中变得越来越不可或缺，用于记录经典的位置信息。然而，当前的位置编码在三个方面存在局限性：（1）大多数位置编码方法使用预定义且固定的函数，这些函数不足以适应复杂的属性图；（2）少数开创性的工作提出了可学习的位置编码，但仍然局限于结构信息，没有考虑现实世界中的时间演化拓扑和特征信息；（3）大多数位置编码方法配备了Transformer的注意力机制，以充分利用其能力，但在大规模结构数据上，密集或关系型的注意力往往是不可承受的。因此，我们旨在以有效且高效的方式开发可学习的空间-时间位置编码，并提出了一种简单的临时链接预测模型L-STEP。具体来说，对于L-STEP，我们证明了提出的定位学习方案可以从空间-时间频谱的角度保留图的性质，验证了多层感知机（MLPs）可以充分挖掘这种编码的表达能力并达到Transformer的性能，展示了不同初始位置编码输入的鲁棒性，分析了理论复杂度并获得了比SOTA更少的经验运行时间，并通过三种不同的采样策略，在归纳和传递设置下，证明了其在13个经典数据集和10种算法上的临时链接预测表现优于其他方法。此外，L-STEP在最新的大规模TGB基准测试中也取得了领先性能。我们的代码可在https://github.com/kthrn22/L-STEP获取。
