[No.149] A Causal World Model Underlying Next Token Prediction: Exploring GPT in a Controlled Environment
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于因果世界模型的下一个词预测：探索在受控环境中GPT模型
类别: general machine learning->causality
作者: Raanan Yehezkel Rohekar;Yaniv Gurwicz;Sungduk Yu;Estelle Aflalo;Vasudev Lal;
机构: Intel;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/43959
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07446
英摘: Are generative pre-trained transformer (GPT) models, trained only to predict the next token, implicitly learning a world model from which sequences are generated one token at a time? We address this question by deriving a causal interpretation of the attention mechanism in GPT and presenting a causal world model that arises from this interpretation. Furthermore, we propose that GPT models, at inference time, can be utilized for zero-shot causal structure learning for input sequences, and introduce a corresponding confidence score. Empirical tests were conducted in controlled environments using the setups of the Othello and Chess strategy games. A GPT, pre-trained on real-world games played with the intention of winning, was tested on out-of-distribution synthetic data consisting of sequences of random legal moves. We find that the GPT model is likely to generate legal next moves for out-of-distribution sequences for which a causal structure is encoded in the attention mechanism with high confidence. In cases where it generates illegal moves, it also fails to capture a causal structure.
中摘: 生成预训练变换器（GPT）模型仅通过预测下一个标记是否隐含地从一个一次生成一个标记的序列中学习了世界模型？我们通过推导GPT中的注意力机制的因果解释来回答这个问题，并提出了由此解释产生的一种因果世界模型。此外，我们提出，在推理阶段，GPT模型可以用于输入序列的零样本因果结构学习，并引入相应的置信度分数。我们在控制环境中使用国际象棋和象棋策略游戏的设置进行了实证测试。测试了一个在具有获胜意图的实际游戏中预训练的GPT模型，该模型被应用于分布外合成数据，这些数据由随机合法走法组成的序列构成。我们发现，对于在注意力机制中编码了因果结构的分布外序列，GPT模型很可能以高置信度生成合法的下一步走法。在生成非法走法的情况下，它也无法捕捉到因果结构。

[No.151] Stochastic Online Conformal Prediction with Semi-Bandit Feedback
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于半 bandeit 反馈的随机在线一致预测
类别: general machine learning->online learning active learning and bandits
作者: Haosen Ge;Hamsa Bastani;Osbert Bastani;
机构: University of Pennsylvania;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45727
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13268
英摘: Conformal prediction has emerged as an effective strategy for uncertainty quantification by modifying a model to output sets of labels instead of a single label. These prediction sets come with the guarantee that they contain the true label with high probability. However, conformal prediction typically requires a large calibration dataset of i.i.d. examples. We consider the online learning setting, where examples arrive over time, and the goal is to construct prediction sets dynamically. Departing from existing work, we assume semi-bandit feedback, where we only observe the true label if it is contained in the prediction set. For instance, consider calibrating a document retrieval model to a new domain; in this setting, a user would only be able to provide the true label if the target document is in the prediction set of retrieved documents. We propose a novel conformal prediction algorithm targeted at this setting, and prove that it obtains sublinear regret compared to the optimal conformal predictor. We evaluate our algorithm on a retrieval task, an image classification task, and an auction price-setting task, and demonstrate that it empirically achieves good performance compared to several baselines.
中摘: 自适应预测作为一种有效策略，通过修改模型使其输出标签集而不是单个标签，已经成为了不确定性量化的一种手段。这些预测集能够保证以高概率包含真实的标签。然而，自适应预测通常需要一个大的独立同分布样例的校准数据集。我们考虑了一种在线学习设置，在这种设置中，样例会随着时间陆续到来，目标是在动态构建预测集的同时进行学习。不同于现有工作，我们假设了半带宽反馈（semi-bandit feedback），即我们仅在真实标签位于预测集中时才能观察到它。例如，设想对文档检索模型进行新的领域校准；在这种情况下，用户只能提供真实标签，如果目标文档出现在检索文档的预测集中。我们提出了一种针对这一场景的新颖自适应预测算法，并证明该算法相对于最优自适应预测器获得了亚线性后悔率。我们在检索任务、图像分类任务以及拍卖价格设定任务上评估了该算法，并展示了其在多种基准方法中取得了良好的实际性能。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.152] PlaySlot: Learning Inverse Latent Dynamics for Controllable Object-Centric Video Prediction and Planning
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: PlaySlot：学习逆隐含动力学以实现可控的物体为中心的视频预测和规划
类别: general machine learning->representation learning
作者: Angel Villar-Corrales;Sven Behnke;
机构: University of Bonn;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45399
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07600
英摘: Predicting future scene representations is a crucial task for enabling robots to understand and interact with the environment. However, most existing methods rely on videos and simulations with precise action annotations, limiting their ability to leverage the large amount of available unlabeled video data. To address this challenge, we propose PlaySlot, an object-centric video prediction model that infers object representations and latent actions from unlabeled video sequences. It then uses these representations to forecast future object states and video frames. PlaySlot allows the generation of multiple possible futures conditioned on latent actions, which can be inferred from video dynamics, provided by a user, or generated by a learned action policy, thus enabling versatile and interpretable world modeling. Our results show that PlaySlot outperforms both stochastic and object-centric baselines for video prediction across different environments. Furthermore, we show that our inferred latent actions can be used to learn robot behaviors sample-efficiently from unlabeled video demonstrations. Videos and code are available on https://play-slot.github.io/PlaySlot/.
中摘: 预测未来场景表示是使机器人理解和互动环境的关键任务。然而，现有大多数方法依赖于带有精确动作标注的视频和模拟，这限制了它们利用大量可用的未标注视频数据的能力。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出了PlaySlot，这是一种以对象为中心的视频预测模型，可以从未标注的视频序列中推断出对象表示和潜在动作。然后，它使用这些表示来预测未来对象的状态和视频帧。PlaySlot允许根据潜在动作生成多种可能的未来场景，这些潜在动作可以来自视频动力学、用户提供的信息或通过学习的动作策略生成，从而实现灵活且可解释的世界建模。我们的结果显示，PlaySlot在不同环境下的视频预测任务中均优于随机性和以对象为中心的基线模型。此外，我们展示了从未标注的视频示范中高效学习机器人行为的能力，可以通过我们推断出的潜在动作来实现。更多视频和代码可在https://play-slot.github.io/PlaySlot/ 获取。

[No.153] Set Valued Predictions For Robust Domain Generalization
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 面向鲁棒领域泛化的集值预测
类别: general machine learning->transfer multitask and metalearning
作者: Ron Tsibulsky;Daniel Nevo;Uri Shalit;
机构: ;Tel Aviv University;Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45305
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03146
英摘: Despite the impressive advancements in modern machine learning, achieving robustness in Domain Generalization (DG) tasks remains a significant challenge. In DG, models are expected to perform well on samples from unseen test distributions (also called domains), by learning from multiple related training distributions. Most existing approaches to this problem rely on single-valued predictions, which inherently limit their robustness. We argue that set-valued predictors could be leveraged to enhance robustness across unseen domains, while also taking into account that these sets should be as small as possible. We introduce a theoretical framework defining successful set prediction in the DG setting, focusing on meeting a predefined performance criterion across as many domains as possible, and provide theoretical insights into the conditions under which such domain generalization is achievable. We further propose a practical optimization method compatible with modern learning architectures, that balances robust performance on unseen domains with small prediction set sizes. We evaluate our approach on several real-world datasets from the WILDS benchmark, demonstrating its potential as a promising direction for robust domain generalization.
中摘: 尽管现代机器学习取得了令人印象深刻的进展，但在域泛化（DG）任务中实现稳健性仍然是一项重大挑战。在DG任务中，模型期望能够在未见过的测试分布（也称为域）上的样本上表现良好，通过从多个相关训练分布中学习。目前大多数解决这一问题的方法依赖于单值预测，这固有限制了它们的稳健性。我们认为，在DG设置中利用集合值预测可以增强对未见过域的稳健性，同时这些集合应尽可能小。我们提出了一种理论框架来定义在DG设置中成功的集合预测，重点在于尽可能多地满足预定义的性能标准，并提供了关于在何种条件下这种域泛化是可以实现的理论见解。此外，我们还提出了一个与现代学习架构兼容的实际优化方法，该方法平衡了在未见过的域上稳健的表现与预测集合大小的最小化。我们在WILDS基准上的几个真实世界数据集上评估了我们的方法，展示了其作为稳健域泛化有前景方向的潜力。

[No.154] Clipped SGD Algorithms for Performative Prediction: Tight Bounds for Stochastic Bias and Remedies

[No.155] Understanding Fixed Predictions via Confined Regions
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 通过受限区域理解固定预测
类别: social aspects->accountability transparency and interpretability
作者: Connor Lawless;Tsui-Wei Weng;Berk Ustun;Madeleine Udell;
机构: Stanford University;University of California, San Diego;Harvard University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46631
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16380
英摘: Machine learning models can assign fixed predictions that preclude individuals from changing their outcome. Existing approaches to audit fixed predictions do so on a pointwise basis, which requires access to an existing dataset of individuals and may fail to anticipate fixed predictions in out-of-sample data. This work presents a new paradigm to identify fixed predictions by finding confined regions of the feature space in which all individuals receive fixed predictions. This paradigm enables the certification of recourse for out-of-sample data, works in settings without representative datasets, and provides interpretable descriptions of individuals with fixed predictions. We develop a fast method to discover confined regions for linear classifiers using mixed-integer quadratically constrained programming. We conduct a comprehensive empirical study of confined regions across diverse applications. Our results highlight that existing pointwise verification methods fail to anticipate future individuals with fixed predictions, while our method both identifies them and provides an interpretable description.
中摘: 机器学习模型可以分配固定的预测，这些预测不允许个人改变其结果。现有的审计固定预测的方法通常是基于点的，这需要访问现有的个体数据集，并且可能无法预见样本外数据中的固定预测。本研究提出了一种新的范式，通过在所有个体都收到固定预测的特征空间受限区域内寻找来识别固定预测。这种范式能够在没有代表性数据集的情况下认证样本外数据的可回溯性，并为具有固定预测的个体提供可解释的描述。我们开发了一种快速方法，使用混合整数二次约束规划来发现线性分类器中的受限区域。我们在多种应用中进行了全面的经验研究。我们的结果显示，现有的基于点的验证方法无法预见未来具有固定预测的个体，而我们的方法不仅能识别它们，还能提供一个可解释的描述。

[No.156] Quantifying Prediction Consistency Under Fine-tuning Multiplicity in Tabular LLMs
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 量化表格式大语言模型微调多重性下的预测一致性
类别: social aspects->robustness
作者: Faisal Hamman;Pasan Dissanayake;Saumitra Mishra;Freddy Lecue;Sanghamitra Dutta;
机构: University of Maryland;JPMorgan Chase & Co.;INRIA;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46165
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04173
英摘: Fine-tuning LLMs on tabular classification tasks can lead to the phenomenon of fine-tuning multiplicity where equally well-performing models make conflicting predictions on the same input. Fine-tuning multiplicity can arise due to variations in the training process, e.g., seed, weight initialization, minor changes to training data, etc., raising concerns about the reliability of Tabular LLMs in high-stakes applications such as finance, hiring, education, healthcare. Our work formalizes this unique challenge of fine-tuning multiplicity in Tabular LLMs and proposes a novel measure to quantify the consistency of individual predictions without expensive model retraining. Our measure quantifies a prediction's consistency by analyzing (sampling) the model's local behavior around that input in the embedding space. Interestingly, we show that sampling in the local neighborhood can be leveraged to provide probabilistic guarantees on prediction consistency under a broad class of fine-tuned models, i.e., inputs with sufficiently high local stability (as defined by our measure) also remain consistent across several fine-tuned models with high probability. We perform experiments on multiple real-world datasets to show that our local stability measure preemptively captures consistency under actual multiplicity across several fine-tuned models, outperforming competing measures.
中摘: 在表格分类任务上微调大型语言模型（LLMs）可能会导致微调多重性的现象，即表现同样良好的模型在同一输入上做出相互矛盾的预测。这种多重性可能源于训练过程中的变化，例如随机种子、权重初始化、训练数据的小幅更改等，从而引发了对金融、招聘、教育和医疗等高风险应用中表格LLMs可靠性的担忧。我们的研究正式定义了表格LLMs中这种独特的微调多重性挑战，并提出了一种新颖的度量方法，用于量化单个预测的一致性而无需昂贵的模型重新训练。该度量通过分析模型在嵌入空间中该输入周围的局部行为来量化预测的一致性。有趣的是，我们展示了在局部邻域进行采样可以利用其来提供广泛的微调模型下预测一致性的概率保证，即根据我们提出的度量定义，具有足够高局部稳定性的输入也更有可能在多个高精度微调模型中保持一致性。我们在多个真实世界的数据集上进行了实验，证明了我们的局部稳定性度量能够前瞻性地捕捉到多个微调模型中的实际一致性，优于其他竞争度量方法。

[No.158] Prediction-Aware Learning in Multi-Agent Systems
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 多智能体系统的预测感知学习
类别: theory->online learning and bandits
作者: Aymeric Capitaine;Etienne Boursier;Eric Moulines;Michael I. Jordan;Alain Oliviero Durmus;
机构: Ecole Polytechnique;INRIA;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45828
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.19144
英摘: The framework of uncoupled online learning in multiplayer games has made significant progress in recent years. In particular, the development of time-varying games has considerably expanded its modeling capabilities. However, current regret bounds quickly become vacuous when the game undergoes significant variations over time, even when these variations are easy to predict. Intuitively, the ability of players to forecast future payoffs should lead to tighter guarantees, yet existing approaches fail to incorporate this aspect. This work aims to fill this gap by introducing a novel prediction-aware framework for time-varying games, where agents can forecast future payoffs and adapt their strategies accordingly. In this framework, payoffs depend on an underlying state of nature that agents predict in an online manner. To leverage these predictions, we propose the POWMU algorithm, a contextual extension of the optimistic Multiplicative Weight Update algorithm, for which we establish theoretical guarantees on social welfare and convergence to equilibrium. Our results demonstrate that, under bounded prediction errors, the proposed framework achieves performance comparable to the static setting. Finally, we empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of POWMU in a traffic routing experiment.
中摘: 近年来，多人游戏中解耦在线学习框架取得了显著进展，特别是时间变化游戏的发展极大地扩展了其建模能力。然而，当前的遗憾界线在游戏经历显著时间变化时会迅速变得无效，即使这些变化很容易预测也是如此。直观上，玩家能够预测未来的收益应该会导致更严格的保证，但现有的方法未能将这一方面纳入考虑。本项工作旨在通过引入一种新的预测感知框架来填补这一空白，在这种框架下，代理可以预测未来的收益并相应地调整策略。在这个框架中，收益取决于代理在线预测的一种潜在状态。为了利用这些预测，我们提出了POWMU算法，这是一种基于上下文的乐观加权更新算法的扩展，并建立了其在社会福利和收敛到均衡方面的理论保证。我们的结果显示，在预测误差有界的情况下，所提出的框架能够实现与静态设置相当的表现。最后，我们在一个交通路由实验中实证展示了POWMU的有效性。

英摘: Dynamic graphs evolve continuously, presenting challenges for traditional graph learning due to their changing structures and temporal dependencies. However, most meta-learning approaches for dynamic graphs rely on fixed weight update parameters, neglecting the essential intrinsic complex high-order topological information of dynamically evolving graphs. [No.162] Efficient Robust Conformal Prediction via Lipschitz-Bounded Networks
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 通过Lipschitz有界网络实现的高效稳健同构预测
类别: deep learning->robustness
作者: Thomas Massena;Léo Andéol;Thibaut Boissin;Franck Mamalet;Corentin Friedrich;Mathieu Serrurier;Sébastien Gerchinovitz;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05434
英摘: Conformal Prediction (CP) has proven to be an effective post-hoc method for improving the trustworthiness of neural networks by providing prediction sets with finite-sample guarantees. However, under adversarial attacks, classical conformal guarantees do not hold anymore: this problem is addressed in the field of Robust Conformal Prediction. Several methods have been proposed to provide robust CP sets with guarantees under adversarial perturbations, but, for large scale problems, these sets are either too large or the methods are too computationally demanding to be deployed in real life scenarios. In this work, we propose a new method that leverages Lipschitz-bounded networks to precisely and efficiently estimate robust CP sets. When combined with a 1-Lipschitz robust network, we demonstrate that our lip-rcp method outperforms state-of-the-art results in both the size of the robust CP sets and computational efficiency in medium and large-scale scenarios such as ImageNet. Taking a different angle, we also study vanilla CP under attack, and derive new worst-case coverage bounds of vanilla CP sets, which are valid simultaneously for all adversarial attack levels. Our lip-rcp method makes this second approach as efficient as vanilla CP while also allowing robustness guarantees.
中摘: 自适应预测（Conformal Prediction, CP）已被证明是一种有效的后处理方法，能够通过提供具有有限样本保证的预测集来提高神经网络的信任度。然而，在对抗攻击下，经典的自适应预测保证不再适用：这一问题在鲁棒自适应预测领域得到了解决。已经提出了一些方法来提供在对抗扰动下具有保证的鲁棒CP集，但在大规模问题中，这些集要么太大，要么计算成本太高，无法在实际场景中部署。在本研究中，我们提出了一种新方法，该方法利用Lipschitz有界网络精确且高效地估计鲁棒CP集。当与1-Lipschitz鲁棒网络结合使用时，我们的lip-rcp方法在ImageNet等中大规模场景中，在鲁棒CP集的大小和计算效率方面均优于现有最佳结果。从另一个角度出发，我们还研究了在攻击下的vanilla CP，并推导出新的适用于所有对抗攻击水平的最坏情况覆盖边界。我们的lip-rcp方法使这种方法在效率上与vanilla CP相当，同时也能提供鲁棒性保证。

[No.163] IMTS is Worth Time $\times$ Channel Patches: Visual Masked Autoencoders for Irregular Multivariate Time Series Prediction
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: IMTS 值得时间 $\times$ 通道补丁：视觉遮蔽自编码器在不规则多变量时间序列预测中的应用

作者: Zhangyi Hu;Jiemin Wu;Hua XU;Mingqian Liao;Ninghui Feng;Bo Gao;Songning Lai;Yutao Yue;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22815
英摘: Irregular Multivariate Time Series (IMTS) forecasting is challenging due to the unaligned nature of multi-channel signals and the prevalence of extensive missing data. Existing methods struggle to capture reliable temporal patterns from such data due to significant missing values. While pre-trained foundation models show potential for addressing these challenges, they are typically designed for Regularly Sampled Time Series (RTS). Motivated by the visual Mask AutoEncoder's (MAE) powerful capability for modeling sparse multi-channel information and its success in RTS forecasting, we propose VIMTS, a framework adapting Visual MAE for IMTS forecasting. To mitigate the effect of missing values, VIMTS first processes IMTS along the timeline into feature patches at equal intervals. These patches are then complemented using learned cross-channel dependencies. Then it leverages visual MAE's capability in handling sparse multichannel data for patch reconstruction, followed by a coarse-to-fine technique to generate precise predictions from focused contexts. In addition, we integrate self-supervised learning for improved IMTS modeling by adapting the visual MAE to IMTS data. Extensive experiments demonstrate VIMTS's superior performance and few-shot capability, advancing the application of visual foundation models in more general time series tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/WHU-HZY/VIMTS.
中摘: 不规则多变量时间序列（IMTS）预测因其多通道信号的不对齐性质以及大量缺失数据的存在而具有挑战性。现有方法难以从这种数据中捕捉可靠的时序模式，主要是因为存在大量的缺失值。虽然预训练的基础模型在解决这些挑战方面显示出潜力，但它们通常设计用于规则采样时间序列（RTS）。受视觉Mask AutoEncoder（MAE）在建模稀疏多通道信息方面的强大能力及其在规则采样时间序列预测中的成功应用的启发，我们提出了一种VIMTS框架，该框架将视觉MAE适应于IMTS预测。为了减轻缺失值的影响，VIMTS首先沿着时间线将IMTS以等间隔分割成特征片段，然后通过学习到的跨通道依赖关系对这些片段进行补充。接着利用视觉MAE处理稀疏多通道数据的能力进行片段重建，并采用粗到细的技术从聚焦的上下文中生成精确的预测。此外，我们还通过将视觉MAE适应于IMTS数据来整合自我监督学习，以改进IMTS建模。广泛的实验表明，VIMTS具有优越的性能和少样本能力，推动了视觉基础模型在更广泛的时间序列任务中的应用。我们的代码可在https://github.com/WHU-HZY/VIMTS获取。

[No.164] The Relationship Between No-Regret Learning and Online Conformal Prediction
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 无遗憾学习与在线符合预测的关系
类别: general machine learning->online learning active learning and bandits
作者: Ramya Ramalingam;Shayan Kiyani;Aaron Roth;
机构: University of Pennsylvania;Amazon;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45708
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10947
英摘: Existing algorithms for online conformal prediction -- guaranteeing marginal coverage in adversarial settings -- are variants of online gradient descent (OGD), but their analyses of worst-case coverage do not follow from the regret guarantee of OGD. What is the relationship between no-regret learning and online conformal prediction? We observe that although standard regret guarantees imply marginal coverage in i.i.d. settings, this connection fails as soon as we either move to adversarial environments or ask for group conditional coverage. On the other hand, we show a tight connection between threshold calibrated coverage and swap-regret in adversarial settings, which extends to group-conditional (multi-valid) coverage. We also show that algorithms in the follow the perturbed leader family of no regret learning algorithms (which includes online gradient descent) can be used to give group-conditional coverage guarantees in adversarial settings for arbitrary grouping functions. Via this connection we analyze and conduct experiments using a multi-group generalization of the ACI algorithm of Gibbs & Candes [2021] (arXiv:2106.00170).
中摘: 现有的在线自适应预测算法——在对抗性环境中保证边际覆盖——是在线梯度下降（OGD）的变体，但它们对最坏情况覆盖的分析并不基于OGD的后悔保证。无悔学习与在线自适应预测之间存在什么样的关系？我们观察到，虽然标准的后悔保证在独立同分布（i.i.d.）设置中暗示了边际覆盖，但这一联系一旦进入对抗性环境或要求组条件覆盖就会失效。另一方面，我们展示了阈值校准覆盖与对抗性环境中的交换后悔之间的紧密联系，这种联系可以扩展到组条件（多有效）覆盖。我们还证明了无悔学习算法中的跟随扰动领导者家族（包括在线梯度下降）的算法可以在对抗性环境中为任意分组函数提供组条件覆盖保证。通过这一联系，我们分析并使用Gibbs & Candes [2021]（arXiv:2106.00170）的ACI算法的多组推广版本进行了实验。

[No.165] Improved Approximations for Hard Graph Problems using Predictions
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 使用预测改进困难图问题的近似算法
类别: theory
作者: Anders Aamand;Justin Y. Chen;Siddharth Gollapudi;Sandeep Silwal;Hao WU;
机构: University of Copenhagen;;University of Wisconsin-Madison;University of Waterloo;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/46430
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23967
英摘: We design improved approximation algorithms for NP-hard graph problems by incorporating predictions (e.g., learned from past data). Our prediction model builds upon and extends the $\varepsilon$-prediction framework by Cohen-Addad, d'Orsi, Gupta, Lee, and Panigrahi (NeurIPS 2024). We consider an edge-based version of this model, where each edge provides two bits of information, corresponding to predictions about whether each of its endpoints belong to an optimal solution. Even with weak predictions where each bit is only $\varepsilon$-correlated with the true solution, this information allows us to break approximation barriers in the standard setting. We develop algorithms with improved approximation ratios for MaxCut, Vertex Cover, Set Cover, and Maximum Independent Set problems (among others). Across these problems, our algorithms share a unifying theme, where we separately satisfy constraints related to high degree vertices (using predictions) and low-degree vertices (without using predictions) and carefully combine the answers.
中摘: 我们通过融入预测（例如从过往数据中学习）设计了改进的近似算法来解决NP难图问题。我们的预测模型建立在Cohen-Addad、d'Orsi、Gupta、Lee和Panigrahi（NeurIPS 2024）的$\varepsilon$-预测框架之上，并对其进行了扩展。我们考虑了这种模型的基于边的版本，其中每条边提供两比特信息，对应于其端点是否属于最优解的预测。即使在这种弱预测情况下，每个比特仅与真实解$\varepsilon$相关，这些信息也允许我们在标准设置中打破近似障碍。我们为MaxCut、Vertex Cover、Set Cover和Maximum Independent Set等问题（以及其他问题）开发了具有改进近似比的算法。在这些问题中，我们的算法有一个统一的主题，即分别使用预测满足高度顶点相关的约束，而不使用预测满足低度顶点相关的约束，并仔细地将这些答案结合起来。

[No.166] QuEst: Enhancing Estimates of Quantile-Based Distributional Measures Using Model Predictions

[No.171] Zero-shot Meta-learning for Tabular Prediction Tasks with Adversarially Pre-trained Transformer
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 针对表格预测任务的零样本元学习与对抗预训练变换器

英摘: We present an Adversarially Pre-trained Transformer (APT) that is able to perform zero-shot meta-learning on tabular prediction tasks without pre-training on any real-world dataset, extending on the recent development of Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) and TabPFN. Specifically, APT is pre-trained with adversarial synthetic data agents, who continue to shift their underlying data generating distribution and deliberately challenge the model with different synthetic datasets. In addition, we propose a mixture block architecture that is able to handle classification tasks with arbitrary number of classes, addressing the class size limitation -- a crucial weakness of prior deep tabular zero-shot learners. In experiments, we show that our framework matches state-of-the-art performance on small classification tasks without filtering on dataset characteristics such as number of classes and number of missing values, while maintaining an average runtime under one second. On common benchmark dataset suites in both classification and regression, we show that adversarial pre-training was able to enhance TabPFN's performance. In our analysis, we demonstrate that the adversarial synthetic data agents were able to generate a more diverse collection of data compared to the ordinary random generator in TabPFN. In addition, we demonstrate that our mixture block neural design has improved generalizability and greatly accelerated pre-training.

[No.172] Prediction via Shapley Value Regression
来源: ICML 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 通过舍勒值回归进行预测
类别: social aspects->accountability transparency and interpretability
作者: Amr Alkhatib;Roman Bresson;Henrik Boström;Michalis Vazirgiannis;
机构: ;KTH Royal Institute of Technology;Ecole Polytechnique;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44871
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04775
英摘: Shapley values have several desirable, theoretically well-supported, properties for explaining black-box model predictions. Traditionally, Shapley values are computed post-hoc, leading to additional computational cost at inference time. To overcome this, a novel method, called ViaSHAP, is proposed, that learns a function to compute Shapley values, from which the predictions can be derived directly by summation. Two approaches to implement the proposed method are explored; one based on the universal approximation theorem and the other on the Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem. Results from a large-scale empirical investigation are presented, showing that ViaSHAP using Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks performs on par with state-of-the-art algorithms for tabular data. It is also shown that the explanations of ViaSHAP are significantly more accurate than the popular approximator FastSHAP on both tabular data and images.
中摘: Shapley值在解释黑盒模型预测方面具有若干理想的、理论支持的良好属性。传统上，Shapley值是在事后计算的，这会在推理阶段增加额外的计算成本。为克服这一问题，提出了一种名为ViaSHAP的新方法，该方法学习一个函数来计算Shapley值，从而可以通过直接求和的方式从该函数推导出预测。探索了实现该方法的两种途径；一种基于通用逼近定理，另一种基于柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺尔德表示定理。大规模实证研究的结果表明，使用柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺尔德网络的ViaSHAP在表格数据上的表现与最先进的算法相当。同时，研究表明ViaSHAP的解释比流行的近似器FastSHAP在表格数据和图像上更为准确。

[No.173] Frame Context Packing and Drift Prevention in Next-Frame-Prediction Video Diffusion Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 下一帧预测视频扩散模型中的框架上下文打包与漂移预防

英摘: We present a neural network structure, FramePack, to train next-frame (or next-frame-section) prediction models for video generation. FramePack compresses input frame contexts with frame-wise importance so that more frames can be encoded within a fixed context length, with more important frames having longer contexts. The frame importance can be measured using time proximity, feature similarity, or hybrid metrics. The packing method allows for inference with thousands of frames and training with relatively large batch sizes. We also present drift prevention methods to address observation bias (error accumulation), including early-established endpoints, adjusted sampling orders, and discrete history representation. Finally, we show that existing video diffusion models can be finetuned with FramePack, and analyze the differences between different packing schedules.

[No.174] High-order Equivariant Flow Matching for Density Functional Theory Hamiltonian Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 高阶对称流匹配方法在密度功能理论哈密顿量预测中的应用
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Seongsu Kim;Nayoung Kim;Dongwoo Kim;Sungsoo Ahn;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Pohang University of Science and Technology;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=iFIjNXb0Y5
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18817
英摘: Density functional theory (DFT) is a fundamental method for simulating quantum chemical properties, but it remains expensive due to the iterative self-consistent field (SCF) process required to solve the Kohn-Sham equations. Recently, deep learning methods are gaining attention as a way to bypass this step by directly predicting the Hamiltonian. However, they rely on deterministic regression and do not consider the highly structured nature of Hamiltonians. In this work, we propose QHFlow, a high-order equivariant flow matching framework that generates Hamiltonian matrices conditioned on molecular geometry. Flow matching models continuous-time trajectories between simple priors and complex targets, learning the structured distributions over Hamiltonians instead of direct regression. To further incorporate symmetry, we use a neural architecture that predicts SE(3)-equivariant vector fields, improving accuracy and generalization across diverse geometries. To further enhance physical fidelity, we additionally introduce a fine-tuning scheme to align predicted orbital energies with the target. QHFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance, reducing Hamiltonian error by 71% on MD17 and 53% on QH9. Moreover, we further show that QHFlow accelerates the DFT process without trading off the solution quality when initializing SCF iterations with the predicted Hamiltonian, significantly reducing the number of iterations and runtime.
中摘: 密度泛函理论（DFT）是一种用于模拟量子化学性质的基本方法，但由于需要通过迭代自洽场（SCF）过程来求解科恩-沙姆方程，它仍然非常昂贵。最近，深度学习方法引起了关注，因为它们可以通过直接预测哈密顿量来绕过这一步骤。然而，这些方法依赖于确定性回归，并没有考虑到哈密顿量的高度结构化特性。在这项工作中，我们提出了QHFlow，这是一种高阶等变流匹配框架，可以根据分子几何结构生成条件化的哈密顿矩阵。流匹配模型在简单的先验和复杂的靶标之间连续时间轨迹，学习哈密顿量的结构分布而非直接回归。为了进一步引入对称性，我们使用了一种神经架构来预测SE(3)-等变向量场，从而提高了不同几何结构下的准确性和泛化能力。为了进一步增强物理保真度，我们还引入了一种微调方案，以使预测的轨道能量与目标一致。QHFlow实现了最先进的性能，在MD17数据集上将哈密顿误差降低了71%，在QH9数据集上降低了53%。此外，我们还展示了QHFlow可以在用预测的哈密顿量初始化SCF迭代时加速DFT过程，而不牺牲解决方案的质量，显著减少了迭代次数和运行时间。

[No.175] Reinforcement Learning with Imperfect Transition Predictions: A Bellman-Jensen Approach
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于不完美转换预测的强化学习：贝尔曼-詹森方法
类别: theory
作者: Chenbei Lu;Zaiwei Chen;Tongxin Li;Chenye Wu;Adam Wierman;
机构: Cornell University;Tsinghua University;;Chinese University of Hong Kong;California Institute of Technology;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=DYuPwwDy9n
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.18687
英摘: Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) assumes the agents make decisions based on Markov decision processes (MDPs) with one-step transition models. In many real-world applications, such as energy management and stock investment, agents can access multi-step predictions of future states, which provide additional advantages for decision making. However, multi-step predictions are inherently high-dimensional: naively embedding these predictions into an MDP leads to an exponential blow-up in state space and the curse of dimensionality. Moreover, existing RL theory provides few tools to analyze prediction-augmented MDPs, as it typically works on one-step transition kernels and cannot accommodate multi-step predictions with errors or partial action-coverage. We address these challenges with three key innovations: First, we propose the \emph{Bayesian value function} to characterize the optimal prediction-aware policy tractably. Second, we develop a novel \emph{Bellman-Jensen Gap} analysis on the Bayesian value function, which enables characterizing the value of imperfect predictions. Third, we introduce BOLA (Bayesian Offline Learning with Online Adaptation), a two-stage model-based RL algorithm that separates offline Bayesian value learning from lightweight online adaptation to real-time predictions. We prove that BOLA remains sample-efficient even under imperfect predictions. We validate our theory and algorithm on synthetic MDPs and a real-world wind energy storage control problem.
中摘: 传统强化学习（RL）假设智能体基于马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）进行一步转移模型的决策。在许多实际应用中，如能源管理与股票投资，智能体可以访问对未来状态的多步预测，这为决策提供了额外的优势。然而，多步预测本质上是高维度的：直接将这些预测嵌入到MDP中会导致状态空间的指数级膨胀和维数灾难。此外，现有的RL理论对分析预测增强的MDP提供的工具很少，因为它们通常仅处理一步转移核，并且无法应对带有误差或部分动作覆盖的多步预测。我们通过三项关键创新来应对这些挑战：首先，我们提出**贝叶斯价值函数**以简洁地描述预测感知的最优策略；其次，我们开发了针对贝叶斯价值函数的新型**贝尔曼-詹森间隙**分析，这使得我们能够刻画不完美预测的价值；最后，我们引入了BOLA（贝叶斯离线学习与在线适应），这是一种两阶段模型导向的RL算法，它将离线的贝叶斯价值学习与轻量级的在线实时预测适应分开。我们证明即使在不完美的预测下，BOLA仍然保持样本效率。我们在合成MDP和一个实际的风能存储控制问题上验证了我们的理论和算法。

[No.177] Credal Prediction based on Relative Likelihood
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于相对似然性的信任预测
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Timo Löhr;Paul Hofman;Felix Mohr;Eyke Hüllermeier;
机构: ;Ludwig-Maximilians-Universität München;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22332
英摘: Predictions in the form of sets of probability distributions, so-called credal sets, provide a suitable means to represent a learner's epistemic uncertainty. In this paper, we propose a theoretically grounded approach to credal prediction based on the statistical notion of relative likelihood: The target of prediction is the set of all (conditional) probability distributions produced by the collection of plausible models, namely those models whose relative likelihood exceeds a specified threshold. This threshold has an intuitive interpretation and allows for controlling the trade-off between correctness and precision of credal predictions. We tackle the problem of approximating credal sets defined in this way by means of suitably modified ensemble learning techniques. To validate our approach, we illustrate its effectiveness by experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrating superior uncertainty representation without compromising predictive performance. We also compare our method against several state-of-the-art baselines in credal prediction.
中摘: 以集合形式的概率分布预测，即所谓的信任集，为表示学习者的认识不确定性提供了一种合适的手段。在本文中，我们提出了一种基于统计相对似然性的理论支持方法来实现信任预测：预测的目标是所有由可能模型生成的（条件）概率分布集合，即那些相对似然性超过指定阈值的模型。该阈值具有直观的解释，并允许控制信任预测的正确性和精确性之间的权衡。为了近似这样定义的信任集，我们采用适当修改的集成学习技术来解决这个问题。通过在基准数据集上的实验，我们展示了这种方法的有效性，在不牺牲预测性能的情况下提供了更好的不确定性表示。此外，我们还将我们的方法与几种最新的信任预测基线进行了比较。

[No.182] FAPEX: Fractional Amplitude-Phase Expressor for Robust Cross-Subject Seizure Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: FAPEX：分数振幅相位表达器，用于稳健的跨subject癫痫发作预测

英摘: Precise, generalizable subject-agnostic seizure prediction (SASP) remains a fundamental challenge due to the intrinsic complexity and significant spectral variability of electrophysiological signals across individuals and recording modalities. Unlike conventional models that exhibit spectral bias toward low frequencies, our FrNFO employs fractional-order convolutions to capture both high and low-frequency dynamics, achieving approximately 10% improvement in F1-score and sensitivity over state-of-the-art baselines. The FrNFO enables the extraction of instantaneous phase and amplitude representations that are particularly informative for preictal biomarker discovery and enhance out-of-distribution generalization. FAPEX further integrates structural state-space modeling and channelwise attention, allowing it to handle heterogeneous electrode montages. Evaluated across 12 benchmarks spanning species (human, rat, dog, macaque) and modalities (Scalp-EEG, SEEG, ECoG, LFP), FAPEX consistently outperforms 23 supervised and 10 self-supervised baselines under nested cross-validation, with gains of up to 15% in sensitivity on complex cross-domain scenarios. It further demonstrates superior performance in several external validation cohorts. To our knowledge, these establish FAPEX as the first epilepsy model to show consistent superiority in SASP, offering a promising solution for discovering epileptic biomarker evidence supporting the existence of a distinct and identifiable preictal state and clinical translation.
中摘: 精确且通用的无特定主题癫痫发作预测（SASP）仍然是一个基本挑战，原因在于个体之间和记录模态之间的电生理信号固有的复杂性和显著的频谱变异性。我们提出了一种名为FAPEX的新架构，该架构引入了可学习的分数神经帧操作符（FrNFO）以实现自适应时频分解。与传统模型倾向于低频谱偏见不同，我们的FrNFO采用分数阶卷积来捕捉高频和低频动态，相比最先进的基线模型，在F1分数和灵敏度上分别实现了约10%的提升。FrNFO能够提取瞬时相位和振幅表示，这些表示特别有助于预发作生物标志物的发现，并增强对分布外数据的一般化能力。FAPEX进一步结合了结构状态空间建模和通道注意力机制，使其能够处理异质性电极配置。在跨物种（人类、大鼠、狗、猕猴）和记录模态（头皮脑电图、深部脑电图、皮层脑电图、局部场电位）的12个基准测试中，FAPEX在嵌套交叉验证下始终优于23个监督和10个半监督基线模型，特别是在复杂的跨域场景中，灵敏度提高了高达15%。此外，FAPEX在外部验证队列中的表现也明显优越。据我们所知，这些结果确立了FAPEX作为首个在SASP方面表现出一致优越性的癫痫模型的地位，为支持存在独特且可识别的预发作状态以及临床转化提供了有希望的解决方案。

[No.183] Evolutionary Prediction Games
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 进化预测游戏
类别: theory
作者: Eden Saig;Nir Rosenfeld;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=qsYbytjmQK
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03401
英摘: When a prediction algorithm serves a collection of users, disparities in prediction quality are likely to emerge. If users respond to accurate predictions by increasing engagement, inviting friends, or adopting trends, repeated learning creates a feedback loop that shapes both the model and the population of its users. In this work, we introduce evolutionary prediction games, a framework grounded in evolutionary game theory which models such feedback loops as natural-selection processes among groups of users. Our theoretical analysis reveals a gap between idealized and real-world learning settings: In idealized settings with unlimited data and computational power, repeated learning creates competition and promotes competitive exclusion across a broad class of behavioral dynamics. However, under realistic constraints such as finite data, limited compute, or risk of overfitting, we show that stable coexistence and mutualistic symbiosis between groups becomes possible. We analyze these possibilities in terms of their stability and feasibility, present mechanisms that can sustain their existence, and empirically demonstrate our findings.
中摘: 当预测算法服务于一组用户时，预测质量的差异很可能会出现。如果用户对准确的预测作出反应，比如增加参与度、邀请朋友或采纳趋势，反复学习会形成一个反馈循环，从而塑造模型及其用户的群体动态。在本项研究中，我们引入了进化预测游戏这一框架，该框架基于进化博弈理论，将这种反馈循环建模为用户群体之间的自然选择过程。我们的理论分析揭示了理想化与现实世界学习环境之间的差距：在无限数据和计算能力的理想环境中，反复学习会产生竞争并促进各种行为动态中的竞争排斥。然而，在有限的数据、有限的计算资源或过拟合风险等现实约束下，我们展示了不同群体之间稳定共存和互利共生的可能性。我们从稳定性和可行性角度分析这些可能性，并提出了可以维持其存在的机制，同时通过实验验证了我们的发现。

[No.184] Maximizing the Value of Predictions in Control: Accuracy Is Not Enough
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 最大化控制中的预测价值：准确度已不足
类别: theory
作者: Yiheng Lin;Christopher Yeh;Zaiwei Chen;Adam Wierman;
机构: California Institute of Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=1YCb07JMyl
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04497
英摘: We study the value of stochastic predictions in online optimal control with random disturbances. Prior work provides performance guarantees based on prediction error but ignores the stochastic dependence between predictions and disturbances. We introduce a general framework modeling their joint distribution and define "prediction power" as the control cost improvement from the optimal use of predictions compared to ignoring the predictions. In the time-varying Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) setting, we derive a closed-form expression for prediction power and discuss its mismatch with prediction accuracy and connection with online policy optimization. To extend beyond LQR, we study general dynamics and costs. We establish a lower bound of prediction power under two sufficient conditions that generalize the properties of the LQR setting, characterizing the fundamental benefit of incorporating stochastic predictions. We apply this lower bound to non-quadratic costs and show that even weakly dependent predictions yield significant performance gains.
中摘: 我们研究了随机预测在具有随机干扰的在线最优控制中的价值。以往的工作提供了基于预测误差的性能保证，但忽略了预测与干扰之间的随机依赖性。我们引入了一个通用框架来建模它们的联合分布，并定义“预测能力”为最优利用预测相比忽略预测所能提高的控制成本。在时间变化的线性二次调节器（LQR）设置中，我们推导出预测能力的闭式表达式，并讨论其与预测准确性的不匹配以及与在线策略优化的关系。为了超越LQR，我们研究了一般动态和成本。我们在两个足以推广LQR设置属性的充分条件下建立了预测能力的下界，从而刻画了整合随机预测的基本优势。我们将这个下界应用于非二次成本，并表明即使弱相关的预测也能带来显著的性能提升。

[No.185] Abstain Mask Retain Core: Time Series Prediction by Adaptive Masking Loss with Representation Consistency
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 弃用掩码保留核心：自适应掩码损失与表示一致性的时间序列预测
类别: deep learning
作者: Renzhao Liang;Sizhe Xu;Chenggang Xie;Jingru Chen;Feiyang Ren;Shu Yang;Takahiro Yabe;
机构: Beihang University;New York University;Beijing University of Aeronautics and Astronautics;Peking University;University of Leeds;

英摘: Time series forecasting plays a pivotal role in critical domains such as energy management and financial markets. Although deep learning-based approaches (e.g., MLP, RNN, Transformer) have achieved remarkable progress, the prevailing "long-sequence information gain hypothesis" exhibits inherent limitations. Through systematic experimentation, this study reveals a counterintuitive phenomenon: appropriately truncating historical data can paradoxically enhance prediction accuracy, indicating that existing models learn substantial redundant features (e.g., noise or irrelevant fluctuations) during training, thereby compromising effective signal extraction. Building upon information bottleneck theory, we propose an innovative solution termed Adaptive Masking Loss with Representation Consistency (AMRC), which features two core components: 1) Dynamic masking loss, which adaptively identified highly discriminative temporal segments to guide gradient descent during model training; 2) Representation consistency constraint, which stabilized the mapping relationships among inputs, labels, and predictions. Experimental results demonstrate that AMRC effectively suppresses redundant feature learning while significantly improving model performance. This work not only challenges conventional assumptions in temporal modeling but also provides novel theoretical insights and methodological breakthroughs for developing efficient and robust forecasting models.
中摘: 时间序列预测在能源管理、金融市场等领域发挥着关键作用。尽管基于深度学习的方法（如MLP、RNN、Transformer）已经取得了显著进展，但主导的“长序列信息增益假说”却存在固有的局限性。通过系统的实验，本研究揭示了一个出乎意料的现象：适当截断历史数据反而可以提升预测准确性，这表明现有模型在训练过程中学会了大量的冗余特征（例如噪声或无关波动），从而影响了有效信号的提取。基于信息瓶颈理论，我们提出了一种名为自适应掩码损失与表示一致性（AMRC）的创新解决方案，其核心组成部分包括：1）动态掩码损失，该组件能够自适应地识别出具有高度区分性的时序段落，以指导模型训练过程中的梯度下降；2）表示一致性约束，该组件稳定了输入、标签和预测之间的映射关系。实验结果表明，AMRC有效地抑制了冗余特征的学习，显著提升了模型性能。这项工作不仅挑战了时间序列建模中的传统假设，还为开发高效且稳健的预测模型提供了新的理论见解和方法论突破。

[No.186] Accident Anticipation via Temporal Occurrence Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 事故预见通过时间发生预测
类别: deep learning
作者: Tianhao Zhao;Yiyang Zou;Zihao Mao;Peilun Xiao;Yulin Huang;Hongda Yang;Yuxuan Li;Qun Li;Guobin Wu;Yutian Lin;
机构: Wuhan University;Didi Research;;DiDi;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=7N6pJb16sh
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22260
英摘: Accident anticipation aims to predict potential collisions in an online manner, enabling timely alerts to enhance road safety. Existing methods typically predict frame-level risk scores as indicators of hazard. However, these approaches rely on ambiguous binary supervision (labeling all frames in accident videos as positive) despite the fact that risk varies continuously over time, leading to unreliable learning and false alarms. To address this, we propose a novel paradigm that shifts the prediction target from current-frame risk scoring to directly estimating accident scores at multiple future time steps (e.g., 0.1s-2.0s ahead), leveraging precisely annotated accident timestamps as supervision. Our method employs a snippet-level encoder to jointly model spatial and temporal dynamics, and a Transformer-based temporal decoder that predicts accident scores for all future horizons simultaneously using dedicated temporal queries. Furthermore, we introduce a refined evaluation protocol that reports Time-to-Accident (TTA) and recall (evaluated at multiple pre-accident intervals (0.5s, 1.0s, and 1.5s)) only when the false alarm rate (FAR) remains within an acceptable range, ensuring practical relevance. Experiments show that our method achieves superior performance in both recall and TTA under realistic FAR constraints.
中摘: 事故预判旨在在线预测潜在碰撞，以便及时发出警报以提升道路安全。现有方法通常预测帧级风险评分作为危险的指标，但这些方法依赖于模糊的二元监督（即将事故视频中的所有帧标记为正样本），尽管风险随时间连续变化，导致学习不可靠并产生误报警。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新的范式，将预测目标从当前帧的风险评分转移到直接估计多个未来时间步长的事故评分（例如，在事故发生前0.1秒至2.0秒），利用精确标注的事故时间戳作为监督信息。我们的方法采用片段级编码器联合建模空间和时间动态，并使用基于Transformer的时间解码器同时预测所有未来的事故评分，通过专用的时间查询来实现。此外，我们引入了一个改进的评估协议，仅在误报警率（FAR）保持在可接受范围内时报告事故前的时间（Time-to-Accident，TTA）和召回率（分别在事故发生前0.5秒、1.0秒和1.5秒的多个时间间隔内进行评估），确保其实用性。实验表明，在实际的FAR约束条件下，我们的方法在召回率和TTA方面均表现出优越性能。

[No.187] Joint‑Embedding vs Reconstruction: Provable Benefits of Latent Space Prediction for Self‑Supervised Learning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 联合嵌入 vs 重构：潜在空间预测在自我监督学习中的可证明优势
类别: deep learning
作者: Hugues Van Assel;Mark Ibrahim;Tommaso Biancalani;Aviv Regev;Randall Balestriero;
机构: Genentech;Meta;;Brown University;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=UOaLsgn5wb
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12477
英摘: Reconstruction and joint embedding have emerged as two leading paradigms in Self Supervised Learning (SSL). Reconstruction methods focus on recovering the original sample from a different view in input space. On the other hand, joint embedding methods align the representations of different views in latent space. Both approaches offer compelling advantages, yet practitioners lack clear guidelines for choosing between them. In this work, we unveil the core mechanisms that distinguish each paradigm. By leveraging closed form solutions for both approaches, we precisely characterize how the view generation process, e.g. data augmentation, impacts the learned representations. We then demonstrate that, unlike supervised learning, both SSL paradigms require a minimal alignment between augmentations and irrelevant features to achieve asymptotic optimality with increasing sample size. Our findings indicate that in scenarios where these irrelevant features have a large magnitude, joint embedding methods are preferable because they impose a strictly weaker alignment condition compared to reconstruction based methods. These results not only clarify the trade offs between the two paradigms but also substantiate the empirical success of joint embedding approaches on real world challenging datasets.
中摘: 自监督学习（SSL）中涌现出两种主要范式：重建和联合嵌入。重建方法侧重于从输入空间的不同视图中恢复原始样本。另一方面，联合嵌入方法则在潜在空间中对不同视图的表示进行对齐。这两种方法各有优势，但实践者缺乏明确的指南来选择其中一种。在这项工作中，我们揭示了区分每种范式的核心机制。通过利用两种方法的闭形式解，我们精确地描述了视图生成过程，例如数据增强，如何影响学到的表示。我们还证明，在样本数量增加时，与有监督学习不同，这两种SSL范式都需要在增强和无关特征之间保持最小的对齐才能达到渐近最优性。我们的发现表明，在这些无关特征具有较大值的情况下，联合嵌入方法更可取，因为它们施加的对齐条件比基于重建的方法要弱得多。这些结果不仅阐明了这两种范式之间的权衡，还证实了在现实世界具有挑战性的数据集上，联合嵌入方法的实证成功。

[No.188] Neural MJD: Neural Non-Stationary Merton Jump Diffusion for Time Series Prediction

英摘: While deep learning methods have achieved strong performance in time series prediction, their black-box nature and inability to explicitly model underlying stochastic processes often limit their generalization to non-stationary data, especially in the presence of abrupt changes. In this work, we introduce Neural MJD, a neural network based non-stationary Merton jump diffusion (MJD) model. Our model explicitly formulates forecasting as a stochastic differential equation (SDE) simulation problem, combining a time-inhomogeneous Itô diffusion to capture non-stationary stochastic dynamics with a time-inhomogeneous compound Poisson process to model abrupt jumps. To enable tractable learning, we introduce a likelihood truncation mechanism that caps the number of jumps within small time intervals and provide a theoretical error bound for this approximation. Additionally, we propose an Euler-Maruyama with restart solver, which achieves a provably lower error bound in estimating expected states and reduced variance compared to the standard solver. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that Neural MJD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art deep learning and statistical learning methods.
中摘: 尽管深度学习方法在时间序列预测方面取得了出色的表现，但它们的黑盒性质以及无法明确建模潜在的随机过程常常限制了其在非平稳数据上的泛化能力，尤其是在存在突然变化的情况下。在本文中，我们介绍了基于神经网络的非平稳梅特恩跳跃扩散（MJD）模型——Neural MJD。我们的模型将预测明确地表述为一个随机微分方程（SDE）模拟问题，结合了时变Itô扩散来捕捉非平稳的随机动态，并使用时变复合泊松过程来建模突然的跳跃。为了使学习变得可处理，我们引入了一种似然性截断机制，该机制限制了小时间间隔内的跳跃次数，并为此近似提供了理论误差界。此外，我们提出了一种重新启动的欧拉-马科维茨解算器，该解算器在估计期望状态和降低方差方面相比标准解算器具有可证明更低的误差界。在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，Neural MJD 一直优于最先进的深度学习和统计学习方法。

[No.189] Toward a Unified Geometry Understanding : Riemannian Diffusion Framework for Graph Generation and Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 统一几何理解：黎曼扩散框架在图生成与预测中的应用

英摘: Graph diffusion models have made significant progress in learning structured graph data and have demonstrated strong potential for predictive tasks. Existing approaches typically embed node, edge, and graph-level features into a unified latent space, modeling prediction tasks including classification and regression as a form of conditional generation. However, due to the non-Euclidean nature of graph data, features of different curvatures are entangled in the same latent space without releasing their geometric potential. To address this issue, we aim to construt an ideal Riemannian diffusion model to capture distinct manifold signatures of complex graph data and learn their distribution. This goal faces two challenges: numerical instability caused by exponential mapping during the encoding proces and manifold deviation during diffusion generation. To address these challenges, we propose GeoMancer: a novel Riemannian graph diffusion framework for both generation and prediction tasks. To mitigate numerical instability, we replace exponential mapping with an isometric-invariant Riemannian gyrokernel approach and decouple multi-level features onto their respective task-specific manifolds to learn optimal representations. To address manifold deviation, we introduce a manifold-constrained diffusion method and a self-guided strategy for unconditional generation, ensuring that the generated data remains aligned with the manifold signature. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our approach, demonstrating superior performance across a variety of tasks.
中摘: 图扩散模型在学习结构化图数据方面取得了显著进展，并且展示了在预测任务中强大的潜力。现有方法通常将节点、边和图级特征嵌入到统一的潜在空间中，将预测任务（包括分类和回归）建模为条件生成的一种形式。然而，由于图数据的非欧几里得性质，在同一潜在空间中不同曲率的特征交织在一起，未能释放它们的几何潜力。为了解决这一问题，我们旨在构建一个理想的黎曼扩散模型，以捕捉复杂图数据的独特流形特征，并学习其分布。实现这一目标面临两个挑战：编码过程中由指数映射引起的数值不稳定性和扩散生成过程中的流形偏差。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种名为GeoMancer的新颖黎曼图扩散框架，适用于生成和预测任务。为了缓解数值不稳定，我们用等距不变的黎曼陀螺核方法替代了指数映射，并将多级特征解耦到各自的任务特定流形上，以学习最优表示。为了应对流形偏差，我们引入了一种受流形约束的扩散方法和一种自引导策略，确保生成的数据与流形特征保持一致。广泛的实验验证了我们方法的有效性，展示了其在各种任务中的优越性能。

英摘: Distribution-free uncertainty estimation for ensemble methods is increasingly desirable due to the widening deployment of multi-modal black-box predictive models. Merging the prediction regions directly, however, sacrifices structures present in the conformal scores that can further reduce conservatism. [No.194] Conformal Prediction Beyond the Seen: A Missing Mass Perspective for Uncertainty Quantification in Generative Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于“超出已见”的同构预测：生成模型中不确定性量化的一种缺失质量视角
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Sima Noorani;Shayan Kiyani;George J. Pappas;Hamed Hassani;
机构: University of Pennsylvania;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=KoVKLxn3Nb
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05497
英摘: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for safe deployment of generative AI models such as large language models (LLMs), especially in high stakes applications. Conformal prediction (CP) offers a principled uncertainty quantification framework, but classical methods focus on regression and classification, relying on geometric distances or softmax scores: tools that presuppose structured outputs. We depart from this paradigm by studying CP in a query only setting, where prediction sets must be constructed solely from finite queries to a black box generative model, introducing a new trade off between coverage, test time query budget, and informativeness. We introduce Conformal Prediction with Query Oracle (CPQ), a framework characterizing the optimal interplay between these objectives. Our finite sample algorithm is built on two core principles: one governs the optimal query policy, and the other defines the optimal mapping from queried samples to prediction sets. Remarkably, both are rooted in the classical missing mass problem in statistics. Specifically, the optimal query policy depends on the rate of decay, or the derivative, of the missing mass, for which we develop a novel estimator. Meanwhile, the optimal mapping hinges on the missing mass itself, which we estimate using Good Turing estimators. We then turn our focus to implementing our method for language models, where outputs are vast, variable, and often under specified. Fine grained experiments on three real world open ended tasks and two LLMs, show CPQ applicability to any black box LLM and highlight: (1) individual contribution of each principle to CPQ performance, and (2) CPQ ability to yield significantly more informative prediction sets than existing conformal methods for language uncertainty quantification.
中摘: 不确定性量化（UQ）对于安全部署生成式AI模型，如大型语言模型（LLMs），尤其是在高风险应用中至关重要。形式化预测（CP）提供了一种原则性的不确定性量化框架，但传统方法主要集中在回归和分类上，依赖于几何距离或softmax分数：这些工具假定了结构化的输出。我们偏离了这一范式，通过在仅查询设置下研究CP，即预测集必须完全基于对黑盒生成模型的有限查询构建，引入了一个新的权衡，即覆盖范围、测试时间查询预算与信息量之间的权衡。我们提出了形式化预测带查询Oracle（CPQ）框架，该框架描述了这些目标之间最优的相互作用。我们的有限样本算法基于两个核心原则：一个原则管理最优查询策略，另一个原则定义从查询样本到预测集的最优映射。令人惊讶的是，这两个原则都根植于统计学中的经典缺失质量问题。具体而言，最优查询策略取决于缺失质量衰减的速度，或者说是缺失质量的导数，为此我们开发了一个新的估计器。同时，最优映射依赖于缺失质量本身，我们使用Good-Turing估计器来估计缺失质量。随后，我们将注意力转向语言模型的实现，其中输出庞大、变化多端且往往不明确。针对三个实际开放任务和两种LLM进行的精细实验表明，CPQ适用于任何黑盒LLM，并突显了以下两点：（1）每个原则对CPQ性能的独立贡献；（2）CPQ能够显著提高语言不确定性量化中预测集的信息量。

[No.195] Improved Bounds for Swap Multicalibration and Swap Omniprediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 改进的换位多校准和换位全预测边界
类别: theory
作者: Haipeng Luo;Spandan Senapati;Vatsal Sharan;
机构: University of Southern California;;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=pXoiIDdynI
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20885
英摘: In this paper, we consider the related problems of multicalibration -- a multigroup fairness notion and omniprediction -- a simultaneous loss minimization paradigm, both in the distributional and online settings. The recent work of Garg et al. (2024) raised the open problem of whether it is possible to efficiently achieve $O(\sqrt{T})$ $\ell_{2}$-multicalibration error against bounded linear functions. In this paper, we answer this question in a strongly affirmative sense. We propose an efficient algorithm that achieves $O(T^{\frac{1}{3}})$ $\ell_{2}$-swap multicalibration error (both in high probability and expectation). On propagating this bound onward, we obtain significantly improved rates for $\ell_{1}$-swap multicalibration and swap omniprediction for a loss class of convex Lipschitz functions. In particular, we show that our algorithm achieves $O(T^{\frac{2}{3}})$ $\ell_{1}$-swap multicalibration and swap omniprediction errors, thereby improving upon the previous best-known bound of $O(T^{\frac{7}{8}})$. As a consequence of our improved online results, we further obtain several improved sample complexity rates in the distributional setting. In particular, we establish a $O(\varepsilon ^ {-3})$ sample complexity of efficiently learning an $\varepsilon$-swap omnipredictor for the class of convex and Lipschitz functions, $O(\varepsilon ^{-2.5})$ sample complexity of efficiently learning an $\varepsilon$-swap agnostic learner for the squared loss, and $O(\varepsilon ^ {-5}), O(\varepsilon ^ {-2.5})$ sample complexities of learning $\ell_{1}, \ell_{2}$-swap multicalibrated predictors against linear functions, all of which significantly improve on the previous best-known bounds.
中摘: 在本文中，我们考虑了多校准——一种多组公平性概念——和全预测——一种同时损失最小化范式的相关问题，分别在分布性和在线设置中进行探讨。Garg等人（2024）的工作提出了一个开放问题：是否有可能高效地实现针对有界线性函数的$O(\sqrt{T})$ $\ell_{2}$-多校准误差。在本文中，我们以非常肯定的态度回答了这个问题。我们提出了一种高效的算法，该算法能够实现$O(T^{\frac{1}{3}})$ $\ell_{2}$-交换多校准误差（在高概率和期望意义上）。通过进一步推广这一界，我们获得了显著改进的$\ell_{1}$-交换多校准和交换全预测错误率，特别是对于凸Lipschitz损失类。特别地，我们证明了我们的算法能够实现$O(T^{\frac{2}{3}})$ $\ell_{1}$-交换多校准和交换全预测错误，从而优于之前最佳已知的$O(T^{\frac{7}{8}})$错误率。由于我们改进的在线结果，我们在分布性设置中还获得了几个显著改进的样本复杂度率。具体而言，我们证明了对于凸和Lipschitz函数类高效学习$\varepsilon$-交换全预测器需要$O(\varepsilon^{-3})$的样本复杂度，高效学习对平方损失的$\varepsilon$-交换agnostic学习器需要$O(\varepsilon^{-2.5})$的样本复杂度，以及学习针对线性函数的$\ell_{1}, \ell_{2}$-交换多校准预测器需要$O(\varepsilon^{-5}), O(\varepsilon^{-2.5})$的样本复杂度，所有这些都显著改进了之前的最佳已知样本复杂度。

[No.196] Smooth Quadratic Prediction Markets
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 平滑二次预测市场
类别: theory
作者: Enrique Nueve;Bo Waggoner;
机构: University of Colorado;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=d3FjdOwLEd
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02959
英摘: When agents trade in a Duality-based Cost Function prediction market, they collectively implement the learning algorithm Follow-The-Regularized-Leader. We ask whether other learning algorithms could be used to inspire the design of prediction markets. By decomposing and modifying the Duality-based Cost Function Market Maker's (DCFMM) pricing mechanism, we propose a new prediction market, called the Smooth Quadratic Prediction Market, the incentivizes agents to collectively implement general steepest gradient descent. Relative to the DCFMM, the Smooth Quadratic Prediction Market has a better worst-case monetary loss for AD securities while preserving axiom guarantees such as the existence of instantaneous price, information incorporation, expressiveness, no arbitrage, and a form of incentive compatibility. To motivate the application of the Smooth Quadratic Prediction Market, we independently examine agents' trading behavior under two realistic constraints: bounded budgets and buy-only securities. Finally, we provide an introductory analysis of an approach to facilitate adaptive liquidity using the Smooth Quadratic Prediction Market. Our results suggest future designs where the price update rule is separate from the fee structure, yet guarantees are preserved.
中摘: 当代理在基于二元性成本函数的预测市场中交易时，他们共同实现了跟随正则化领先者的学习算法。我们探讨是否可以使用其他学习算法来启发预测市场的设计。通过分解并修改基于二元性成本函数市场制作人的（DCFMM）定价机制，我们提出了一种新的预测市场，称为平滑二次预测市场，该市场激励代理共同实施广义最速梯度下降。与DCFMM相比，平滑二次预测市场在AD证券方面具有更好的最坏情况货币损失，同时保留了诸如即时价格的存在性、信息整合、表达能力、无套利和一种形式的激励相容等公理保证。为了促进平滑二次预测市场的应用，我们独立地考察了代理在两种现实约束下的交易行为：预算限制和只能买入的证券。最后，我们提供了关于如何利用平滑二次预测市场促进适应性流动性的初步分析。我们的研究结果表明，未来的设计可以在价格更新规则与费用结构分离的情况下仍然保持这些保证。

[No.198] ARMesh: Autoregressive Mesh Generation via Next-Level-of-Detail Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: ARMesh：通过预测下一层次细节实现的自回归网格生成
类别: applications
作者: Jiabao Lei;Kewei Shi;Zhihao Liang;Kui Jia;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;University of Hong Kong;Tencent;South China University of Technology;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=xlQ4QUB9VC
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20824
英摘: Directly generating 3D meshes, the default representation for 3D shapes in the graphics industry, using auto-regressive (AR) models has become popular these days, thanks to their sharpness, compactness in the generated results, and ability to represent various types of surfaces. However, AR mesh generative models typically construct meshes face by face in lexicographic order, which does not effectively capture the underlying geometry in a manner consistent with human perception. Inspired by 2D models that progressively refine images, such as the prevailing next-scale prediction AR models, we propose generating meshes auto-regressively in a progressive coarse-to-fine manner. Specifically, we view mesh simplification algorithms, which gradually merge mesh faces to build simpler meshes, as a natural fine-to-coarse process. Therefore, we generalize meshes to simplicial complexes and develop a transformer-based AR model to approximate the reverse process of simplification in the order of level of detail, constructing meshes initially from a single point and gradually adding geometric details through local remeshing, where the topology is not predefined and is alterable. Our experiments show that this novel progressive mesh generation approach not only provides intuitive control over generation quality and time consumption by early stopping the auto-regressive process but also enables applications such as mesh refinement and editing.
中摘: 直接使用自回归（AR）模型生成三维网格，这是图形行业默认表示三维形状的方式，如今变得越来越流行，得益于其生成结果的清晰度、紧凑性以及能够表示各种类型的表面。然而，AR网格生成模型通常按字典顺序逐个构建网格面，这种方式在捕捉底层几何结构方面并不符合人类的感知方式。受到逐步细化图像的2D模型，如盛行的下一级预测AR模型的启发，我们提出了一种逐步从粗到细生成网格的方法。具体而言，我们将网格简化算法视为一种自然的从细到粗的过程，这些算法逐渐合并网格面以构建更简单的网格。因此，我们将网格推广为单纯复形，并开发了一种基于变换器的AR模型，用于在细节层次的顺序上近似网格简化的逆过程，最初从单个点开始构建网格，然后通过局部重新网格化逐步添加几何细节，其中拓扑结构并非预先定义且可变。我们的实验表明，这种新颖的逐步网格生成方法不仅可以通过提前停止自回归过程来直观地控制生成质量和时间消耗，还能够支持网格细化和编辑等应用。

英摘: Despite remarkable progress in driving world models, their potential for autonomous systems remains largely untapped: the world models are mostly learned for world simulation and decoupled from trajectory planning. 中摘: 尽管在驱动世界模型方面取得了显著进展，但其在自主系统中的潜力尚未充分开发：这些世界模型主要用于世界模拟，并与轨迹规划脱钩。虽然近期努力旨在在一个统一框架中结合世界建模和规划，但通过提出的无动作未来状态预测方案，世界建模如何促进规划仍需进一步探索。在这项工作中，我们引入了一种新的驾驶范式，称为策略世界模型（PWM），不仅在统一架构中整合了世界建模和轨迹规划，还能够通过所提出的学习世界知识的无动作未来状态预测方案来提高规划性能。通过协作的状态-动作预测，PWM 可以模拟人类的预见性感知能力，从而提供更可靠的规划表现。为了提高视频预测的效率，我们进一步引入了一种动态增强并行令牌生成机制，该机制配备了上下文引导的分词器和自适应动态焦点损失函数。尽管仅使用前视摄像头输入，我们的方法在依赖多视角和多模态输入的方法中达到了或超过了最先进的水平。相关代码和模型权重将在 https://github.com/6550Zhao/Policy-World-Model 释放。

[No.201] NEP: Autoregressive Image Editing via Next Editing Token Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: NEP：基于下一个编辑令牌预测的自回归图像编辑
类别: deep learning
作者: Huimin Wu;Xiaojian Ma;Haozhe Zhao;Yanpeng Zhao;Qing Li;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06044
英摘: Text-guided image editing involves modifying a source image based on a language instruction and, typically, requires changes to only small local regions. However, existing approaches generate the entire target image rather than selectively regenerate only the intended editing areas. This results in (1) unnecessary computational costs and (2) a bias toward reconstructing non-editing regions, which compromises the quality of the intended edits. To resolve these limitations, we propose to formulate image editing as Next Editing-token Prediction (NEP) based on autoregressive image generation, where only regions that need to be edited are regenerated, thus avoiding unintended modification to the non-editing areas. To enable any-region editing, we propose to pre-train an any-order autoregressive text-to-image (T2I) model. Once trained, it is capable of zero-shot image editing and can be easily adapted to NEP for image editing, which achieves a new state-of-the-art on widely used image editing benchmarks. Moreover, our model naturally supports test-time scaling (TTS) through iteratively refining its generation in a zero-shot manner. The project page is: https://nep-bigai.github.io/
中摘: 基于文本指导的图像编辑涉及根据语言指令修改源图像，并且通常只需要对小局部区域进行更改。然而，现有的方法生成整个目标图像，而不是仅选择性地再生需要编辑的区域。这导致了（1）不必要的计算成本和（2）偏向于重建非编辑区域，从而影响了预期编辑的质量。为了解决这些限制，我们提出将图像编辑形式化为自回归图像生成中的“下一个编辑标记预测”(Next Editing-token Prediction, NEP)，仅再生需要编辑的区域，从而避免对非编辑区域进行意外修改。为了实现任意区域编辑，我们建议预先训练一个任意顺序的自回归文本到图像（T2I）模型。一旦训练完成，该模型能够实现零样本图像编辑，并且可以轻松适应NEP以实现图像编辑，从而在广泛使用的图像编辑基准测试中达到新的最佳性能。此外，我们的模型自然支持测试时缩放（Test-Time Scaling, TTS），通过迭代零样本方式精炼其生成过程。项目页面：https://nep-bigai.github.io/

[No.202] Class conditional conformal prediction for multiple inputs by p-value aggregation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于p值聚合的多输入条件自适应预测
类别: general machine learning
作者: Jean-Baptiste Fermanian;Mohamed Hebiri;Joseph Salmon;
机构: University of Montpelier;Université Gustave Eiffel;INRIA;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=d4eAHgIttM
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07150
英摘: Conformal prediction methods are statistical tools designed to quantify uncertainty and generate predictive sets with guaranteed coverage probabilities. This work introduces an innovative refinement to these methods for classification tasks, specifically tailored for scenarios where multiple observations (multi-inputs) of a single instance are available at prediction time. Our approach is particularly motivated by applications in citizen science, where multiple images of the same plant or animal are captured by individuals. Our method integrates the information from each observation into conformal prediction, enabling a reduction in the size of the predicted label set while preserving the required class-conditional coverage guarantee. The approach is based on the aggregation of conformal p-values computed from each observation of a multi-input. By exploiting the exact distribution of these p-values, we propose a general aggregation framework using an abstract scoring function, encompassing many classical statistical tools. Knowledge of this distribution also enables refined versions of standard strategies, such as majority voting. We evaluate our method on simulated and real data, with a particular focus on Pl@ntNet, a prominent citizen science platform that facilitates the collection and identification of plant species through user-submitted images.
中摘: 齐纳预测方法是统计工具，用于量化不确定性并生成具有保证覆盖概率的预测集。本研究为分类任务引入了一种创新改进方法，特别适用于在预测时间可获得单个实例多个观测（多输入）的情况。我们的方法尤其受到公民科学应用的启发，在这些应用中，同一植物或动物的多张图像由个人捕获。我们的方式将每个观测的信息整合到齐纳预测中，能够在保持所需的类条件覆盖保证的同时减少预测标签集的大小。该方法基于从多输入的每个观测计算的齐纳p值的聚合。通过利用这些p值的确切分布，我们提出了一种通用的聚合框架，使用抽象评分函数涵盖许多经典的统计工具。了解这种分布还允许对标准策略进行更精细的版本，例如多数投票。我们在模拟数据和真实数据上评估了我们的方法，特别是针对Pl@ntNet这一重要的公民科学平台，该平台通过用户提交的图像促进植物物种的收集和识别。

[No.204] TAMI: Taming Heterogeneity in Temporal Interactions for Temporal Graph Link Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: TAMI: 面向时间图链接预测的时间交互异质性驯化
类别: general machine learning
作者: Zhongyi Yu;Jianqiu Wu;Zhenghao Wu;Shuhan Zhong;Weifeng Su;Chul-Ho Lee;Weipeng Zhuo;
机构: Beijing Normal University;HKBU-Zhuhai;University College Dublin;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;United International College;Texas State University;Beijing Normal-Hong Kong Baptist University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=zjQLUiguRz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23577
英摘: Temporal graph link prediction aims to predict future interactions between nodes in a graph based on their historical interactions, which are encoded in node embeddings. We observe that heterogeneity naturally appears in temporal interactions, e.g., a few node pairs can make most interaction events, and interaction events happen at varying intervals. This leads to the problems of ineffective temporal information encoding and forgetting of past interactions for a pair of nodes that interact intermittently for their link prediction. Existing methods, however, do not consider such heterogeneity in their learning process, and thus their learned temporal node embeddings are less effective, especially when predicting the links for infrequently interacting node pairs. To cope with the heterogeneity, we propose a novel framework called TAMI, which contains two effective components, namely log time encoding function (LTE) and link history aggregation (LHA). LTE better encodes the temporal information through transforming interaction intervals into more balanced ones, and LHA prevents the historical interactions for each target node pair from being forgotten. State-of-the-art temporal graph neural networks can be seamlessly and readily integrated into TAMI to improve their effectiveness. Experiment results on 13 classic datasets and three newest temporal graph benchmark (TGB) datasets show that TAMI consistently improves the link prediction performance of the underlying models in both transductive and inductive settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/Alleinx/TAMI_temporal_graph.
中摘: 时间图链接预测旨在基于节点的历史交互来预测图中节点未来可能发生的交互，这些历史交互通过节点嵌入进行编码。我们观察到时间交互自然表现出异质性，例如，少数节点对可以主导大多数交互事件，并且交互事件发生在不同的时间间隔上。这导致了对于间歇性交互的节点对来说，在链接预测时无法有效编码时间信息以及忘记过去交互的问题。现有的方法在其学习过程中并未考虑这种异质性，因此它们学到的时间节点嵌入效果较差，特别是在预测不频繁交互节点对的链接时表现尤为明显。为了应对这种异质性，我们提出了一种名为TAMI的新框架，该框架包含两个有效的组件，即日志时间编码函数（LTE）和链接历史聚合（LHA）。LTE通过将交互间隔转换为更平衡的形式，更好地编码时间信息，而LHA则防止目标节点对的历史交互被遗忘。最新的时间图神经网络状态可以在TAMI框架中无缝集成，以提高其有效性。在13个经典数据集和三个最新的时间图基准（TGB）数据集上的实验结果显示，TAMI在归纳设置和传递设置下都能一致地提升底层模型的链接预测性能。我们的代码可在https://github.com/Alleinx/TAMI_temporal_graph获取。

[No.206] L-MTP: Leap Multi-Token Prediction Beyond Adjacent Context for Large Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: L-MTP：超越相邻上下文的大语言模型多令牌预测
类别: deep learning
作者: Xiaohao Liu;Xiaobo Xia;Weixiang Zhao;Manyi Zhang;Xianzhi Yu;Xiu Su;Shuo Yang;See-Kiong Ng;Tat-Seng Chua;
机构: ;National University of Singapore;Harbin Institute of Technology;Huawei;Central South University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=0VDmWjW456
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17505
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved notable progress. Despite their success, next-token prediction (NTP), the dominant method for LLM training and inference, is constrained in both contextual coverage and inference efficiency due to its inherently sequential process. To overcome these challenges, we propose leap multi-token prediction~(L-MTP), an innovative token prediction method that extends the capabilities of multi-token prediction (MTP) by introducing a leap-based mechanism. Unlike conventional MTP, which generates multiple tokens at adjacent positions, L-MTP strategically skips over intermediate tokens, predicting non-sequential ones in a single forward pass. This structured leap not only enhances the model's ability to capture long-range dependencies but also enables a decoding strategy specially optimized for non-sequential leap token generation, effectively accelerating inference. We theoretically demonstrate the benefit of L-MTP in improving inference efficiency. Experiments across diverse benchmarks validate its merit in boosting both LLM performance and inference speed. The source code is available at https://github.com/Xiaohao-Liu/L-MTP.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）已经取得了显著的进步。尽管它们取得了成功，但主导的训练和推理方法——下一个标记预测（NTP），由于其本质上是顺序处理的过程，所以在上下文覆盖范围和推理效率方面仍然受到限制。为了克服这些挑战，我们提出了跳跃多标记预测（L-MTP）这一创新性的标记预测方法，该方法通过引入基于跳跃的机制来扩展多标记预测（MTP）的能力。与传统的MTP不同，后者在相邻位置生成多个标记，L-MTP战略性地跳过中间标记，在一次前向传递中预测非顺序的标记。这种结构化的跳跃不仅增强了模型捕捉长距离依赖关系的能力，还允许特别优化用于非顺序跳跃标记生成的解码策略，从而有效加速推理过程。我们从理论上证明了L-MTP在提高推理效率方面的优势。跨多种基准的实验验证了它在提升LLM性能和推理速度方面的 merits。源代码可在 https://github.com/Xiaohao-Liu/L-MTP 获取。

[No.208] How Benchmark Prediction from Fewer Data Misses the Mark
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 如何基于较少的数据进行基准预测往往会偏离目标
类别: evaluation
作者: Guanhua Zhang;Florian E. Dorner;Moritz Hardt;
机构: Max Planck Institute for Intelligent Systems;ETH Zurich;Max-Planck-Institute for Intelligent Systems;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=o3bftqj17e
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07673
英摘: Large language model (LLM) evaluation is increasingly costly, prompting interest in methods that speed up evaluation by shrinking benchmark datasets. Benchmark prediction (also called efficient LLM evaluation) aims to select a small subset of evaluation points and predict overall benchmark performance from that subset. In this paper, we systematically assess the strengths and limitations of 11 benchmark prediction methods across 19 diverse benchmarks. First, we identify a highly competitive baseline: Take a random sample and fit a regression model on the sample to predict missing entries. Outperforming most existing methods, this baseline challenges the assumption that careful subset selection is necessary for benchmark prediction. Second, we discover that all existing methods crucially depend on model similarity. They work best when interpolating scores among similar models. The effectiveness of benchmark prediction sharply declines when new models have higher accuracy than previously seen models. In this setting of extrapolation, none of the previous methods consistently beat a simple average over random samples. To improve over the sample average, we introduce a new method inspired by augmented inverse propensity weighting. This method consistently outperforms the random sample average even for extrapolation. However, its performance still relies on model similarity and the gains are modest in general. This shows that benchmark prediction fails just when it is most needed: at the evaluation frontier, where the goal is to evaluate new models of unknown capabilities.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）评估的成本越来越高，促使人们关注能够通过缩小基准数据集来加速评估的方法。基准预测（也称为高效的LLM评估）旨在选择一小部分评估点，并从这些点预测整体基准性能。在本文中，我们系统地评估了11种基准预测方法在19个不同基准上的优缺点。首先，我们确定了一个极具竞争力的基础方法：随机抽取样本并在样本上拟合回归模型以预测缺失的条目。这个基础方法超越了大多数现有方法，挑战了基准预测中细致的选择子集是必要的这一假设。其次，我们发现所有现有的方法都极其依赖于模型的相似性。当在相似模型之间进行插值评分时，它们表现最佳。然而，当新模型的准确性高于之前见过的模型时，基准预测的有效性急剧下降。在这种外推情境下，之前的任何方法都无法一致地超过随机样本平均值。为了超越样本平均值，我们引入了一种新的方法，该方法灵感来源于增强的逆倾向加权。即使在外推情况下，这种方法也始终优于随机样本平均值。然而，其性能仍然依赖于模型的相似性，总体改进有限。这表明基准预测恰好在最需要的时候失败：即在评估未知能力的新模型时。

[No.209] AMBER: Adaptive Mesh Generation by Iterative Mesh Resolution Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: AMBER：自适应网格生成通过迭代网格分辨率预测
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Niklas Freymuth;Tobias Würth;Nicolas Schreiber;Balázs Gyenes;Andreas Boltres;Johannes Mitsch;Aleksandar Taranovic;Tai Hoang;Philipp Dahlinger;Philipp Becker;Luise Kärger;Gerhard Neumann;

英摘: The cost and accuracy of simulating complex physical systems using the Finite Element Method (FEM) scales with the resolution of the underlying mesh. Adaptive meshes improve computational efficiency by refining resolution in critical regions, but typically require task-specific heuristics or cumbersome manual design by a human expert. We propose Adaptive Meshing By Expert Reconstruction (AMBER), a supervised learning approach to mesh adaptation. Starting from a coarse mesh, AMBER iteratively predicts the sizing field, i.e., a function mapping from the geometry to the local element size of the target mesh, and uses this prediction to produce a new intermediate mesh using an out-of-the-box mesh generator. This process is enabled through a hierarchical graph neural network, and relies on data augmentation by automatically projecting expert labels onto AMBER-generated data during training. We evaluate AMBER on 2D and 3D datasets, including classical physics problems, mechanical components, and real-world industrial designs with human expert meshes. AMBER generalizes to unseen geometries and consistently outperforms multiple recent baselines, including ones using Graph and Convolutional Neural Networks, and Reinforcement Learning-based approaches.
中摘: 使用有限元方法（FEM）模拟复杂物理系统时，成本和准确性与底层网格的分辨率相关。自适应网格可以通过在关键区域细化分辨率来提高计算效率，但通常需要特定任务的启发式方法或由人类专家进行繁琐的手动设计。我们提出了一种名为专家重建自适应网格化（AMBER）的方法，这是一种监督学习方法，用于网格自适应。从一个粗略的网格开始，AMBER 通过迭代预测目标网格的大小场，即一个从几何形状映射到局部元素大小的函数，并利用这一预测生成一个新的中间网格，使用现成的网格生成器。这一过程通过层次化的图神经网络实现，并依赖于训练期间自动将专家标签投影到 AMBER 生成的数据上以进行数据增强。我们在 2D 和 3D 数据集上评估了 AMBER，包括经典物理学问题、机械组件以及具有人类专家网格的真实工业设计。AMBER 能够泛化到未见过的几何形状，并且在多个最新的基线方法中表现优异，包括那些使用图神经网络和卷积神经网络的方法，以及基于强化学习的方法。

[No.210] Reaction Prediction via Interaction Modeling of Symmetric Difference Shingle Sets
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于对称差分文集集合作用模型的反应预测
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Runhan Shi;Letian Chen;Gufeng Yu;Yang Yang;

类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Ezi8uXAanu
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06356
英摘: Chemical reaction prediction remains a fundamental challenge in organic chemistry, where existing machine learning models face two critical limitations: sensitivity to input permutations (molecule/atom orderings) and inadequate modeling of substructural interactions governing reactivity. These shortcomings lead to inconsistent predictions and poor generalization to real-world scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose ReaDISH, a novel reaction prediction model that learns permutation-invariant representations while incorporating interaction-aware features. It introduces two innovations: (1) symmetric difference shingle encoding, which extends the differential reaction fingerprint (DRFP) by representing shingles as continuous high-dimensional embeddings, capturing structural changes while eliminating order sensitivity; and (2) geometry-structure interaction attention, a mechanism that models intra- and inter-molecular interactions at the shingle level. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReaDISH improves reaction prediction performance across diverse benchmarks. It shows enhanced robustness with an average improvement of 8.76% on R$^2$ under permutation perturbations.
中摘: 化学反应预测仍然是有机化学中的一个基本挑战，现有的机器学习模型面临两个关键限制：对输入排列（分子/原子顺序）的敏感性和对调控反应性的子结构相互作用建模不足。这些缺陷导致预测结果不一致，并且难以泛化到实际场景中。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种名为ReaDISH的新颖反应预测模型，该模型学习不变排列的表示方式并结合了交互感知特征。它引入了两项创新：（1）对称差分楔形编码，这种编码扩展了差异反应指纹（DRFP），将楔形表示为连续的高维嵌入，捕获结构变化同时消除顺序敏感性；（2）几何-结构交互注意力机制，这是一种在楔形级别建模分子内和分子间相互作用的方法。广泛的实验表明，ReaDISH在多种基准测试中提高了反应预测性能，并且在排列扰动下显示出增强的鲁棒性，平均改进率为8.76%的R²值。

[No.211] Non-exchangeable Conformal Prediction with Optimal Transport: Tackling Distribution Shift with Unlabeled Data
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 非交换齐性预测与最优传输：利用未标记数据应对分布偏移
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Alvaro Correia;Christos Louizos;
机构: 
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=GfyvbLYKMW
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10425
英摘: Conformal prediction is a distribution-free uncertainty quantification method that has gained popularity in the machine learning community due to its finite-sample guarantees and ease of use. Its most common variant, dubbed split conformal prediction, is also computationally efficient as it boils down to collecting statistics of the model predictions on some calibration data not yet seen by the model. Nonetheless, these guarantees only hold if the calibration and test data are exchangeable, a condition that is difficult to verify and often violated in practice due to so-called distribution shifts. The literature is rife with methods to mitigate the loss in coverage in this non-exchangeable setting, but these methods require some prior information on the type of distribution shift to be expected at test time. In this work, we study this problem via a new perspective, through the lens of optimal transport, and show that it is possible to estimate the loss in coverage and mitigate arbitrary distribution shifts, offering a principled and broadly applicable solution.
中摘: 自适应预测是一种分布无关的不确定性量化方法，在机器学习社区中因其有限样本保证和使用便捷性而受到欢迎。其最常见的变体称为拆分自适应预测，这种方法在计算效率方面也很高，因为它只需要收集模型在未见过的校准数据上的预测统计信息。然而，这些保证仅在校准数据和测试数据可互换时成立，而在实践中由于所谓的分布偏移现象，这一条件往往难以验证并被违反。文献中提出了许多方法来缓解这种不可互换设置下的覆盖损失，但这些方法通常需要对预期的测试时间分布偏移类型有一定的先验信息。在这项工作中，我们通过最优传输的新视角研究了这一问题，并展示了如何估计覆盖损失并缓解任意的分布偏移，提供了一个原理上合理且广泛适用的解决方案。

中摘: 现代芯片设计复杂，直接从Verilog代码（一种广泛用于硬件设计的编程语言）预测关键设计质量指标如时序和布线拥塞的需求至关重要。尤其是在早期阶段预测导致时序违规或后续布线拥塞的个别代码行方面，这一需求尤为重要且复杂。以往的研究尝试将Verilog转换为中间图表示，并结合其他特征使用大规模语言模型（LLM）嵌入来预测模块级别的质量，但没有考虑代码行级别的质量预测。我们提出了VeriLoC，这是首个能够直接从Verilog代码在代码行和模块级别预测设计质量的方法。为此，VeriLoC 利用了最新的Verilog代码生成LLM来提取局部代码行级和模块级嵌入，并通过这些嵌入的拼接训练下游分类器/回归器。VeriLoC 在代码行级拥塞和时序预测上达到了0.86-0.95的高F1分数，并将当前最佳方法（SOTA）的平均百分比误差从14%-18%降低到了仅4%。我们认为，VeriLoC 的嵌入和我们工作中获得的见解对于复杂硬件设计的其他预测性和优化任务也将具有价值。

英摘: We propose a unified framework for adaptive routing in multitask, multimodal prediction settings where data heterogeneity and task interactions vary across samples. Our model defines multiple modality paths, including raw and fused representations of text and numeric features and learns to route each input through the most informative expert combination.
