[No.215] Failure Prediction at Runtime for Generative Robot Policies
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 运行时生成型机器人策略的失败预测
类别: reinforcement learning
作者: Ralf Römer;Adrian Kobras;Luca Worbis;Angela P. Schoellig;
机构: Technische Universität München;Technical University of Munich;University of Toronto;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=BXJWKpEfro
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09459
英摘: Imitation learning (IL) with generative models, such as diffusion and flow matching, has enabled robots to perform complex, long-horizon tasks. However, distribution shifts from unseen environments or compounding action errors can still cause unpredictable and unsafe behavior, leading to task failure. Early failure prediction during runtime is therefore essential for deploying robots in human-centered and safety-critical environments. We propose FIPER, a general framework for Failure Prediction at Runtime for generative IL policies that does not require failure data. FIPER identifies two key indicators of impending failure: (i) out-of-distribution (OOD) observations detected via random network distillation in the policy's embedding space, and (ii) high uncertainty in generated actions measured by a novel action-chunk entropy score. Both failure prediction scores are calibrated using a small set of successful rollouts via conformal prediction. A failure alarm is triggered when both indicators, aggregated over short time windows, exceed their thresholds. We evaluate FIPER across five simulation and real-world environments involving diverse failure modes. Our results demonstrate that FIPER better distinguishes actual failures from benign OOD situations and predicts failures more accurately and earlier than existing methods. We thus consider this work an important step towards more interpretable and safer generative robot policies. Code, data and videos are available at https://tum-lsy.github.io/fiper_website.
中摘: 基于生成模型（如扩散和流匹配）的模仿学习（IL）使机器人能够执行复杂的、长期的任务。然而，从未见过的环境中的分布变化或累积的动作错误仍可能导致不可预测且不安全的行为，从而导致任务失败。因此，在人类中心和安全关键环境中部署机器人时，运行时早期故障预测至关重要。我们提出了一种名为FIPER的一般框架，用于为生成型IL策略进行运行时故障预测，该框架不需要故障数据。FIPER识别了两种即将发生故障的关键指标：(i) 通过在策略嵌入空间中使用随机网络蒸馏检测到的离群观察值；(ii) 由新颖的动作片段熵分数测量的生成动作的高度不确定性。这两种故障预测评分均通过校准预测使用一小部分成功的滚动展开来调整。当短时间窗口内两个指标超过其阈值时，会触发故障警报。我们在涉及多种故障模式的五个仿真和真实世界环境中评估了FIPER。我们的结果表明，FIPER更好地区分了实际故障与良性离群情况，并比现有方法更早、更准确地预测了故障。因此，我们认为这项工作是迈向更具可解释性和安全性生成型机器人策略的重要一步。更多代码、数据和视频请参阅https://tum-lsy.github.io/fiper_website。

[No.217] Understanding Fairness and Prediction Error through Subspace Decomposition and Influence Analysis

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23935
英摘: Machine learning models have achieved widespread success but often inherit and amplify historical biases, resulting in unfair outcomes. Traditional fairness methods typically impose constraints at the prediction level, without addressing underlying biases in data representations. In this work, we propose a principled framework that adjusts data representations to balance predictive utility and fairness. Using sufficient dimension reduction, we decompose the feature space into target-relevant, sensitive, and shared components, and control the fairness-utility trade-off by selectively removing sensitive information. We provide a theoretical analysis of how prediction error and fairness gaps evolve as shared subspaces are added, and employ influence functions to quantify their effects on the asymptotic behavior of parameter estimates. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets validate our theoretical insights and show that the proposed method effectively improves fairness while preserving predictive performance.

[No.219] Prediction-Powered Semi-Supervised Learning with Online Power Tuning
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于预测的动力调谐半监督学习
类别: general machine learning
作者: Noa Shoham;Ron Dorfman;Shalev Shaer;Kfir Yehuda Levy;Yaniv Romano;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=oYIXUwmNig
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22586
英摘: Prediction-Powered Inference (PPI) is a recently proposed statistical inference technique for parameter estimation that leverages pseudo-labels on both labeled and unlabeled data to construct an unbiased, low-variance estimator. In this work, we extend its core idea to semi-supervised learning (SSL) for model training, introducing a novel unbiased gradient estimator. This extension addresses a key challenge in SSL: while unlabeled data can improve model performance, its benefit heavily depends on the quality of pseudo-labels. Inaccurate pseudo-labels can introduce bias, leading to suboptimal models.To balance the contributions of labeled and pseudo-labeled data, we utilize an interpolation parameter and tune it on the fly, alongside the model parameters, using a one-dimensional online learning algorithm. We verify the practical advantage of our approach through experiments on both synthetic and real datasets, demonstrating improved performance over classic SSL baselines and PPI methods that tune the interpolation parameter offline.
中摘: 预测驱动的推理（PPI）是一种最近提出的统计推理技术，用于参数估计，它利用标记数据和未标记数据上的伪标签来构建无偏且低方差的估计器。在本文中，我们将其核心思想扩展到半监督学习（SSL），以用于模型训练，并引入了一个新的无偏梯度估计器。这一扩展解决了半监督学习中的一个关键挑战：虽然未标记数据可以提高模型性能，但其优势很大程度上取决于伪标签的质量。不准确的伪标签会引入偏差，导致模型性能不佳。为了平衡标记数据和伪标签数据的贡献，我们利用一个插值参数，并在线调整该参数以及模型参数，使用一维在线学习算法。通过在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验验证了我们方法的实际优势，展示了相对于经典的半监督学习基线方法和离线调整插值参数的PPI方法，具有更好的性能。

[No.220] ELECTRA: A Cartesian Network for 3D Charge Density Prediction with Floating Orbitals
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: ELECTRA：一种用于预测浮动轨道三维电荷密度的笛卡尔网络
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Jonas Elsborg;Luca Thiede;Alan Aspuru-Guzik;Tejs Vegge;Arghya Bhowmik;
机构: Technical University of Denmark;Univerity of Toronto;Vector Institute;University of Toronto;
类型: Spotlight
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Rz9ISbLxf0
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08305
英摘: We present the Electronic Tensor Reconstruction Algorithm (ELECTRA) - an equivariant model for predicting electronic charge densities using floating orbitals. Floating orbitals are a long-standing concept in the quantum chemistry community that promises more compact and accurate representations by placing orbitals freely in space, as opposed to centering all orbitals at the position of atoms. Finding the ideal placement of these orbitals requires extensive domain knowledge, though, which thus far has prevented widespread adoption. We solve this in a data-driven manner by training a Cartesian tensor network to predict the orbital positions along with orbital coefficients. This is made possible through a symmetry-breaking mechanism that is used to learn position displacements with lower symmetry than the input molecule while preserving the rotation equivariance of the charge density itself. Inspired by recent successes of Gaussian Splatting in representing densities in space, we are using Gaussian orbitals and predicting their weights and covariance matrices. Our method achieves a state-of-the-art balance between computational efficiency and predictive accuracy on established benchmarks. Furthermore, ELECTRA is able to lower the compute time required to arrive at converged DFT solutions - initializing calculations using our predicted densities yields an average 50.72 % reduction in self-consistent field (SCF) iterations on unseen molecules.
中摘: 我们提出了电子张量重构算法（ELECTRA）——一种使用浮动轨道预测电子密度的对称模型。浮动轨道是量子化学领域的一个长期概念，通过在空间中自由放置轨道而非将所有轨道置于原子位置上，它承诺提供更为紧凑和准确的表示。然而，找到这些轨道的理想位置需要大量的领域专业知识，这至今阻碍了其广泛应用。我们通过数据驱动的方式解决了这一问题，训练了一个笛卡尔张量网络来同时预测轨道的位置和轨道系数。这得益于一个对称性破坏机制，该机制允许学习输入分子较低对称性的位置位移，同时保持电子密度本身的旋转不变性。受最近在空间表示密度方面使用高斯点积取得成功的影响，我们采用了高斯轨道，并预测它们的权重和协方差矩阵。我们的方法在现有基准测试中实现了计算效率和预测准确性之间的最佳平衡。此外，ELECTRA 能够减少达到收敛密度泛函理论（DFT）解所需的时间——使用我们预测的密度初始化计算，在未见过的分子上平均减少了50.72%的自洽场（SCF）迭代次数。

英摘: While large transformer models excel in predictive performance, their lack of interpretability restricts their usefulness in high-stakes domains. [No.222] Conditional Forecasts and Proper Scoring Rules for Reliable and Accurate Performative Predictions
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 条件预测与适当的评分规则以确保预测的可靠性和准确性
类别: theory
作者: Philip Boeken;Onno Zoeter;Joris M. Mooij;
机构: ;Booking AI Research;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=0KAdFd5zku
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21335
英摘: Performative predictions are forecasts which influence the outcomes they aim to predict, undermining the existence of correct forecasts and standard methods of elicitation and estimation. We show that conditioning forecasts on covariates that separate them from the outcome renders the target distribution forecast-invariant, guaranteeing well-posedness of the forecasting problem. However, even under this condition, classical proper scoring rules fail to elicit correct forecasts. We prove a general impossibility result and identify two solutions: (i) in decision-theoretic settings, elicitation of correct and incentive-compatible forecasts is possible if forecasts are separating; (ii) scoring with unbiased estimates of the divergence between the forecast and the induced distribution of the target variable yields correct forecasts. Applying these insights to parameter estimation, conditional forecasts and proper scoring rules enable performatively stable estimation of performatively correct parameters, resolving the issues raised by Perdomo et al. (2020). Our results expose fundamental limits of classical forecast evaluation and offer new tools for reliable and accurate forecasting in performative settings.
中摘: 表演性预测是指那些能够影响其预测结果的预估，从而破坏了正确预估和标准预估方法的存在性。我们证明，当条件预估依赖于能够将预估与结果分开的协变量时，目标分布的预估不变，确保了预估问题的明确性。然而，即使在这一条件下，经典的适当评分规则也无法引出正确的预估。我们证明了一个普遍的不可能性结果，并提出了两种解决方案：(i) 在决策理论框架下，如果预估能够分离，则可以引出正确且激励相容的预估；(ii) 使用无偏估计量来计算预测与目标变量诱导分布之间的差异得分，可以引出正确的预估。将这些见解应用于参数估计、条件预估和适当评分规则，能够实现表演性稳定的、表演性正确的参数估计，解决了Perdomo等人（2020）提出的诸多问题。我们的研究揭示了经典预估评估的基本局限性，并为表演性环境中的可靠准确预估提供了新的工具。

[No.224] Online Prediction with Limited Selectivity

英摘: Selective prediction [Dru13, QV19] models the scenario where a forecaster freely decides on the prediction window that their forecast spans. Many data statistics can be predicted to a non-trivial error rate without any distributional assumptions or expert advice, yet these results rely on that the forecaster may predict at any time. We introduce a model of Prediction with Limited Selectivity (PLS) where the forecaster can start the prediction only on a subset of the time horizon. [No.225] Unsupervised Learning for Optimal Transport plan prediction between unbalanced graphs
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 无监督学习在不平衡图之间的最优运输计划预测
类别: deep learning
作者: Sonia Mazelet;Rémi Flamary;Bertrand Thirion;
机构: Ecole Polytechnique;INRIA;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Jd8UcXJDCz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12025
英摘: Optimal transport between graphs, based on Gromov-Wasserstein and other extensions, is a powerful tool for comparing and aligning graph structures. However, solving the associated non-convex optimization problems is computationally expensive, which limits the scalability of these methods to large graphs. In this work, we present Unbalanced Learning of Optimal Transport (ULOT), a deep learning method that predicts optimal transport plans between two graphs. Our method is trained by minimizing the fused unbalanced Gromov-Wasserstein (FUGW) loss. We propose a novel neural architecture with cross-attention that is conditioned on the FUGW tradeoff hyperparameters. We evaluate ULOT on synthetic stochastic block model (SBM) graphs and on real cortical surface data obtained from fMRI. ULOT predicts transport plans with competitive loss up to two orders of magnitude faster than classical solvers. Furthermore, the predicted plan can be used as a warm start for classical solvers to accelerate their convergence. Finally, the predicted transport plan is fully differentiable with respect to the graph inputs and FUGW hyperparameters, enabling the optimization of functionals of the ULOT plan.
中摘: 基于Gromov-Wasserstein和其他扩展的图之间的最优传输，是一种强大的工具，用于比较和对齐图结构。然而，解决相关的非凸优化问题在计算上非常昂贵，这限制了这些方法在大型图上的可扩展性。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为Unbalanced Learning of Optimal Transport（ULOT）的深度学习方法，该方法可以预测两个图之间的最优传输计划。我们的方法通过最小化融合不平衡Gromov-Wasserstein（FUGW）损失进行训练。我们提出了一种新颖的神经网络架构，该架构使用交叉注意力机制，并根据FUGW权衡超参数进行条件训练。我们在合成的随机块模型（SBM）图以及来自fMRI的真实皮层表面数据上评估了ULOT。ULOT能够在比经典求解器快两个数量级的时间内预测出具有竞争力损失的传输计划。此外，预测的计划可以作为经典求解器的预热启动，以加速其收敛。最后，预测的传输计划完全对图输入和FUGW超参数可微，从而能够优化ULOT计划的功能。

[No.226] Zero-shot protein stability prediction by inverse folding models: a free energy interpretation
来源: NeurIPS 2025

[No.228] One Sample is Enough to Make Conformal Prediction Robust
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 一个样本足以使同质预测变得 robust
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Soroush H. Zargarbashi;Mohammad Sadegh Akhondzadeh;Aleksandar Bojchevski;
机构: Saarland University;University of Cologne;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=h5NsMrUK4g
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16553
英摘: For any black-box model, conformal prediction (CP) returns prediction sets guaranteed to include the true label with high adjustable probability. Robust CP (RCP) extends the guarantee to the worst case noise up to a pre-defined magnitude. For RCP, a well-established approach is to use randomized smoothing since it is applicable to any black-box model and provides smaller sets compared to deterministic methods. However, smoothing-based robustness requires many model forward passes per each input which is computationally expensive. We show that conformal prediction attains some robustness even with a single forward pass on a randomly perturbed input. Using any binary certificate we propose a single sample robust CP (RCP1). Our approach returns robust sets with smaller average set size compared to SOTA methods which use many (e.g. 100) passes per input. Our key insight is to certify the conformal procedure itself rather than individual conformity scores. Our approach is agnostic to the task (classification and regression). We further extend our approach to smoothing-based robust conformal risk control.
中摘: 对于任何黑盒模型，校准预测（CP）会返回一个预测集，该预测集能够以高可调节的概率包含真实标签。鲁棒校准预测（RCP）将这一保证扩展到预定义幅度内的最坏情况噪声。对于RCP，一种成熟的方法是使用随机化平滑，因为它适用于任何黑盒模型，并且与确定性方法相比能提供更小的预测集。然而，基于平滑的鲁棒性需要对每个输入进行多次模型前向传递，这在计算上是昂贵的。我们展示了即使是对随机扰动输入进行一次前向传递，校准预测也能获得一定程度的鲁棒性。利用任何二元证书，我们提出了一种单样本鲁棒校准预测（RCP1）。我们的方法在平均集合大小方面比使用多次传递（例如100次）的当前最佳方法（SOTA）返回更小的鲁棒集合。我们的关键洞察是验证校准预测过程本身，而不是单独的校准分数。我们的方法对任务是无偏的（既适用于分类也适用于回归）。我们进一步将这种方法扩展到基于平滑的鲁棒校准风险控制。

[No.230] Bandit and Delayed Feedback in Online Structured Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 在线结构化预测中的强盗与延迟反馈
类别: theory
作者: Yuki Shibukawa;Taira Tsuchiya;Shinsaku Sakaue;Kenji Yamanishi;
机构: University of Tokyo;RIKEN;CyberAgent;Tokyo Institute of Technology;NTT Corporation;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=7DDmAHXWmi
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18709
英摘: Online structured prediction is a task of sequentially predicting outputs with complex structures based on inputs and past observations, encompassing online classification. Recent studies showed that in the full-information setting, we can achieve finite bounds on the \textit{surrogate regret}, \textit{i.e.,}~the extra target loss relative to the best possible surrogate loss. In practice, however, full-information feedback is often unrealistic as it requires immediate access to the whole structure of complex outputs. Motivated by this, we propose algorithms that work with less demanding feedback, \textit{bandit} and \textit{delayed} feedback. For bandit feedback, by using a standard inverse-weighted gradient estimator, we achieve a surrogate regret bound of $O(\sqrt{KT})$ for the time horizon $T$ and the size of the output set $K$. However, $K$ can be extremely large when outputs are highly complex, resulting in an undesirable bound. To address this issue, we propose another algorithm that achieves a surrogate regret bound of $O(T^{2/3})$, which is independent of $K$. This is achieved with a carefully designed pseudo-inverse matrix estimator. Furthermore, we numerically compare the performance of these algorithms, as well as existing ones. Regarding delayed feedback, we provide algorithms and regret analyses that cover various scenarios, including full-information and bandit feedback, as well as fixed and variable delays.
中摘: 在线结构化预测是指基于输入和以往观察，顺序预测具有复杂结构的输出的任务，包括在线分类。最近的研究表明，在完整信息设置下，我们可以针对最佳可能替代损失实现有限的目标遗憾边界，即相对于最佳替代损失的额外目标损失。然而，在实践中，获取完整信息反馈往往是不现实的，因为它需要立即访问复杂输出的整个结构。鉴于此，我们提出了适用于更苛刻反馈条件的算法，即“多臂老虎机”（bandit）反馈和延迟反馈。对于多臂老虎机反馈，通过使用标准的逆加权梯度估计器，我们实现了时间范围$T$和输出集大小$K$的时间区间$T$上的替代遗憾边界为$O(\sqrt{KT})$。但是，当输出极其复杂时，$K$可以非常大，导致一个不理想的边界。为了解决这个问题，我们提出另一种算法，实现了与$K$无关的替代遗憾边界$O(T^{2/3})$，这是通过精心设计的伪逆矩阵估计器实现的。此外，我们还对这些算法以及现有的算法进行了数值性能比较。关于延迟反馈，我们提供了涵盖各种场景的算法和遗憾分析，包括完整信息反馈、多臂老虎机反馈以及固定延迟和可变延迟的情况。

[No.232] Backward Conformal Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 逆向保形预测
类别: theory
作者: Etienne Gauthier;Francis Bach;Michael I. Jordan;
机构: INRIA;Ecole Normale Superieure;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=23ichdd74N
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13732
英摘: We introduce $\textit{Backward Conformal Prediction}$, a method that guarantees conformal coverage while providing flexible control over the size of prediction sets. Unlike standard conformal prediction, which fixes the coverage level and allows the conformal set size to vary, our approach defines a rule that constrains how prediction set sizes behave based on the observed data, and adapts the coverage level accordingly. Our method builds on two key foundations: (i) recent results by Gauthier et al. [2025] on post-hoc validity using e-values, which ensure marginal coverage of the form $\mathbb{P}(Y_{\rm test} \in \hat C_n^{\tildeα}(X_{\rm test})) \ge 1 - \mathbb{E}[\tildeα]$ for any data-dependent miscoverage $\tildeα$, and (ii) a novel leave-one-out estimator $\hatα^{\rm LOO}$ of the marginal miscoverage $\mathbb{E}[\tildeα]$ based on the calibration set, ensuring that the theoretical guarantees remain computable in practice. This approach is particularly useful in applications where large prediction sets are impractical such as medical diagnosis. We provide theoretical results and empirical evidence supporting the validity of our method, demonstrating that it maintains computable coverage guarantees while ensuring interpretable, well-controlled prediction set sizes.
中摘: 我们介绍了**后验一致性校准预测（Backward Conformal Prediction）**，这是一种在保证一致性覆盖的同时提供灵活的预测集大小控制的方法。与标准的一致性预测不同，后者固定了覆盖水平并允许预测集大小变化，我们的方法基于观察数据定义了一条规则，以约束预测集大小的行为，并相应地调整覆盖水平。我们的方法建立在两个关键基础之上：(i) Gauthier等人[2025]关于使用e值进行事后一致性的最新成果，确保了边际覆盖的形式为$\mathbb{P}(Y_{\rm test} \in \hat C_n^{\tildeα}(X_{\rm test})) \ge 1 - \mathbb{E}[\tildeα]$，适用于任何数据相关的错误覆盖$\tildeα$；(ii) 基于校准集的一个新颖的留一.out估计量$\hatα^{\rm LOO}$，用于估计边际错误覆盖$\mathbb{E}[\tildeα]$，确保理论上的保证在实践中仍然是可计算的。这种方法特别适用于医疗诊断等应用场合，其中大型预测集不切实际。我们提供了理论结果和实证证据来支持该方法的有效性，证明它能够在保持可计算的覆盖保证的同时，确保预测集大小具有解释性和良好的可控性。

[No.235] Tight Lower Bounds and Improved Convergence in Performative Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 稳健的下界和性能预测中的改进收敛性
类别: optimization
作者: Pedram Khorsandi;Rushil Gupta;Mehrnaz Mofakhami;Simon Lacoste-Julien;Gauthier Gidel;
机构: Université de Montréal;New York University;Quebec Artificial Intelligence Institute;University of Montreal;Samsung;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=1ajMY3nUKg
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03671
英摘: Performative prediction is a framework accounting for the shift in the data distribution induced by the prediction of a model deployed in the real world. Ensuring rapid convergence to a stable solution where the data distribution remains the same after the model deployment is crucial, especially in evolving environments. This paper extends the Repeated Risk Minimization (RRM) framework by utilizing historical datasets from previous retraining snapshots, yielding a class of algorithms that we call Affine Risk Minimizers and enabling convergence to a performatively stable point for a broader class of problems. We introduce a new upper bound for methods that use only the final iteration of the dataset and prove for the first time the tightness of both this new bound and the previous existing bounds within the same regime. We also prove that utilizing historical datasets can surpass the lower bound for last iterate RRM, and empirically observe faster convergence to the stable point on various performative prediction benchmarks. We offer at the same time the first lower bound analysis for RRM within the class of Affine Risk Minimizers, quantifying the potential improvements in convergence speed that could be achieved with other variants in our framework.
中摘: 表演性预测是一种框架，用于解释模型在现实世界部署后引起的数据分布变化。确保模型部署后的数据分布保持稳定并迅速收敛到一个稳定的解决方案尤其重要，特别是在不断变化的环境中。本文通过利用之前重新训练快照的历史数据集，扩展了重复风险最小化（RRM）框架，从而产生了一类我们称为仿射风险最小化器的算法，并使RRM能够在更广泛的问题类别中达到表演性稳定点。我们引入了一个新的上界，仅适用于使用数据集最终迭代的方法，并首次证明了这个新上界和同一范畴内先前存在的上界的紧致性。我们还证明，利用历史数据集可以超越最后迭代RRM的下界，并在各种表演性预测基准上观察到更快地收敛到稳定点。同时，我们提供了仿射风险最小化器范畴内RRM的第一个下界分析，量化了在我们的框架中使用其他变体可能实现的收敛速度改进。

[No.236] Conformal Prediction in The Loop: A Feedback-Based Uncertainty Model for Trajectory Optimization
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 循环中的 conformal 预测：一种基于反馈的不确定性模型用于轨迹优化
类别: social and economic aspects of machine learning
作者: Han Wang;Chao Ning;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=lg51XnHt2H
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16376
英摘: Conformal Prediction (CP) is a powerful statistical machine learning tool to construct uncertainty sets with coverage guarantees, which has fueled its extensive adoption in generating prediction regions for decision-making tasks, e.g., Trajectory Optimization (TO) in uncertain environments. However, existing methods predominantly employ a sequential scheme, where decisions rely unidirectionally on the prediction regions, and consequently the information from decision-making fails to be fed back to instruct CP. In this paper, we propose a novel Feedback-Based CP (Fb-CP) framework for shrinking-horizon TO with a joint risk constraint over the entire mission time. Specifically, a CP-based posterior risk calculation method is developed by fully leveraging the realized trajectories to adjust the posterior allowable risk, which is then allocated to future times to update prediction regions. In this way, the information in the realized trajectories is continuously fed back to the CP, enabling attractive feedback-based adjustments of the prediction regions and a provable online improvement in trajectory performance. Furthermore, we theoretically prove that such adjustments consistently maintain the coverage guarantees of the prediction regions, thereby ensuring provable safety. Additionally, we develop a decision-focused iterative risk allocation algorithm with theoretical convergence analysis for allocating the posterior allowable risk which closely aligns with Fb-CP. Furthermore, we extend the proposed method to handle distribution shift. The effectiveness and superiority of the proposed method are demonstrated through benchmark experiments.
中摘: 自适应预测（Conformal Prediction，CP）是一种强大的统计机器学习工具，用于构建具有覆盖保证的不确定性集，这推动了其在生成决策任务中的预测区域方面的广泛应用，例如在不确定环境下的轨迹优化（Trajectory Optimization, TO）。然而，现有的方法主要采用顺序方案，其中决策单向依赖于预测区域，因此来自决策过程的信息未能反馈以指导CP。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的基于反馈的自适应预测（Feedback-Based Conformal Prediction，Fb-CP）框架，用于整个任务时间范围内的联合风险约束下的收缩视界轨迹优化。具体来说，通过充分利用实现的轨迹来调整后验允许的风险，并将该风险分配给未来的时间以更新预测区域。这样，实现轨迹中的信息可以持续反馈到CP，从而实现有吸引力的基于反馈的预测区域调整，并在线证明轨迹性能的改进。此外，我们理论上证明了这种调整一致地保持预测区域的覆盖保证，从而确保可证明的安全性。我们还开发了一种与Fb-CP紧密对齐的基于决策的迭代风险分配算法，并对其理论收敛性进行了分析。此外，我们将所提出的方法扩展以处理分布转移。通过基准实验展示了所提出方法的有效性和优越性。

[No.237] See through the Dark: Learning Illumination-affined Representations for Nighttime Occupancy Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 穿越黑暗：学习适应照明的夜间 occupancy 预测表示
类别: applications
作者: Wuyuan;Zhiqiang Yan;Yigong Zhang;Xiang Li;Jian Yang;
机构: Nanjing University of Science and Technology;National University of Singapore;Nankai University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=2Yk4GnB3DY
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20641
英摘: Occupancy prediction aims to estimate the 3D spatial distribution of occupied regions along with their corresponding semantic labels. Existing vision-based methods perform well on daytime benchmarks but struggle in nighttime scenarios due to limited visibility and challenging lighting conditions. To address these challenges, we propose LIAR, a novel framework that learns illumination-affined representations. LIAR first introduces Selective Low-light Image Enhancement (SLLIE), which leverages the illumination priors from daytime scenes to adaptively determine whether a nighttime image is genuinely dark or sufficiently well-lit, enabling more targeted global enhancement. Building on the illumination maps generated by SLLIE, LIAR further incorporates two illumination-aware components: 2D Illumination-guided Sampling (2D-IGS) and 3D Illumination-driven Projection (3D-IDP), to respectively tackle local underexposure and overexposure. Specifically, 2D-IGS modulates feature sampling positions according to illumination maps, assigning larger offsets to darker regions and smaller ones to brighter regions, thereby alleviating feature degradation in underexposed areas. Subsequently,3D-IDP enhances semantic understanding in overexposed regions by constructing illumination intensity fields and supplying refined residual queries to the BEV context refinement process. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic datasets demonstrate the superior performance of LIAR under challenging nighttime scenarios. The source code and pretrained models are available [here](https://github.com/yanzq95/LIAR).
中摘:  occupancy预测旨在估计占用区域的3D空间分布及其对应的语义标签。现有的基于视觉的方法在白天基准测试中表现良好，但在夜间场景中遇到困难，因为夜间能见度有限且光照条件具有挑战性。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种名为LIAR的新框架，该框架学习适应光照的表示。LIAR首先引入了选择性低光图像增强（SLLIE），利用白天场景中的光照先验信息来判断夜间图像是否真正黑暗或足够照明，从而实现更有针对性的全局增强。基于SLLIE生成的光照图，LIAR进一步整合了两个光照感知组件：2D光照引导采样（2D-IGS）和3D光照驱动投影（3D-IDP），分别解决局部曝光不足和过度曝光的问题。具体而言，2D-IGS根据光照图调节特征采样位置，在曝光不足的区域分配较大的偏移量，在明亮的区域分配较小的偏移量，从而缓解曝光不足区域的特征退化。随后，3D-IDP通过构建光照强度场并提供细化的残差查询来增强过度曝光区域的语义理解。在真实数据集和合成数据集上的广泛实验表明，LIAR在具有挑战性的夜间场景中表现出优越的性能。源代码和预训练模型可在此处获取：[这里](https://github.com/yanzq95/LIAR)。

[No.238] Listwise Preference Diffusion Optimization for User Behavior Trajectories Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 逐条偏好扩散优化在用户行为轨迹预测中的应用
类别: deep learning
作者: Hongtao Huang;Chengkai Huang;Junda Wu;Tong Yu;Julian McAuley;Lina Yao;
机构: University of New South Wales;University of California, San Diego;Adobe;CSIRO;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=x5KUOlYKQr
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00530
英摘: Forecasting multi-step user behavior trajectories requires reasoning over structured preferences across future actions, a challenge overlooked by traditional sequential recommendation. This problem is critical for applications such as personalized commerce and adaptive content delivery, where anticipating a user's complete action sequence enhances both satisfaction and business outcomes. We identify an essential limitation of existing paradigms: their inability to capture global, listwise dependencies among sequence items. To address this, we formulate User Behavior Trajectory Prediction (UBTP) as a new task setting that explicitly models long-term user preferences. We introduce Listwise Preference Diffusion Optimization (LPDO), a diffusion-based training framework that directly optimizes structured preferences over entire item sequences. LPDO incorporates a Plackett-Luce supervision signal and derives a tight variational lower bound aligned with listwise ranking likelihoods, enabling coherent preference generation across denoising steps and overcoming the independent-token assumption of prior diffusion methods. To rigorously evaluate multi-step prediction quality, we propose the task-specific metric Sequential Match (SeqMatch), which measures exact trajectory agreement, and adopt Perplexity (PPL), which assesses probabilistic fidelity. Extensive experiments on real-world user behavior benchmarks demonstrate that LPDO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, establishing a new benchmark for structured preference learning with diffusion models.
中摘: 多步用户行为轨迹预测需要对未来的动作结构化偏好进行推理，这是传统序列推荐所忽视的挑战。这个问题对于个性化商务和自适应内容交付等应用至关重要，因为在这些应用中，预测用户的完整动作序列能够同时提高用户满意度和商业成果。我们识别出现有范式的本质局限：它们无法捕捉序列项目之间的全局、列表依赖关系。为了解决这一问题，我们将用户行为轨迹预测（UBTP）定义为一个新的任务设置，明确建模长期用户偏好。我们引入了基于扩散的训练框架——列表偏好扩散优化（LPDO），该框架直接优化整个项目序列上的结构化偏好。LPDO结合了Plackett-Luce监督信号，并推导出与列表排名似然性紧密对齐的变分下界，从而在去噪步骤中实现一致的偏好生成，并克服了先前扩散方法中的独立标记假设。为了严格评估多步预测质量，我们提出了针对该任务的具体度量标准——顺序匹配（SeqMatch），该度量标准衡量轨迹的精确一致性，并采用困惑度（PPL）来评估概率保真度。在真实世界的用户行为基准测试上的广泛实验表明，LPDO始终优于最先进的基线方法，确立了使用扩散模型进行结构化偏好学习的新基准。

[No.239] Multi-Token Prediction Needs Registers
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 多令牌预测需要寄存器
类别: deep learning
作者: Anastasios Gerontopoulos;Spyros Gidaris;Nikos Komodakis;
机构: University of Crete;Athena Research and Innovation Centre;Valeo;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=WDdBhcwzGe
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10518
英摘: Multi-token prediction has emerged as a promising objective for improving language model pretraining, but its benefits have not consistently generalized to other settings such as fine-tuning. In this paper, we propose MuToR, a simple and effective approach to multi-token prediction that interleaves learnable register tokens into the input sequence, each tasked with predicting future targets. Compared to existing methods, MuToR offers several key advantages: it introduces only a negligible number of additional parameters, requires no architectural changes--ensuring compatibility with off-the-shelf pretrained language models--and remains aligned with the next-token pretraining objective, making it especially well-suited for supervised fine-tuning. Moreover, it naturally supports scalable prediction horizons. We demonstrate the effectiveness and versatility of MuToR across a range of use cases, including supervised fine-tuning, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), and pretraining, on challenging generative tasks in both language and vision domains. Our code will be available at: https://github.com/nasosger/MuToR.
中摘: 多令牌预测已成为提高语言模型预训练效果的一个有前途的目标，但其优势并未一致推广到其他设置，如微调。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为MuToR的简单而有效的方法来实现多令牌预测，该方法将可学习的注册令牌插入输入序列中，每个令牌负责预测未来的目标。与现有方法相比，MuToR具有几个关键优势：它仅引入了极少数量的额外参数，不需要进行架构更改——确保与现成的预训练语言模型兼容——并且仍然与下一个令牌的预训练目标保持一致，使其特别适合监督微调。此外，它自然支持可扩展的预测时间范围。我们在一系列应用场景中展示了MuToR的有效性和多功能性，包括监督微调、参数高效微调（PEFT）、以及预训练，在语言和视觉领域的具有挑战性的生成任务中均表现出色。我们的代码将在以下地址提供：https://github.com/nasosger/MuToR。

[No.244] Uncertainty-Calibrated Prediction of Randomly-Timed Biomarker Trajectories with Conformal Bands
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于校准不确定性预测随机时间点生物标志物轨迹的方法

英摘: Despite recent progress in predicting biomarker trajectories from real clinical data, uncertainty in the predictions poses high-stakes risks (e.g., misdiagnosis) that limit their clinical deployment. To enable safe and reliable use of such predictions in healthcare, we introduce a conformal method for uncertainty-calibrated prediction of biomarker trajectories resulting from randomly-timed clinical visits of patients. Our approach extends conformal prediction to the setting of randomly-timed trajectories via a novel nonconformity score that produces prediction bands guaranteed to cover the unknown biomarker trajectories with a user-prescribed probability. We apply our method across a wide range of standard and state-of-the-art predictors for two well-established brain biomarkers of Alzheimer's disease, using neuroimaging data from real clinical studies. We observe that our conformal prediction bands consistently achieve the desired coverage, while also being tighter than baseline prediction bands. To further account for population heterogeneity, we develop group-conditional conformal bands and test their coverage guarantees across various demographic and clinically relevant subpopulations. Moreover, we demonstrate the clinical utility of our conformal bands in identifying subjects at high risk of progression to Alzheimer's disease. Specifically, we introduce an uncertainty-calibrated risk score that enables the identification of 17.5% more high-risk subjects compared to standard risk scores, highlighting the value of uncertainty calibration in real-world clinical decision making. Our code is available at github.com/vatass/ConformalBiomarkerTrajectories.

[No.246] Geometric Mixture Models for Electrolyte Conductivity Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 几何混合模型在电解质电导率预测中的应用
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Anyi Li;Jiacheng Cen;Songyou Li;Mingze Li;YANG YU;Wenbing Huang;
机构: Renmin University of China;;Tencent;

英摘: Accurate prediction of ionic conductivity in electrolyte systems is crucial for advancing numerous scientific and technological applications. While significant progress has been made, current research faces two fundamental challenges: (1) the lack of high-quality standardized benchmarks, and (2) inadequate modeling of geometric structure and intermolecular interactions in mixture systems. To address these limitations, we first reorganize and enhance the CALiSol and DiffMix electrolyte datasets by incorporating geometric graph representations of molecules. We then propose GeoMix, a novel geometry-aware framework that preserves Set-SE(3) equivariance-an essential but challenging property for mixture systems. At the heart of GeoMix lies the Geometric Interaction Network (GIN), an equivariant module specifically designed for intermolecular geometric message passing. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that GeoMix consistently outperforms diverse baselines (including MLPs, GNNs, and geometric GNNs) across both datasets, validating the importance of cross-molecular geometric interactions and equivariant message passing for accurate property prediction. This work not only establishes new benchmarks for electrolyte research but also provides a general geometric learning framework that advances modeling of mixture systems in energy materials, pharmaceutical development, and beyond.
中摘: 准确预测电解质系统中的离子电导率对于推进众多科学和技术应用至关重要。尽管取得了显著进展，当前研究仍面临两大根本挑战：（1）缺乏高质量的标准基准数据集，（2）对混合系统中的几何结构和分子间相互作用的建模不足。为解决这些限制，我们首先通过引入分子的几何图表示，重新组织并增强了CALiSol和DiffMix电解质数据集。然后，我们提出了一种名为GeoMix的新颖几何感知框架，该框架保留了Set-SE(3)不变性——这一对混合系统至关重要的但极具挑战性的性质。GeoMix的核心在于几何交互网络（GIN），这是一种专门设计用于分子间几何消息传递的不变性模块。全面的实验表明，GeoMix在两个数据集上均能持续超越各种基线模型（包括MLPs、GNNs和几何GNNs），验证了跨分子几何交互和不变性消息传递对于准确预测性质的重要性。这项工作不仅为电解质研究建立了新的基准，还提供了一个通用的几何学习框架，推动了能量材料、药物开发等领域中混合系统建模的进步。

[No.248] Impartial Selection with Predictions
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 公正选择与预测
类别: theory
作者: Javier Cembrano;Felix Fischer;Max Klimm;
机构: Max-Planck Institute;;Technische Universität Berlin;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=vyI84ToW3q
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19002
英摘: We study the selection of agents based on mutual nominations, a theoretical problem with many applications from committee selection to AI alignment. As agents both select and are selected, they may be incentivized to misrepresent their true opinion about the eligibility of others to influence their own chances of selection. Impartial mechanisms circumvent this issue by guaranteeing that the selection of an agent is independent of the nominations cast by that agent. Previous research has established strong bounds on the performance of impartial mechanisms, measured by their ability to approximate the number of nominations for the most highly nominated agents. We study to what extent the performance of impartial mechanisms can be improved if they are given a prediction of a set of agents receiving a maximum number of nominations. Specifically, we provide bounds on the consistency and robustness of such mechanisms, where consistency measures the performance of the mechanisms when the prediction is accurate and robustness its performance when the prediction is inaccurate. For the general setting where up to $k$ agents are to be selected and agents nominate any number of other agents, we give a mechanism with consistency $1-O\big(\frac{1}{k}\big)$ and robustness $1-\frac{1}{e}-O\big(\frac{1}{k}\big)$. For the special case of selecting a single agent based on a single nomination per agent, we prove that $1$-consistency can be achieved while guaranteeing $\frac{1}{2}$-robustness. A close comparison with previous results shows that (asymptotically) optimal consistency can be achieved with little to no sacrifice in terms of robustness.
中摘: 我们研究基于相互提名选择代理的问题，这是一个从委员会选拔到人工智能对齐等多个应用领域都具有理论意义的问题。由于代理既进行选择又接受被选，他们可能会受到激励去歪曲他们对其他代理资格的真实看法，从而影响自己的被选机会。公平机制通过确保一个代理的选拔与其自身的提名无关来规避这一问题。先前的研究已经建立了公平机制性能的强大边界，这些边界是根据它们近似最高得票代理数量的能力来衡量的。我们研究如果公平机制获得一组将收到最多提名的代理的预测，其性能可以得到怎样的提升。具体来说，我们提供了关于这种机制一致性和稳健性的边界，其中一致性衡量预测准确时机制的表现，而稳健性衡量预测不准确时机制的表现。对于一般情况，最多可选择$k$个代理且每个代理可以提名任意数量的其他代理，我们提供了一个一致性能达到$1-O\big(\frac{1}{k}\big)$、稳健性能达到$1-\frac{1}{e}-O\big(\frac{1}{k}\big)$的机制。对于每个代理仅能提名一个其他代理并据此选择单个代理的特殊情况，我们证明了一致性能达到$1$，同时保证稳健性不低于$\frac{1}{2}$。与先前的结果进行详细比较表明，可以在几乎不影响稳健性的前提下实现最优的一致性。

[No.249] Procurement Auctions with Predictions: Improved Frugality for Facility Location
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 预测辅助的采购拍卖：设施定位中的改进节约性
类别: theory
作者: Eric Balkanski;Nicholas DeFilippis;Vasilis Gkatzelis;Xizhi Tan;
机构: Columbia University;New York University;;Drexel University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=sce9lLS3Gn
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09367
英摘: We study the problem of designing procurement auctions for the strategic uncapacitated facility location problem: a company needs to procure a set of facility locations in order to serve its customers and each facility location is owned by a strategic agent. Each owner has a private cost for providing access to their facility (e.g., renting it or selling it to the company) and needs to be compensated accordingly. The goal is to design truthful auctions that decide which facilities the company should procure and how much to pay the corresponding owners, aiming to minimize the total cost, i.e., the monetary cost paid to the owners and the connection cost suffered by the customers (their distance to the nearest facility). We evaluate the performance of these auctions using the \emph{frugality ratio}.
  We first analyze the performance of the classic VCG auction in this context and prove that its frugality ratio is exactly $3$. We then leverage the learning-augmented framework and design auctions that are augmented with predictions regarding the owners' private costs. Specifically, we propose a family of learning-augmented auctions that achieve significant payment reductions when the predictions are accurate, leading to much better frugality ratios. At the same time, we demonstrate that these auctions remain robust even if the predictions are arbitrarily inaccurate, and maintain reasonable frugality ratios even under adversarially chosen predictions. We finally provide a family of ``error-tolerant'' auctions that maintain improved frugality ratios even if the predictions are only approximately accurate, and we provide upper bounds on their frugality ratio as a function of the prediction error.
中摘: 我们研究了为战略无容量设施定位问题设计采购拍卖的问题：一家公司需要采购一组设施位置以服务其客户，而每个设施位置均由一个战略代理拥有。每个所有者都有提供其设施访问权的私人成本（例如，租给或卖给公司），并且需要相应地得到补偿。目标是设计诚实的拍卖机制，决定公司应该采购哪些设施以及向相应的所有者支付多少费用，旨在最小化总成本，即支付给所有者的货币成本和客户所遭受的连接成本（他们到最近设施的距离）。我们使用“节约率”来评估这些拍卖的表现。

首先，我们分析了经典VCG拍卖在这种情境下的表现，并证明其节约率为精确的3。然后，我们利用学习增强框架，设计了结合预测所有者私人成本的拍卖机制。具体来说，我们提出了一组学习增强拍卖机制，在预测准确时能够显著减少支付金额，从而获得更好的节约率。同时，我们展示了即使预测任意不准确，这些拍卖机制仍然具有鲁棒性，并且即使在对抗性选择的预测下也能保持合理的节约率。最后，我们提供了一组“容错型”拍卖机制，即使预测仅近似准确也能维持改进的节约率，并提供了它们节约率的上界，作为预测误差的函数。

[No.250] Whole-Body Conditioned Egocentric Video Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: We train models to Predict Ego-centric Video from human Actions (PEVA), given the past video and an action represented by the relative 3D body pose. [No.251] Multiscale guidance of protein structure prediction with heterogeneous cryo-EM data
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 使用异构冷冻电镜数据的蛋白质结构预测的多尺度指导
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Rishwanth Raghu;Axel Levy;Gordon Wetzstein;Ellen D Zhong;
机构: Princeton University;Stanford University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=T3DF4yxcPz
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04490
英摘: Protein structure prediction models are now capable of generating accurate 3D structural hypotheses from sequence alone. However, they routinely fail to capture the conformational diversity of dynamic biomolecular complexes, often requiring heuristic MSA subsampling approaches for generating alternative states. In parallel, cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) has emerged as a powerful tool for imaging near-native structural heterogeneity, but is challenged by arduous pipelines to transform raw experimental data into atomic models. Here, we bridge the gap between these modalities, combining cryo-EM density maps with the rich sequence and biophysical priors learned by protein structure prediction models. Our method, CryoBoltz, guides the sampling trajectory of a pretrained biomolecular structure prediction model using both global and local structural constraints derived from density maps, driving predictions towards conformational states consistent with the experimental data. We demonstrate that this flexible yet powerful inference-time approach allows us to build atomic models into heterogeneous cryo-EM maps across a variety of dynamic biomolecular systems including transporters and antibodies. Code is available at https://github.com/ml-struct-bio/cryoboltz .
中摘: 蛋白质结构预测模型现在能够仅从序列信息生成准确的三维结构假设。然而，它们通常无法捕捉动态生物分子复合物的构象多样性，常常需要使用启发式的MSA子采样方法来生成替代状态。与此同时，冷冻电子显微镜（cryo-EM）已成为成像接近天然结构异质性的强大工具，但其将原始实验数据转化为原子模型的过程极为复杂。在这里，我们弥合了这两种方法之间的差距，结合了来自冷冻电子显微镜密度图的全局和局部结构约束，以及蛋白质结构预测模型学习到的丰富序列和生物物理先验知识。我们的方法CryoBoltz使用这些约束来引导预训练的生物分子结构预测模型的采样轨迹，推动预测向与实验数据一致的构象状态靠拢。我们证明，这种灵活而强大的推理时方法使我们能够在包括转运蛋白和抗体在内的各种动态生物分子系统中构建原子模型，并将其整合到异质的冷冻电子显微镜地图中。相关代码可在https://github.com/ml-struct-bio/cryoboltz 获取。

[No.252] Zero-Shot Performance Prediction for Probabilistic Scaling Laws
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 零样本性能预测与概率缩放定律
类别: general machine learning
作者: Viktoria Schram;Markus Hiller;Daniel Beck;Trevor Cohn;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16743
英摘: The prediction of learning curves for Natural Language Processing (NLP) models enables informed decision-making to meet specific performance objectives, while reducing computational overhead and lowering the costs associated with dataset acquisition and curation. In this work, we formulate the prediction task as a multitask learning problem, where each task's data is modelled as being organized within a two-layer hierarchy. To model the shared information and dependencies across tasks and hierarchical levels, we employ latent variable multi-output Gaussian Processes, enabling to account for task correlations and supporting zero-shot prediction of learning curves (LCs). We demonstrate that this approach facilitates the development of probabilistic scaling laws at lower costs. Applying an active learning strategy, LCs can be queried to reduce predictive uncertainty and provide predictions close to ground truth scaling laws. We validate our framework on three small-scale NLP datasets with up to $30$ LCs. These are obtained from nanoGPT models, from bilingual translation using mBART and Transformer models, and from multilingual translation using M2M100 models of varying sizes.
中摘: 自然语言处理（NLP）模型的学习曲线预测能够促进基于特定性能目标的知情决策，同时减少计算开销并降低数据集获取和整理的相关成本。在本项工作中，我们将预测任务建模为一个多任务学习问题，其中每个任务的数据被组织在一个两层层次结构内。为了建模跨任务和层次结构的信息共享与依赖关系，我们采用了潜在变量多输出高斯过程，这使得我们可以考虑任务间的相关性，并支持零样本预测学习曲线（LCs）。我们证明了这种方法可以以较低的成本开发出概率缩放定律。通过应用主动学习策略，可以从LCs中查询信息来减少预测不确定性，并提供接近实际缩放定律的预测。我们在三个小型NLP数据集中验证了我们的框架，这些数据集最多包含30条LCs，分别来自nanoGPT模型、使用mBART和Transformer模型的双语翻译以及使用不同大小的M2M100模型的多语言翻译。

[No.253] Adaptive Prediction-Powered AutoEval with Reliability and Efficiency Guarantees
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 自适应预测驱动的自动评估，附有可靠性和效率保证
类别: theory
作者: Sangwoo Park;Matteo Zecchin;Osvaldo Simeone;
机构: Imperial College London;King's College London;
类型: Spotlight

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18659
英摘: Selecting artificial intelligence (AI) models, such as large language models (LLMs), from multiple candidates requires accurate performance estimation. This is ideally achieved through empirical evaluations involving abundant real-world data. However, such evaluations are costly and impractical at scale. To address this challenge, autoevaluation methods leverage synthetic data produced by automated evaluators, such as LLMs-as-judges, reducing variance but potentially introducing bias. Recent approaches have employed semi-supervised prediction-powered inference (PPI) to correct for the bias of autoevaluators. However, the use of autoevaluators may lead in practice to a degradation in sample efficiency compared to conventional methods using only real-world data. In this paper, we propose R-AutoEval+, a novel framework that provides finite-sample reliability guarantees on the model evaluation, while also ensuring an enhanced (or at least no worse) sample efficiency compared to conventional methods. The key innovation of R-AutoEval+ is an adaptive construction of the model evaluation variable, which dynamically tunes its reliance on synthetic data, reverting to conventional methods when the autoevaluator is insufficiently accurate. Experiments on the use of LLMs-as-judges for the optimization of quantization settings for the weights of an LLM, for prompt design in LLMs, and for test-time reasoning budget allocation in LLMs confirm the reliability and efficiency of R-AutoEval+.
中摘: 从多个候选者中选择人工智能（AI）模型，例如大型语言模型（LLMs），需要准确的性能估计。理想情况下，这可以通过涉及大量真实世界数据的实际评估来实现。然而，这种评估在大规模应用中成本高昂且不切实际。为了解决这一挑战，自评估方法利用自动化评估器生成的合成数据，如“LLM作为裁判”，减少方差但可能引入偏差。最近的方法采用了半监督预测驱动的推理（PPI）来纠正自评估器的偏差。然而，在实践中，使用自评估器可能会导致与仅使用真实世界数据的传统方法相比，样本效率下降。在这篇论文中，我们提出了一种名为R-AutoEval+的新框架，它能够在有限样本下提供模型评估的可靠性保证，同时确保样本效率至少不低于传统方法。R-AutoEval+的关键创新在于自适应构建模型评估变量，该变量动态调整其对合成数据的依赖性，当自评估器不够准确时，自动切换回传统方法。实验表明，使用“LLM作为裁判”进行LLM权重的量化设置优化、LLM提示设计以及LLM测试时推理预算分配都能验证R-AutoEval+的可靠性和效率。

[No.254] Prediction-Powered Causal Inferences
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于预测的因果推断
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Riccardo Cadei;Ilker Demirel;Piersilvio De Bartolomeis;Lukas Lindorfer;Sylvia Cremer;Cordelia Schmid;Francesco Locatello;

英摘: In many scientific experiments, the data annotating cost constraints the pace for testing novel hypotheses. Yet, modern machine learning pipelines offer a promising solution, provided their predictions yield correct conclusions. We focus on Prediction-Powered Causal Inferences (PPCI), i.e., estimating the treatment effect in an unlabeled target experiment, relying on training data with the same outcome annotated but potentially different treatment or effect modifiers. We first show that conditional calibration guarantees valid PPCI at population level. Then, we introduce a sufficient representation constraint transferring validity across experiments, which we propose to enforce in practice in Deconfounded Empirical Risk Minimization, our new model-agnostic training objective. We validate our method on synthetic and real-world scientific data, solving impossible problem instances for Empirical Risk Minimization even with standard invariance constraints. In particular, for the first time, we achieve valid causal inference on a scientific experiment with complex recording and no human annotations, fine-tuning a foundational model on our similar annotated experiment.
中摘: 在许多科学实验中，标注数据的成本限制了测试新颖假设的进度。然而，现代机器学习管道提供了一个有希望的解决方案，前提是它们的预测能够得出正确的结论。我们关注的是预测驱动因果推断（Prediction-Powered Causal Inferences, PPCI），即利用具有相同结果标注但可能有不同的治疗或效应修饰的训练数据来估计未标记目标实验中的治疗效果。首先，我们证明条件校准保证了PPCI在总体水平上的有效性。然后，我们引入了一种足够的表示约束，用于在不同实验之间转移有效性，提出在我们的新型模型无关训练目标——去混杂的经验风险最小化（Deconfounded Empirical Risk Minimization）中实际实施这种约束。我们在合成和真实世界的科学数据上验证了该方法，即使使用标准不变性约束，也能解决经验风险最小化无法解决的问题实例。特别是，我们首次实现了在复杂记录且无人类标注的情况下对科学实验进行有效的因果推断，并在类似标注的实验上对基础模型进行了微调。

[No.255] Exploring the Noise Robustness of Online Conformal Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 探索在线自适应预测的抗噪性
类别: general machine learning
作者: HuaJun Xi;Kangdao Liu;Hao Zeng;Wenguang Sun;Hongxin Wei;
机构: Southern University of Science and Technology;University of Macau;Zhejiang University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=3veDGO9KiK
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18363
英摘: Conformal prediction is an emerging technique for uncertainty quantification that constructs prediction sets guaranteed to contain the true label with a predefined probability. Recent work develops online conformal prediction methods that adaptively construct prediction sets to accommodate distribution shifts. However, existing algorithms typically assume perfect label accuracy which rarely holds in practice. In this work, we investigate the robustness of online conformal prediction under uniform label noise with a known noise rate, in both constant and dynamic learning rate schedules. We show that label noise causes a persistent gap between the actual mis-coverage rate and the desired rate $α$, leading to either overestimated or underestimated coverage guarantees. To address this issue, we propose Noise Robust Online Conformal Prediction (dubbed NR-OCP) by updating the threshold with a novel robust pinball loss, which provides an unbiased estimate of clean pinball loss without requiring ground-truth labels. Our theoretical analysis shows that NR-OCP eliminates the coverage gap in both constant and dynamic learning rate schedules, achieving a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ for both empirical and expected coverage errors under uniform label noise. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method by achieving both precise coverage and improved efficiency.
中摘: 自适应预测是新兴的一种不确定性量化技术，它构建的预测集能够以预设的概率包含真实的标签。最近的研究发展了在线自适应预测方法，这些方法能够适应性地构建预测集以应对分布变化。然而，现有的算法通常假设标签完全准确，而在实践中这种情况很少成立。在本文中，我们探讨了在线自适应预测在已知噪声率的均匀标签噪声下的鲁棒性，无论是恒定的学习率还是动态的学习率安排。我们展示了标签噪声会导致实际的覆盖率误差与期望的误差率 $\alpha$ 之间存在持续的差距，从而导致覆盖保证要么被高估要么被低估。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为噪声鲁棒在线自适应预测（简称NR-OCP）的方法，通过使用一种新的鲁棒Pinball损失更新阈值，无需真实标签即可提供清洁Pinball损失的无偏估计。我们的理论分析表明，NR-OCP 在恒定和动态学习率安排下均能消除覆盖率差距，在均匀标签噪声下实现了经验覆盖率误差和期望覆盖率误差的收敛速率均为 $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$。广泛的实验验证了该方法的有效性，不仅实现了精确的覆盖率，还提高了效率。

[No.257] QuadricFormer: Scene as Superquadrics for 3D Semantic Occupancy Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: QuadricFormer：场景的超二次曲面表示用于3D语义占用预测

作者: Sicheng Zuo;Wenzhao Zheng;Xiaoyong Han;Longchao Yang;Yong Pan;Jiwen Lu;

英摘: 3D occupancy prediction is crucial for robust autonomous driving systems as it enables comprehensive perception of environmental structures and semantics. Most existing methods employ dense voxel-based scene representations, ignoring the sparsity of driving scenes and resulting in inefficiency. Recent works explore object-centric representations based on sparse Gaussians, but their ellipsoidal shape prior limits the modeling of diverse structures. In real-world driving scenes, objects exhibit rich geometries (e.g., cuboids, cylinders, and irregular shapes), necessitating excessive ellipsoidal Gaussians densely packed for accurate modeling, which leads to inefficient representations. To address this, we propose to use geometrically expressive superquadrics as scene primitives, enabling efficient representation of complex structures with fewer primitives through their inherent shape diversity. We develop a probabilistic superquadric mixture model, which interprets each superquadric as an occupancy probability distribution with a corresponding geometry prior, and calculates semantics through probabilistic mixture. Building on this, we present QuadricFormer, a superquadric-based model for efficient 3D occupancy prediction, and introduce a pruning-and-splitting module to further enhance modeling efficiency by concentrating superquadrics in occupied regions. Extensive experiments on the nuScenes dataset demonstrate that QuadricFormer achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining superior efficiency.
中摘: 三维占用预测对于稳健的自动驾驶系统至关重要，因为它能够全面感知环境结构和语义。现有的大多数方法采用密集体素场景表示，忽视了驾驶场景的稀疏性，导致效率低下。近期的研究探索了基于稀疏高斯分布的对象中心化表示，但其椭球形状先验限制了对多样结构的建模能力。在现实世界的驾驶场景中，物体表现出丰富的几何形状（例如，立方体、圆柱体和不规则形状），这需要密集地使用大量的椭球高斯分布来实现精确建模，从而导致效率低下。为了解决这一问题，我们提出使用几何表达能力强的超二次曲面作为场景基本单元，通过其固有的形状多样性，用更少的基本单元高效地表示复杂结构。我们开发了一种概率超二次曲面混合模型，将每个超二次曲面解释为一个与对应几何形状先验相关的占用概率分布，并通过概率混合计算语义。在此基础上，我们提出了QuadricFormer，这是一种基于超二次曲面的高效三维占用预测模型，并引入了一个剪枝和分裂模块，进一步通过集中超二次曲面在占据区域来增强建模效率。在nuScenes数据集上的广泛实验表明，QuadricFormer在保持优越效率的同时实现了最先进的性能。

[No.258] UniMotion: A Unified Motion Framework for Simulation, Prediction and Planning

英摘: Motion simulation, prediction and planning are foundational tasks in autonomous driving, each essential for modeling and reasoning about dynamic traffic scenarios. [No.261] Prot2Text-V2: Protein Function Prediction with Multimodal Contrastive Alignment
来源: NeurIPS 2025

标题: Prot2Text-V2：基于多模态对比对齐的蛋白质功能预测
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Xiao Fei;Michail Chatzianastasis;Sarah Almeida Carneiro;Hadi Abdine;Lawrence Paul Petalidis;Michalis Vazirgiannis;

英摘: Predicting protein function from sequence is a central challenge in computational biology. While existing methods rely heavily on structured ontologies or similarity-based techniques, they often lack the flexibility to express structure-free functional descriptions and novel biological functions. In this work, we introduce Prot2Text-V2, a novel multimodal sequence-to-text model that generates free-form natural language descriptions of protein function directly from amino acid sequences. Our method combines a protein language model as a sequence encoder (ESM-3B) and a decoder-only language model (LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct) through a lightweight nonlinear modality projector. A key innovation is our Hybrid Sequence-level Contrastive Alignment Learning (H-SCALE), which improves cross-modal learning by matching mean- and std-pooled protein embeddings with text representations via contrastive loss. After the alignment phase, we apply instruction-based fine-tuning using LoRA on the decoder to teach the model how to generate accurate protein function descriptions conditioned on the protein sequence. We train Prot2Text-V2 on about 250K curated entries from SwissProt and evaluate it under low-homology conditions, where test sequences have low similarity with training samples. Prot2Text-V2 consistently outperforms traditional and LLM-based baselines across various metrics.
中摘: 从序列预测蛋白质功能是计算生物学中的一个核心挑战。尽管现有的方法依赖于结构化的本体论或基于相似性的技术，但它们往往缺乏表达无结构的功能描述和新型生物功能的灵活性。在本工作中，我们引入了Prot2Text-V2，这是一种新颖的多模态序列到文本模型，可以直接从氨基酸序列生成自由格式的自然语言描述。我们的方法结合了一个蛋白质语言模型作为序列编码器（ESM-3B）和一个仅解码器的语言模型（LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct），并通过一个轻量级的非线性模态投影器进行连接。关键创新是我们提出的混合序列级对比对齐学习（H-SCALE），它通过对比损失将均值和方差池化的蛋白质嵌入与文本表示匹配，从而提高跨模态学习的效果。在对齐阶段之后，我们使用LoRA进行指令基础的微调，教导模型如何根据蛋白质序列生成准确的蛋白质功能描述。我们在大约250K个来自SwissProt的精心策划条目上训练了Prot2Text-V2，并在测试序列与训练样本相似度较低的条件下对其进行评估。Prot2Text-V2在各种指标上始终优于传统方法和基于大语言模型的方法。

[No.264] Scalable and adaptive prediction bands with kernel sum-of-squares
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于核平方和的可扩展和自适应预测带
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Louis Allain;Sébastien Da Veiga;Brian Staber;
机构: Ecole Nationale de la Statistique et de l'Analyse de l'information;Safran Tech;;Safran;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=thc972q5a9
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21039
英摘: Conformal Prediction (CP) is a popular framework for constructing prediction bands with valid coverage in finite samples, while being free of any distributional assumption. A well-known limitation of conformal prediction is the lack of adaptivity, although several works introduced practically efficient alternate procedures. In this work, we build upon recent ideas that rely on recasting the CP problem as a statistical learning problem, directly targeting coverage and adaptivity. This statistical learning problem is based on reproducible kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHS) and kernel sum-of-squares (SoS) methods. First, we extend previous results with a general representer theorem and exhibit the dual formulation of the learning problem. Crucially, such dual formulation can be solved efficiently by accelerated gradient methods with several hundreds or thousands of samples, unlike previous strategies based on off-the-shelf semidefinite programming algorithms. Second, we introduce a new hyperparameter tuning strategy tailored specifically to target adaptivity through bounds on test-conditional coverage. This strategy, based on the Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC), is introduced here to tune kernel lengthscales in our framework, but has broader applicability since it could be used in any CP algorithm where the score function is learned. Finally, extensive experiments are conducted to show how our method compares to related work. All figures can be reproduced with the accompanying code.
中摘:  conformal预测（CP）是一种流行的框架，用于在有限样本中构建具有有效覆盖范围的预测区间，同时无需任何分布假设。conformal预测的一个众所周知的局限性是缺乏适应性，尽管有一些工作引入了实际高效的替代程序。在这项工作中，我们基于近期的一些想法，将CP问题重新表述为统计学习问题，直接针对覆盖范围和适应性进行优化。这一统计学习问题基于再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）和核二次和方法（SoS）。首先，我们通过一个通用的表现者定理扩展了先前的结果，并展示了学习问题的对偶形式。这种对偶形式可以通过加速梯度方法高效地解决，即使有几百或几千个样本，而之前的策略依赖于现成的半定规划算法。其次，我们提出了一种新的超参数调整策略，专门针对通过测试条件覆盖的界来目标适应性。该策略基于希尔伯克拉梅尔独立性判别准则（HSIC），在这里用于调整我们的框架中的核长度尺度，但其应用范围更广，因为还可以用于任何CP算法，其中评分函数是学习得到的。最后，进行了大量的实验，以展示我们的方法与相关工作的比较。所有图表都可以通过随附的代码进行再现。

[No.266] ODG: Occupancy Prediction Using Dual Gaussians
来源: NeurIPS 2025

英摘: Occupancy prediction infers fine-grained 3D geometry and semantics from camera images of the surrounding environment, making it a critical perception task for autonomous driving. Existing methods either adopt dense grids as scene representation, which is difficult to scale to high resolution, or learn the entire scene using a single set of sparse queries, which is insufficient to handle the various object characteristics. In this paper, we present ODG, a hierarchical dual sparse Gaussian representation to effectively capture complex scene dynamics. Building upon the observation that driving scenes can be universally decomposed into static and dynamic counterparts, we define dual Gaussian queries to better model the diverse scene objects. We utilize a hierarchical Gaussian transformer to predict the occupied voxel centers and semantic classes along with the Gaussian parameters. Leveraging the real-time rendering capability of 3D Gaussian Splatting, we also impose rendering supervision with available depth and semantic map annotations injecting pixel-level alignment to boost occupancy learning. [No.267] TimeXL: Explainable Multi-modal Time Series Prediction with LLM-in-the-Loop
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: TimeXL：具有LLM参与的可解释多模态时间序列预测
类别: deep learning
作者: Yushan Jiang;Wenchao Yu;Geon Lee;Dongjin Song;Kijung Shin;Wei Cheng;Yanchi Liu;Haifeng Chen;
机构: University of Connecticut;University of California, Los Angeles;Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;;NEC Laboratories;

链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=WRwr2YZ4zt
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01013
英摘: Time series analysis provides essential insights for real-world system dynamics and informs downstream decision-making, yet most existing methods often overlook the rich contextual signals present in auxiliary modalities. To bridge this gap, we introduce TimeXL, a multi-modal prediction framework that integrates a prototype-based time series encoder with three collaborating Large Language Models (LLMs) to deliver more accurate predictions and interpretable explanations. First, a multi-modal prototype-based encoder processes both time series and textual inputs to generate preliminary forecasts alongside case-based rationales. These outputs then feed into a prediction LLM, which refines the forecasts by reasoning over the encoder's predictions and explanations. Next, a reflection LLM compares the predicted values against the ground truth, identifying textual inconsistencies or noise. Guided by this feedback, a refinement LLM iteratively enhances text quality and triggers encoder retraining. This closed-loop workflow-prediction, critique (reflect), and refinement-continuously boosts the framework's performance and interpretability. Empirical evaluations on four real-world datasets demonstrate that TimeXL achieves up to 8.9% improvement in AUC and produces human-centric, multi-modal explanations, highlighting the power of LLM-driven reasoning for time series prediction.
中摘: 时间序列分析为现实世界系统的动态提供了宝贵的洞察，并指导后续的决策制定，然而现有的大多数方法往往忽视了辅助模态中丰富的上下文信号。为了弥合这一差距，我们引入了TimeXL，这是一个多模态预测框架，它结合了一种原型为基础的时间序列编码器和三个协作的大型语言模型（LLM），以提供更准确的预测和可解释的解释。首先，一个多模态原型编码器处理时间序列和文本输入，生成初步预测和基于案例的理由。这些输出随后传入一个预测LLM，通过推理编码器的预测和解释来细化预测。接着，一个反思LLM将预测值与真实值进行对比，识别文本中的不一致或噪声。根据这种反馈，一个细化LLM会迭代地提升文本质量并触发编码器重新训练。这种闭环工作流程——预测、批判（反思）和细化——不断提升了框架的性能和可解释性。在四个实际数据集上的实证评估表明，TimeXL在AUC上实现了高达8.9%的改进，并生成了以人为本的多模态解释，突显了LLM驱动的推理在时间序列预测中的强大能力。

[No.269] RoME: Domain-Robust Mixture-of-Experts for MILP Solution Prediction across Domains
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: RoME：跨领域稳健的混合专家模型用于MILP解预测
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Tianle Pu;Zijie Geng;Haoyang Liu;Shixuan Liu;Jie Wang;Li Zeng;Chao Chen;Changjun Fan;
机构: National University of Defense Technology;University of Science and Technology of China;;AMS;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=wRQmQ6UXYF
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02331
英摘: Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) is a fundamental and powerful framework for modeling complex optimization problems across diverse domains. Recently, learning-based methods have shown great promise in accelerating MILP solvers by predicting high-quality solutions. However, most existing approaches are developed and evaluated in single-domain settings, limiting their ability to generalize to unseen problem distributions. This limitation poses a major obstacle to building scalable and general-purpose learning-based solvers. To address this challenge, we introduce RoME, a domain-Robust Mixture-of-Experts framework for predicting MILP solutions across domains. RoME dynamically routes problem instances to specialized experts based on learned task embeddings. The model is trained using a two-level distributionally robust optimization strategy: inter-domain to mitigate global shifts across domains, and intra-domain to enhance local robustness by introducing perturbations on task embeddings. We reveal that cross-domain training not only enhances the model's generalization capability to unseen domains but also improves performance within each individual domain by encouraging the model to capture more general intrinsic combinatorial patterns. Specifically, a single RoME model trained on three domains achieves an average improvement of 67.7% then evaluated on five diverse domains. We further test the pretrained model on MIPLIB in a zero-shot setting, demonstrating its ability to deliver measurable performance gains on challenging real-world instances where existing learning-based approaches often struggle to generalize.
中摘: 混合整数线性规划（MILP）是一种基本且强大的框架，用于跨不同领域建模复杂的优化问题。近年来，基于学习的方法在加速MILP求解器方面显示出了巨大的潜力，通过预测高质量的解决方案。然而，大多数现有方法都是在单一领域环境中开发和评估的，限制了它们向未见过的问题分布泛化的能力。这一局限性构成了构建可扩展且通用的学习型求解器的主要障碍。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了RoME（域鲁棒专家混合框架），这是一种能够跨领域预测MILP解决方案的模型。RoME 动态地将问题实例路由给基于学习的任务嵌入的专业专家。该模型采用两层分布鲁棒优化策略进行训练：跨域层面以缓解不同领域之间的全局偏移，以及跨任务层面通过在任务嵌入上引入扰动来增强局部鲁棒性。我们发现，跨域训练不仅提高了模型对未见过领域的泛化能力，还通过鼓励模型捕捉更普遍的内在组合模式，在每个单独领域内也提升了性能。具体而言，一个在三个领域上训练的RoME模型，在五个不同领域上的评估中实现了平均67.7%的改进。我们进一步在MIPLIB上测试了预训练的RoME模型，在零样本设置下展示了其在具有挑战性的现实世界实例中提供可测量性能增益的能力，而这些实例往往是现有基于学习的方法难以泛化的领域。

[No.271] Dynamics of Spontaneous Topic Changes in Next Token Prediction with Self-Attention
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 自发话题变化在自注意力驱动的下一个词预测中的动态研究
类别: deep learning
作者: Mumin Jia;Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez;
机构: York University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=nMLvTru6Zo
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06382
英摘: Human cognition is punctuated by abrupt, spontaneous shifts between topics-driven by emotional, contextual, or associative cues-a phenomenon known as spontaneous thought in neuroscience. In contrast, self-attention based models depend on structured patterns over their inputs to predict each next token, lacking spontaneity. Motivated by this distinction, we characterize spontaneous topic changes in self-attention architectures, revealing both their similarities and their divergences from spontaneous human thought. First, we establish theoretical results under a simplified, single-layer self-attention model with suitable conditions by defining the topic as a set of Token Priority Graphs (TPGs). Specifically, we demonstrate that (1) the model maintains the priority order of tokens related to the input topic, (2) a spontaneous topic change can occur only if lower-priority tokens outnumber all higher-priority tokens of the input topic, and (3) unlike human cognition, the longer context length or the more ambiguous input topic reduces the likelihood of spontaneous change. Second, we empirically validate that these dynamics persist in modern, state-of-the-art LLMs, underscoring a fundamental disparity between human cognition and AI behaviour in the context of spontaneous topic changes. To the best of our knowledge, no prior work has explored these questions with a focus as closely aligned to human thought.
中摘: 人类的认知是由话题驱动的，会突然、自发地在不同话题之间切换，这种现象在神经科学中被称为自发思维。相比之下，基于自注意力模型依赖于输入中的结构化模式来预测下一个词，缺乏自发性。受这一区别启发，我们对自注意力架构中的自发话题变化进行了特征描述，揭示了它们与人类自发思维之间的相似性和差异性。首先，我们在一个简化的一层自注意力模型下建立了理论结果，通过定义话题为一组Token优先图（TPGs）来实现。具体来说，我们证明了以下几点：（1）模型能够保持与输入话题相关的词的优先顺序；（2）只有当低优先级的词的数量超过所有高优先级的词时，才会发生自发的话题变化；（3）与人类认知不同，在更长的上下文长度或更含糊不清的输入话题的情况下，自发变化的可能性会降低。其次，我们通过实验证明了这些动态在现代最先进的语言模型中依然存在，强调了人类认知和AI行为在自发话题变化情境下的根本差异。据我们所知，目前还没有先有研究从如此紧密地与人类思维相一致的角度探讨这些问题。

[No.272] Holistic Order Prediction in Natural Scenes
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction

作者: Pierre Musacchio;Hyunmin Lee;Jaesik Park;

英摘: Even in controlled settings, understanding instance-wise geometries is a challenging task for a wide range of visual models. We mitigate these limitations by proposing InstaFormer, a network capable of holistic order prediction. That is, solely given an input RGB image, InstaFormer returns the full occlusion and depth orderings for all the instances in the scene in a single forward pass. 中摘: 即使在受控环境中，理解实例级别的几何结构也是广泛存在的视觉模型面临的一项具有挑战性的任务。尽管存在专门的系统，但现代艺术依赖于昂贵的输入格式（类别标签、二元分割掩码）和推理成本（大量正向传递）。我们通过提出InstaFormer网络来缓解这些限制。具体来说，仅给定一个输入的RGB图像，InstaFormer就能在一个前向传递过程中返回场景中所有实例的完整遮挡和深度顺序。其核心在于对象查询与潜在掩码描述符之间的交互，这些潜在掩码描述符从语义上代表相同的对象，同时携带互补信息。我们全面地对我们的方法进行了基准测试和消融分析，以突出其有效性。我们的代码和模型已开源，并可从以下URL获取：https://github.com/SNU-VGILab/InstaOrder。

[No.273] Next Semantic Scale Prediction via Hierarchical Diffusion Language Models
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于层次扩散语言模型的下一语义层级预测
类别: deep learning
作者: Cai Zhou;Chenyu Wang;Dinghuai Zhang;Shangyuan Tong;Yifei Wang;Stephen Bates;Tommi Jaakkola;
机构: Massachusetts Institute of Technology;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=kUzBGEuu7w
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08632
英摘: In this paper we introduce Hierarchical Diffusion Language Models (HDLM) -- a novel family of discrete diffusion models for language modeling. HDLM builds on a hierarchical vocabulary where low-level tokens with detailed semantics are surjectively mapped to high-level tokens with coarse-grained meanings. In the forward process, each token is independently perturbed to its higher-level ancestor with more abstract semantics according to the scheduler, while in the reverse process the model progressively predicts the next, more detailed semantics. Taken together, HDLM provides a general time-varying next semantic scale prediction process for language modeling. We derive closed-form expressions for the diffusion Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO), and show that HDLM can be implemented in a flexible manner while including the existing MDLM as a special case. We also propose practical training techniques based on the insights. Extensive text generation experiments validate the effectiveness of HDLM, which demonstrates consistently lower validation and generative perplexity than baselines.
中摘: 在这篇论文中，我们介绍了层次扩散语言模型（HDLM）——一种新的离散扩散模型家族，用于语言建模。HDLM 基于一个分层词汇表，其中低级词汇具有详细的语义，而高级词汇则映射到粗粒度的意义上。在正向过程中，每个词汇独立地被扰动为其更具抽象意义的高级祖先词汇，扰动过程由调度器控制；而在逆向过程中，模型逐步预测更详细的意义。总体而言，HDLM 提供了一种一般的时间变化的下一个语义尺度预测过程，适用于语言建模。我们推导出了扩散证据下界（ELBO）的闭式表达式，并展示了 HDLM 可以灵活实现，同时将现有的MDLM作为其特殊情况包含在内。我们还基于这些见解提出了实用的训练技术。广泛的文本生成实验验证了 HDLM 的有效性，它的一致性表现优于基线模型，在验证和生成困惑度方面均显示出较低的结果。

[No.276] Topology-Aware Conformal Prediction for Stream Networks
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 拓扑感知同伦预测在流网络中的应用
类别: general machine learning
作者: Jifan Zhang;Fangxin Wang;Zihe Song;Philip S. Yu;Kaize Ding;Shixiang Zhu;
机构: Northwestern University;University of Illinois at Chicago;Carnegie Mellon University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Ldni3xeyIa
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04981
英摘: Stream networks, a unique class of spatiotemporal graphs, exhibit complex directional flow constraints and evolving dependencies, making uncertainty quantification a critical yet challenging task. Traditional conformal prediction methods struggle in this setting due to the need for joint predictions across multiple interdependent locations and the intricate spatio-temporal dependencies inherent in stream networks. Existing approaches either neglect dependencies, leading to overly conservative predictions, or rely solely on data-driven estimations, failing to capture the rich topological structure of the network. To address these challenges, we propose Spatio-Temporal Adaptive Conformal Inference (\texttt{STACI}), a novel framework that integrates network topology and temporal dynamics into the conformal prediction framework. \texttt{STACI} introduces a topology-aware nonconformity score that respects directional flow constraints and dynamically adjusts prediction sets to account for temporal distributional shifts. We provide theoretical guarantees on the validity of our approach and demonstrate its superior performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets. Our results show that \texttt{STACI} effectively balances prediction efficiency and coverage, outperforming existing conformal prediction methods for stream networks.
中摘: 流网络是一种独特的时空图类，表现出复杂的方向性流动约束和不断变化的依赖关系，使得不确定性量化成为一个关键但具有挑战性的任务。传统的容规预测方法在这种情况下难以发挥作用，因为需要在多个相互依赖的位置进行联合预测，并且流网络中固有的复杂时空依赖关系。现有方法要么忽略了依赖关系，导致过于保守的预测，要么仅依赖数据驱动的估计，无法捕捉网络丰富的拓扑结构。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了时空自适应容规推理（\texttt{STACI}），这是一种新颖的框架，将网络拓扑和时间动态整合到容规预测框架中。\texttt{STACI} 引入了一种拓扑感知非一致性分数，该分数尊重方向性流动约束，并动态调整预测集以应对时间分布的变化。我们提供了我们方法有效性的理论保证，并在合成和真实世界数据集上展示了其优越性能。我们的结果显示，\texttt{STACI} 有效地平衡了预测效率和覆盖率，在流网络的容规预测方法中表现出色。

[No.277] Stochastically Dominant Peer Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 随机占优同伴预测
类别: theory
作者: Yichi Zhang;Shengwei Xu;Grant Schoenebeck;David Pennock;
机构: ;University of Michigan;Rutgers University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=kmooIAb3Pd
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02259
英摘: Eliciting reliable human feedback is essential for many machine learning tasks, such as learning from noisy labels and aligning AI systems with human preferences. Peer prediction mechanisms incentivize truthful reporting without ground truth verification by scoring agents based on correlations with peers. Traditional mechanisms, which ensure that truth-telling maximizes the expected scores in equilibrium, can elicit honest information while assuming agents' utilities are linear functions of their scores. However, in practice, non-linear payment rules are usually preferred, or agents' utilities are inherently non-linear.
  We propose stochastically dominant truthfulness (SD-truthfulness) as a stronger guarantee: the score distribution of truth-telling stochastically dominates all other strategies, incentivizing truthful reporting for a wide range of monotone utility functions. Our first observation is that no existing peer prediction mechanism naturally satisfies this criterion without strong assumptions. A simple solution -- rounding scores into binary lotteries -- can enforce SD-truthfulness, but often degrades sensitivity, a key property related to fairness and statistical efficiency. We demonstrate how a more careful application of rounding can better preserve sensitivity. Furthermore, we introduce a new enforced agreement (EA) mechanism that is theoretically guaranteed to be SD-truthful in binary-signal settings under mild assumptions, and empirically achieves the highest sensitivity among all known SD-truthful mechanisms.
中摘: 从嘈杂标签中获取可靠的人类反馈对于许多机器学习任务至关重要，例如从嘈杂标签中学习和使AI系统与人类偏好保持一致。同伴预测机制通过根据与同伴的相关性评分代理，激励其真实报告而不进行事实验证。传统机制确保在均衡状态下，说实话能够最大化预期得分，从而在假设代理的效用与其得分呈线性关系的情况下获取诚实信息。然而，在实践中，通常更倾向于使用非线性的支付规则，或者代理的效用本身就是非线性的。

我们提出了一种更强的保证：即随机占优真实性（SD-真实性），这意味着说实话的得分分布随机占优所有其他策略，从而在广泛的单调效用函数下激励真实报告。我们的第一个观察是，没有任何现有的同伴预测机制能够在不作强烈假设的情况下自然满足这一标准。一个简单的解决方案——将得分转换为二元彩票——可以强制执行SD-真实性，但往往会降低敏感性，这是一种与公平性和统计效率密切相关的关键属性。我们展示了如何更加谨慎地应用这种转换方法来更好地保留敏感性。此外，我们引入了一种新的强制一致性（EA）机制，在轻微假设下理论上保证在二元信号设置中SD-真实性，并且在所有已知的SD-真实性机制中实现了最高的敏感性。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.278] Multi-scale Temporal Prediction via Incremental Generation and Multi-agent Collaboration
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 多尺度时间预测通过增量生成和多agent协作
类别: applications
作者: Zhitao Zeng;Guojian Yuan;Junyuan Mao;Yuxuan Wang;Xiaoshuang Jia;Yueming Jin;
机构: National University of Singapore;University of Science and Technology of China;Alibaba Group;Beijing Institute for General Artificial Intelligence;Renmin University of China;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=wTBhWbCRpN
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17429
英摘: Accurate temporal prediction is the bridge between comprehensive scene understanding and embodied artificial intelligence. However, predicting multiple fine-grained states of a scene at multiple temporal scales is difficult for vision-language models. We formalize the Multi-Scale Temporal Prediction (MSTP) task in general and surgical scenes by decomposing multi-scale into two orthogonal dimensions: the temporal scale, forecasting states of humans and surgery at varying look-ahead intervals, and the state scale, modeling a hierarchy of states in general and surgical scenes. For example, in general scenes, states of contact relationships are finer-grained than states of spatial relationships. In surgical scenes, medium-level steps are finer-grained than high-level phases yet remain constrained by their encompassing phase. To support this unified task, we introduce the first MSTP Benchmark, featuring synchronized annotations across multiple state scales and temporal scales. We further propose a method, Incremental Generation and Multi-agent Collaboration (IG-MC), which integrates two key innovations. First, we present a plug-and-play incremental generation module that continuously synthesizes up-to-date visual previews at expanding temporal scales to inform multiple decision-making agents, keeping decisions and generated visuals synchronized and preventing performance degradation as look-ahead intervals lengthen. Second, we present a decision-driven multi-agent collaboration framework for multi-state prediction, comprising generation, initiation, and multi-state assessment agents that dynamically trigger and evaluate prediction cycles to balance global coherence and local fidelity.
中摘: 准确的时间预测是全面场景理解与具身人工智能之间的桥梁。然而，对于视觉语言模型来说，在多个时间尺度上预测场景的多种精细状态是非常困难的。我们通过将多尺度时间分解为两个正交维度，即时间尺度和状态尺度，来形式化一般场景和手术场景中的多尺度时间预测（Multi-Scale Temporal Prediction, MSTP）任务：时间尺度用于预测人类和手术在不同预见时间间隔下的状态；状态尺度则用于建模一般场景和手术场景中的状态层次结构。例如，在一般场景中，接触关系的状态比空间关系的状态更为精细。而在手术场景中，中等步骤的状态比高级阶段更为精细，但仍受其包含阶段的约束。为了支持这一统一任务，我们引入了首个MSTP基准，该基准包含了跨多个状态尺度和时间尺度的同步注释。此外，我们还提出了一种方法——增量生成与多智能体协作（Incremental Generation and Multi-agent Collaboration, IG-MC），该方法集成了两项关键创新。首先，我们提出了一个可插拔的增量生成模块，该模块能够不断合成最新的视觉预览，并在扩展的时间尺度上持续提供信息，以供多个决策智能体使用，从而保持决策与生成的视觉内容的一致性，并防止随着预见时间间隔延长而导致性能下降。其次，我们提出了一种基于决策的多智能体协作框架，用于多状态预测，该框架包括生成、启动和多状态评估智能体，它们能够动态触发并评估预测周期，以平衡全局一致性与局部准确性。

[No.279] Equivariant Eikonal Neural Networks: Grid-Free, Scalable Travel-Time Prediction on Homogeneous Spaces
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 等变Eikonal神经网络：同质空间上无网格、可扩展的旅行时间预测
类别: deep learning
作者: Alejandro García-Castellanos;David Wessels;Nicky J. van den Berg;Remco Duits;Daniel Pelt;Erik J Bekkers;
机构: ;University of Amsterdam;Eindhoven University of Technology;Leiden University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=dK2BnCH68p
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16035
英摘: We introduce Equivariant Neural Eikonal Solvers, a novel framework that integrates Equivariant Neural Fields (ENFs) with Neural Eikonal Solvers. Our approach employs a single neural field where a unified shared backbone is conditioned on signal-specific latent variables - represented as point clouds in a Lie group - to model diverse Eikonal solutions. The ENF integration ensures equivariant mapping from these latent representations to the solution field, delivering three key benefits: enhanced representation efficiency through weight-sharing, robust geometric grounding, and solution steerability. This steerability allows transformations applied to the latent point cloud to induce predictable, geometrically meaningful modifications in the resulting Eikonal solution. By coupling these steerable representations with Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), our framework accurately models Eikonal travel-time solutions while generalizing to arbitrary Riemannian manifolds with regular group actions. This includes homogeneous spaces such as Euclidean, position-orientation, spherical, and hyperbolic manifolds. We validate our approach through applications in seismic travel-time modeling of 2D, 3D, and spherical benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate superior performance, scalability, adaptability, and user controllability compared to existing Neural Operator-based Eikonal solver methods.
中摘: 我们介绍了一种新颖的框架——对称神经场欧克那诺求解器（Equivariant Neural Eikonal Solvers），该框架将对称神经场（ENFs）与神经欧克那诺求解器相结合。我们的方法采用了一个单一的神经场，其中统一的共享骨干网络根据信号特定的潜在变量——这些潜在变量以李群中的点云形式表示——来建模多种不同的欧克那诺解。ENF的集成确保了从这些潜在表示到解场的对称映射，提供了三个关键优势：通过权重共享增强的表示效率、稳健的几何基础以及解的可控性。这种可控性使得应用于潜在点云的变换能够引起可预测且几何上有意义的结果欧克那诺解的变化。通过将这些可控表示与物理导向神经网络（PINNs）耦合，我们的框架能够在任意具有正规群作用的黎曼流形上准确地建模欧克那诺旅行时间解，这包括欧几里得空间、位置-方向空间、球面空间和双曲空间等均匀空间。我们通过在2D、3D和球面基准数据集中的地震旅行时间建模应用验证了该方法。实验结果表明，与现有的基于神经算子的欧克那诺求解器方法相比，我们的框架具有更优的性能、更高的可扩展性、更强的适应性和用户可控性。

[No.282] Causal Spatio-Temporal Prediction: An Effective and Efficient Multi-Modal Approach
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 因果时空预测：一种有效且高效的多模态方法
类别: deep learning
作者: Yuting Huang;Ziquan Fang;Zhihao Zeng;Lu Chen;Yunjun Gao;
机构: Zhejiang University;;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17637
英摘: Spatio-temporal prediction plays a crucial role in intelligent transportation, weather forecasting, and urban planning. While integrating multi-modal data has shown potential for enhancing prediction accuracy, key challenges persist: (i) inadequate fusion of multi-modal information, (ii) confounding factors that obscure causal relations, and (iii) high computational complexity of prediction models. To address these challenges, we propose E^2-CSTP, an Effective and Efficient Causal multi-modal Spatio-Temporal Prediction framework. E^2-CSTP leverages cross-modal attention and gating mechanisms to effectively integrate multi-modal data. Building on this, we design a dual-branch causal inference approach: the primary branch focuses on spatio-temporal prediction, while the auxiliary branch mitigates bias by modeling additional modalities and applying causal interventions to uncover true causal dependencies. To improve model efficiency, we integrate GCN with the Mamba architecture for accelerated spatio-temporal encoding. Extensive experiments on 4 real-world datasets show that E^2-CSTP significantly outperforms 9 state-of-the-art methods, achieving up to 9.66% improvements in accuracy as well as 17.37%-56.11% reductions in computational overhead.
