[No.283] Learning from Delayed Feedback in Games via Extra Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 通过额外预测从延迟反馈游戏中学习
类别: theory
作者: Yuma Fujimoto;Kenshi Abe;Kaito Ariu;
机构: CyberAgent;University of Electro-Communications;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=FFEFRQGlsS
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22426
英摘: This study raises and addresses the problem of time-delayed feedback in learning in games. Because learning in games assumes that multiple agents independently learn their strategies, a discrepancy in optimization often emerges among the agents. To overcome this discrepancy, the prediction of the future reward is incorporated into algorithms, typically known as Optimistic Follow-the-Regularized-Leader (OFTRL). However, the time delay in observing the past rewards hinders the prediction. Indeed, this study firstly proves that even a single-step delay worsens the performance of OFTRL from the aspects of social regret and convergence. This study proposes the weighted OFTRL (WOFTRL), where the prediction vector of the next reward in OFTRL is weighted $n$ times. We further capture an intuition that the optimistic weight cancels out this time delay. We prove that when the optimistic weight exceeds the time delay, our WOFTRL recovers the good performances that social regret is constant in general-sum normal-form games, and the strategies last-iterate converge to the Nash equilibrium in poly-matrix zero-sum games. The theoretical results are supported and strengthened by our experiments.
中摘: 这项研究提出了游戏学习中的时间延迟反馈问题，并对此进行了探讨。因为在游戏中假设多个代理独立地学习自己的策略，所以代理之间的优化差异经常会出现。为了克服这种差异，通常会在算法中加入对未来奖励的预测，这被称为乐观的跟随正则化领导者（OFTRL）。然而，观察过去奖励的时间延迟会妨碍这种预测。事实上，这项研究首次证明，即使是一步时间延迟也会从社会后悔和社会收敛的角度恶化OFTRL的表现。该研究提出了一种加权的OFTRL（WOFTRL），其中在OFTRL中用于预测下一个奖励的向量被加权了n倍。我们进一步捕捉到了一种直觉，即乐观权重可以抵消这种时间延迟。我们证明，在一般和形式的游戏和社会后悔保持恒定时，当乐观权重超过时间延迟时，我们的WOFTRL能够恢复良好的表现；而在多项矩阵零和博弈中，策略的最后迭代将收敛到纳什均衡。理论结果得到了我们的实验的支持和加强。

[No.284] Future Link Prediction Without Memory or Aggregation
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 未来链接预测无需记忆或聚合
类别: deep learning
作者: Lu Yi;Runlin Lei;Fengran Mo;Yanping Zheng;Zhewei Wei;Yuhang Ye;
机构: Renmin University of China;Université de Montréal;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=kHDUCUqPEc
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19408
英摘: Future link prediction on temporal graphs is a fundamental task with wide applicability in real-world dynamic systems. These scenarios often involve both recurring (seen) and novel (unseen) interactions, requiring models to generalize effectively across both types of edges. However, existing methods typically rely on complex memory and aggregation modules, yet struggle to handle unseen edges. In this paper, we revisit the architecture of existing temporal graph models and identify two essential but overlooked modeling requirements for future link prediction: representing nodes with unique identifiers and performing target-aware matching between source and destination nodes. To this end, we propose Cross-Attention based Future Link Predictor on Temporal Graphs (CRAFT), a simple yet effective architecture that discards memory and aggregation modules and instead builds on two components: learnable node embeddings and cross-attention between the destination and the source's recent interactions. This design provides strong expressive power and enables target-aware modeling of the compatibility between candidate destinations and the source's interaction patterns. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets demonstrate that CRAFT consistently achieves superior performance with high efficiency, making it well-suited for large-scale real-world applications.
中摘: 未来时间图上的链接预测是一项在现实动态系统中有广泛应用的基础任务。这些场景通常涉及重复（已见过）和新颖（未见过）的交互，要求模型能够在处理这两种类型的边时有效泛化。然而，现有方法通常依赖于复杂的记忆和聚合模块，但在处理未见过的边时却表现不佳。在这篇论文中，我们重新审视了现有时间图模型的架构，并识别出未来链接预测中两个重要的但被忽视的建模需求：使用唯一的节点标识符表示节点以及在源节点和目标节点之间进行目标感知匹配。为此，我们提出了基于交叉注意力的时间图未来链接预测器（CRAFT），这是一种简单而有效的架构，摒弃了记忆和聚合模块，而是基于可学习的节点嵌入和目标与源节点最近交互之间的交叉注意力设计。这种设计提供了强大的表达能力，并能够实现对候选目标节点与源节点交互模式兼容性的目标感知建模。在多种数据集上的广泛实验表明，CRAFT在保持高效性的同时，始终能够获得更优的性能，使其非常适合大规模的实际应用。

[No.285] OCN: Effectively Utilizing Higher-Order Common Neighbors for Better Link Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: OCN：有效利用高阶公共邻居以提高链接预测效果
类别: deep learning
作者: Juntong Wang;Xiyuan Wang;Muhan Zhang;
机构: Peking University;Beijing Jiao Tong University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=E3bMQCKgiX
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19719
英摘: Common Neighbors (CNs) and their higher-order variants are important pairwise features widely used in state-of-the-art link prediction methods. However, existing methods often struggle with the repetition across different orders of CNs and fail to fully leverage their potential. We identify that these limitations stem from two key issues: redundancy and over-smoothing in high-order common neighbors. To address these challenges, we design orthogonalization to eliminate redundancy between different-order CNs and normalization to mitigate over-smoothing. By combining these two techniques, we propose Orthogonal Common Neighbor (OCN), a novel approach that significantly outperforms the strongest baselines by an average of 7.7% on popular link prediction benchmarks. A thorough theoretical analysis is provided to support our method. Ablation studies also verify the effectiveness of our orthogonalization and normalization techniques.
中摘: 常见的邻居（CNs）及其高阶变体是广泛应用于先进链接预测方法中的重要成对特征。然而，现有的方法往往难以应对不同阶次CNs之间的重复问题，并未能充分利用它们的潜力。我们发现这些限制源于两个关键问题：不同阶次常见邻居之间的冗余以及高阶常见邻居中的过度平滑。为了解决这些问题，我们设计了正交化来消除不同阶次CNs之间的冗余，并引入了规范化来减轻过度平滑。通过结合这两种技术，我们提出了一种名为正交常见邻居（OCN）的新方法，在流行的链接预测基准测试中平均比最强的基线方法高出7.7%的表现。我们还提供了详尽的理论分析以支持我们的方法，并通过消融研究验证了我们的正交化和规范化技术的有效性。

[No.288] Sparse Diffusion Autoencoder for Test-time Adapting Prediction of Complex Systems
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于稀疏扩散自编码器的测试时复杂系统适应性预测
类别: machine learning for sciences
作者: Jingwen Cheng;Ruikun Li;Huandong Wang;Yong Li;
机构: Tsinghua University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=iWWPtwXfnO
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17459
英摘: Predicting the behavior of complex systems is critical in many scientific and engineering domains, and hinges on the model's ability to capture their underlying dynamics. Existing methods encode the intrinsic dynamics of high-dimensional observations through latent representations and predict autoregressively. However, these latent representations lose the inherent spatial structure of spatiotemporal dynamics, leading to the predictor's inability to effectively model spatial interactions and neglect emerging dynamics during long-term prediction. In this work, we propose SparseDiff, introducing a test-time adaptation strategy to dynamically update the encoding scheme to accommodate emergent spatiotemporal structures during the long-term evolution of the system. Specifically, we first design a codebook-based sparse encoder, which coarsens the continuous spatial domain into a sparse graph topology. Then, we employ a graph neural ordinary differential equation to model the dynamics and guide a diffusion decoder for reconstruction. SparseDiff autoregressively predicts the spatiotemporal evolution and adjust the sparse topological structure to adapt to emergent spatiotemporal patterns by adaptive re-encoding. Extensive evaluations on representative systems demonstrate that SparseDiff achieves an average prediction error reduction of 49.99\% compared to baselines, requiring only 1% of the spatial resolution.
中摘: 预测复杂系统的行为在许多科学和工程领域中至关重要，这依赖于模型捕捉其内在动态的能力。现有方法通过潜在表示来编码高维观察的固有动态，并进行自回归预测。然而，这些潜在表示会丢失时空动态的固有空间结构，导致预测器无法有效地建模空间交互，并在长期预测中忽略新兴动态。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种SparseDiff方法，引入了一种测试时适应策略，在系统长期演变过程中动态更新编码方案以容纳新兴的时空结构。具体来说，我们首先设计了一个基于码本的稀疏编码器，将连续的空间域粗化为稀疏图拓扑结构。然后，我们使用图神经普通微分方程来建模动态，并指导一个扩散解码器进行重构。SparseDiff 自回归地预测时空演化，并通过自适应重编码调整稀疏拓扑结构以适应新兴的时空模式。在代表性系统上的广泛评估表明，SparseDiff 相对于基线方法平均预测误差降低了 49.99%，仅需要 1% 的空间分辨率。

[No.289] Conformal Prediction for Causal Effects of Continuous Treatments
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 连续治疗因果效应的保距预测
类别: probabilistic methods
作者: Maresa Schröder;Dennis Frauen;Jonas Schweisthal;Konstantin Hess;Valentyn Melnychuk;Stefan Feuerriegel;
机构: ;Ludwig-Maximilians-Universität München;Ludwig Maximilian University of Munich;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=1nL84tQNnK
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03094
英摘: Uncertainty quantification of causal effects is crucial for safety-critical applications such as personalized medicine. A powerful approach for this is conformal prediction, which has several practical benefits due to model-agnostic finite-sample guarantees. Yet, existing methods for conformal prediction of causal effects are limited to binary/discrete treatments and make highly restrictive assumptions such as known propensity scores. In this work, we provide a novel conformal prediction method for potential outcomes of continuous treatments. We account for the additional uncertainty introduced through propensity estimation so that our conformal prediction intervals are valid even if the propensity score is unknown. Our contributions are three-fold: (1) We derive finite-sample prediction intervals for potential outcomes of continuous treatments. (2) We provide an algorithm for calculating the derived intervals. (3) We demonstrate the effectiveness of the conformal prediction intervals in experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to propose conformal prediction for continuous treatments when the propensity score is unknown and must be estimated from data.
中摘: 在安全关键应用如个性化医疗中，因果效应的不确定性量化至关重要。一种强大的方法是符合性预测，它由于具有基于样本量的模型无关保证而具备多个实用优势。然而，现有的因果效应符合性预测方法仅限于二元/离散治疗，并且需要进行高度限制性的假设，例如已知倾向得分。在这项工作中，我们提供了一种新颖的符合性预测方法，用于连续治疗的潜在结果。我们考虑了通过倾向估计引入的额外不确定性，使得我们的符合性预测区间即使在倾向得分未知的情况下仍然有效。我们的贡献包括三个方面：（1）我们推导出连续治疗潜在结果的有限样本预测区间。（2）我们提供了一个计算这些区间的方法的算法。（3）我们在合成和真实世界数据集上的实验中展示了符合性预测区间的有效性。据我们所知，这是首次提出在倾向得分未知且需从数据中估计时用于连续治疗的符合性预测方法。

[No.290] FlexVAR: Flexible Visual Autoregressive Modeling without Residual Prediction
来源: NeurIPS 2025
命中: prediction
标题: FlexVAR：灵活的视觉自回归建模无需残差预测
类别: deep learning
作者: Siyu Jiao;Gengwei Zhang;Yinlong Qian;Jiancheng Huang;Yao Zhao;Humphrey Shi;Lin Ma;Yunchao Wei;ZEQUN JIE;
机构: Beijing Jiao Tong University;University of Technology Sydney;Meituan;Chinese Academy of Sciences;;
类型: Poster
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=1qKvZX4qnp
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20313
英摘: This work challenges the residual prediction paradigm in visual autoregressive modeling and presents FlexVAR, a new Flexible Visual AutoRegressive image generation paradigm. FlexVAR facilitates autoregressive learning with ground-truth prediction, enabling each step to independently produce plausible images. This simple, intuitive approach swiftly learns visual distributions and makes the generation process more flexible and adaptable. Trained solely on low-resolution images ($\leq$ 256px), FlexVAR can: (1) Generate images of various resolutions and aspect ratios, even exceeding the resolution of the training images. (2) Support various image-to-image tasks, including image refinement, in/out-painting, and image expansion. (3) Adapt to various autoregressive steps, allowing for faster inference with fewer steps or enhancing image quality with more steps. Our 1.0B model outperforms its VAR counterpart on the ImageNet 256$\times$256 benchmark. Moreover, when zero-shot transfer the image generation process with 13 steps, the performance further improves to 2.08 FID, outperforming state-of-the-art autoregressive models AiM/VAR by 0.25/0.28 FID and popular diffusion models LDM/DiT by 1.52/0.19 FID, respectively. When transferring our 1.0B model to the ImageNet 512$\times$512 benchmark in a zero-shot manner, FlexVAR achieves competitive results compared to the VAR 2.3B model, which is a fully supervised model trained at 512$\times$512 resolution.
中摘: 这项工作挑战了视觉自回归建模中的残差预测范式，并提出了FlexVAR，一种新的灵活视觉自回归图像生成范式。FlexVAR 通过使用真实预测来促进自回归学习，使每一步都能够独立地生成可信的图像。这一简单直观的方法能够迅速学习视觉分布，并使生成过程更加灵活和适应性强。仅通过训练低分辨率图像（$\leq$ 256px），FlexVAR 可以：（1）生成各种分辨率和纵横比的图像，甚至可以超过训练图像的分辨率。（2）支持各种图像到图像的任务，包括图像细化、前景/背景补画以及图像扩展。（3）适应各种自回归步骤，允许更快的推理或通过更多步骤增强图像质量。我们的1.0B模型在ImageNet 256×256基准测试上优于其VAR对应模型。此外，在零样本迁移过程中，使用13步进行图像生成，性能进一步提升至2.08 FID，分别优于最先进的自回归模型AiM/VAR（FID差距为0.25/0.28）和流行的扩散模型LDM/DiT（FID差距为1.52/0.19）。当以零样本方式将我们的1.0B模型转移到ImageNet 512×512基准测试时，FlexVAR 在结果上与全监督训练的VAR 2.3B模型竞争，后者是在512×512分辨率下训练的。

[No.298] Optimizing Gene-Based Testing for Antibiotic Resistance Prediction
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于基因的测试以优化抗生素耐药性预测

作者: David Hagerman;Anna Johnning;Roman Naeem;Fredrik Kahl;Erik Kristiansson;Lennart Svensson;

类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/35021
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14919
英摘: Antibiotic Resistance (AR) is a critical global health challenge that necessitates the development of cost-effective, efficient, and accurate diagnostic tools. Given the genetic basis of AR, techniques such as Polymerase Chain Reaction (PCR) that target specific resistance genes offer a promising approach for predictive diagnostics using a limited set of key genes. This study introduces GenoARM, a novel framework that integrates reinforcement learning (RL) with transformer-based models to optimize the selection of PCR gene tests and improve AR predictions, leveraging observed metadata for improved accuracy. In our evaluation, we developed several high-performing baselines and compared them using publicly available datasets derived from real-world bacterial samples representing multiple clinically relevant pathogens. The results show that all evaluated methods achieve strong and reliable performance when metadata is not utilized. When metadata is introduced and the number of selected genes increases, GenoARM demonstrates superior performance due to its capacity to approximate rewards for unseen and sparse combinations. Overall, our framework represents a major advancement in optimizing diagnostic tools for AR in clinical settings.
中摘: 抗生素耐药性（AR）是一个关键的全球健康挑战，需要开发成本效益高、高效且准确的诊断工具。鉴于AR的遗传基础，靶向特定耐药基因的聚合酶链反应（PCR）技术为利用少量关键基因进行预测性诊断提供了有前景的方法。本研究引入了GenoARM这一新颖框架，该框架结合了强化学习（RL）与基于变换器的模型，以优化PCR基因测试的选择并提高AR预测的准确性，通过利用观察到的元数据来提升精度。在我们的评估中，我们开发了几种高性能的基础模型，并使用公开可用的真实世界细菌样本数据集（代表多种临床相关病原体）进行了比较。结果显示，在未利用元数据的情况下，所有评估方法均表现出强大的可靠性能。当引入元数据且选择的基因数量增加时，GenoARM展示了优越的性能，因为其能够近似计算未见过和稀疏组合的奖励。总体而言，我们的框架代表了在临床环境中优化AR诊断工具的一项重大进展。

[No.299] Physics-Guided Fair Graph Sampling for Water Temperature Prediction in River Networks
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于物理指导的公平图采样在河流网络中的水温预测
类别: ai for social impact track
作者: Erhu He;Declan Kutscher;Yiqun Xie;Jacob Zwart;Zhe Jiang;Huaxiu Yao;Xiaowei Jia;

英摘: This work introduces a novel graph neural networks (GNNs)-based method to predict stream water temperature and reduce model bias across locations of different income and education levels. Recently, there has been an increasing interest of using GNNs in modeling complex water dynamics in stream networks. Despite their promise in improving the accuracy, GNNs can bring additional model bias through the aggregation process, where node features are updated by aggregating neighboring nodes. The bias can be especially pronounced when nodes with similar sensitive attributes are frequently connected. We introduce a new method that leverages physical knowledge to represent the node influence in GNNs, and then utilizes physics-based influence to refine the selection and weights over the neighbors. The objective is to facilitate equitable treatment over different sensitive groups in the graph aggregation, which helps reduce spatial bias over locations, especially for those in underprivileged groups. 英摘: Urban socioeconomic indicator prediction aims to infer various metrics related to sustainable development in diverse urban landscapes using data-driven methods. However, prevalent pretrained models, particularly those reliant on satellite imagery, face dual challenges. Firstly, concentrating solely on macro-level patterns from satellite data may introduce bias, lacking nuanced details at micro levels, such as architectural details at a place. Secondly, the text generated by the precursor work UrbanCLIP, which fully utilizes the extensive knowledge of LLMs, frequently exhibits issues such as hallucination and homogenization, resulting in a lack of reliable quality. In response to these issues, we devise a novel framework entitled UrbanVLP based on Vision-Language Pretraining. Our UrbanVLP seamlessly integrates multi-granularity information from both macro (satellite) and micro (street-view) levels, overcoming the limitations of prior pretrained models. Moreover, it introduces automatic text generation and calibration, providing a robust guarantee for producing high-quality text descriptions of urban imagery. Rigorous experiments conducted across six socioeconomic indicator prediction tasks underscore its superior performance.
中摘: 基于视觉-语言预训练的UrbanVLP框架旨在利用数据驱动的方法推断各种与可持续发展相关的城市社会经济指标，在不同城市景观中进行预测。然而，现有的预训练模型，尤其是依赖卫星图像的模型，面临着双重挑战。首先，仅专注于卫星数据中的宏观模式可能会引入偏差，缺乏微观层面的细微差别，如某个地方的建筑细节。其次，UrbanCLIP前身工作生成的文本虽然充分利用了大规模语言模型（LLM）的知识，但经常出现幻觉和同质化的问题，导致文本质量不可靠。为应对这些问题，我们设计了一个新的框架UrbanVLP，该框架无缝地结合了来自宏观（卫星）和微观（街景）两个不同粒度级别的信息，克服了先前预训练模型的局限性。此外，UrbanVLP引入了自动文本生成和校准功能，为生成高质量的城市图像文本描述提供了坚实保障。在六个社会经济指标预测任务上的严格实验进一步证实了其优越性能。

[No.302] XTSFormer: Cross-Temporal-Scale Transformer for Irregular-Time Event Prediction in Clinical Applications
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: XTSFormer：临床应用中不规则时间事件预测的跨时空尺度变换器
类别: ai for social impact track
作者: Tingsong Xiao;Zelin Xu;Wenchong He;Zhengkun Xiao;Yupu Zhang;Zibo Liu;Shigang Chen;My T. Thai;Jiang Bian;Parisa Rashidi;Zhe Jiang;

类型: Technical

英摘: Adverse clinical events related to unsafe care are among the top ten causes of death in the U.S. Accurate modeling and prediction of clinical events from electronic health records (EHRs) play a crucial role in patient safety enhancement. An example is modeling de facto care pathways that characterize common step-by-step plans for treatment or care. However, clinical event data pose several unique challenges, including the irregularity of time intervals between consecutive events, the existence of cycles, periodicity, multi-scale event interactions, and the high computational costs associated with long event sequences. Existing neural temporal point processes (TPPs) methods do not effectively capture the multi-scale nature of event interactions, which is common in many real-world clinical applications. To address these issues, we propose the cross-temporal-scale transformer (XTSFormer), specifically designed for irregularly timed event data. Our model consists of two vital components: a novel Feature-based Cycle-aware Time Positional Encoding (FCPE) that adeptly captures the cyclical nature of time, and a hierarchical multi-scale temporal attention mechanism, where different temporal scales are determined by a bottom-up clustering approach. Extensive experiments on several real-world EHR datasets show that our XTSFormer outperforms multiple baseline methods. The code is available at https://github.com/spatialdatasciencegroup/XTSFormer.
中摘: 与不安全护理相关的不良临床事件是美国十大死亡原因之一。从电子健康记录（EHR）准确建模和预测临床事件在提高患者安全性方面发挥着关键作用。例如，可以建模实际存在的护理路径，以描述治疗或护理中的常见步骤计划。然而，临床事件数据面临着几个独特的挑战，包括连续事件之间的时间间隔不规律、存在周期性、循环性和多尺度事件交互，以及处理长期事件序列时的高计算成本。现有的神经临时点过程（TPP）方法未能有效捕捉事件交互的多尺度特性，这是许多实际临床应用中常见的。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了跨时间尺度变换器（XTSFormer），专门针对不规律时间事件数据。我们的模型包含两个关键组件：一种新颖的功能基周期感知时间位置编码（FCPE），能够巧妙地捕捉时间的周期性；以及一个分层多尺度时间注意力机制，其中不同的时间尺度通过自底向上的聚类方法确定。在多个真实世界的EHR数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的XTSFormer优于多种基准方法。代码可在https://github.com/spatialdatasciencegroup/XTSFormer获取。

英摘: Protein structure prediction is pivotal for understanding the structure-function relationship of proteins, advancing biological research, and facilitating pharmaceutical development and experimental design. While deep learning methods and the expanded availability of experimental 3D protein structures have accelerated structure prediction, the dynamic nature of protein structures has received limited attention. This study introduces an innovative 4D diffusion model incorporating molecular dynamics (MD) simulation data to learn dynamic protein structures. Our approach is distinguished by the following components: (1) a unified diffusion model capable of generating dynamic protein structures, including both the backbone and side chains, utilizing atomic grouping and side-chain dihedral angle predictions; (2) a reference network that enhances structural consistency by integrating the latent embeddings of the initial 3D protein structures; and (3) a motion alignment module aimed at improving temporal structural coherence across multiple time steps. To our knowledge, this is the first diffusion-based model aimed at predicting protein trajectories across multiple time steps simultaneously. Validation on benchmark datasets demonstrates that our model exhibits high accuracy in predicting dynamic 3D structures of proteins containing up to 256 amino acids over 32 time steps, effectively capturing both local flexibility in stable states and significant conformational changes. CoPRA reaches state-of-the-art performance on all the datasets. [No.307] Efficient Traffic Prediction Through Spatio-Temporal Distillation
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 通过时空提炼实现高效的交通预测
类别: application domains
作者: Qianru Zhang;Xinyi Gao;Haixin Wang;Siu Ming Yiu;Hongzhi Yin;
机构: University of Hong Kong;University of Queensland;University of California, Los Angeles;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10459
英摘: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have gained considerable attention in recent years for traffic flow prediction due to their ability to learn spatio-temporal pattern representations through a graph-based message-passing framework. Although GNNs have shown great promise in handling traffic datasets, their deployment in real-life applications has been hindered by scalability constraints arising from high-order message passing. Additionally, the over-smoothing problem of GNNs may lead to indistinguishable region representations as the number of layers increases, resulting in performance degradation. To address these challenges, we propose a new knowledge distillation paradigm termed LightST that transfers spatial and temporal knowledge from a high-capacity teacher to a lightweight student. Specifically, we introduce a spatio-temporal knowledge distillation framework that helps student MLPs capture graph-structured global spatio-temporal patterns while alleviating the over-smoothing effect with adaptive knowledge distillation. Extensive experiments verify that LightST significantly speeds up traffic flow predictions by 5X to 40X compared to state-of-the-art spatio-temporal GNNs, all while maintaining superior accuracy.
中摘: 图神经网络（GNNs）近年来在交通流预测领域引起了广泛关注，这是因为它们能够通过基于图的消息传递框架学习空间-时间模式表示。尽管GNNs在处理交通数据集方面展现了巨大的潜力，但在实际应用中的部署却受到了高阶消息传递带来的可扩展性限制的阻碍。此外，GNNs的过度平滑问题可能会导致随着层数增加，区域表示变得难以区分，从而导致性能下降。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种新的知识蒸馏范式，称为LightST，该范式将高容量教师的知识转移到轻量级学生模型上。具体而言，我们引入了一种空间-时间知识蒸馏框架，帮助学生MLPs捕捉到图结构的全局空间-时间模式，并通过自适应知识蒸馏缓解过度平滑效应。大量实验验证了与最先进的时空GNN相比，LightST可以将交通流预测速度显著提升5至40倍，同时保持更高的准确性。

英摘: As a fundamental method in economics and finance, the factor model has been extensively utilized in quantitative investment. In recent years, there has been a paradigm shift from traditional linear models with expert-designed factors to more flexible nonlinear machine learning-based models with data-driven factors, aiming to enhance the effectiveness of these factor models. However, due to the low signal-to-noise ratio in market data, mining effective factors in data-driven models remains challenging. In this work, we propose a hypergraph-based factor model with temporal residual contrastive learning (FactorGCL) that employs a hypergraph structure to better capture high-order nonlinear relationships among stock returns and factors. To mine hidden factors that supplement human-designed prior factors for predicting stock returns, we design a cascading residual hypergraph architecture, in which the hidden factors are extracted from the residual information after removing the influence of prior factors. Additionally, we propose a temporal residual contrastive learning method to guide the extraction of effective and comprehensive hidden factors by contrasting stock-specific residual information over different time periods. Our extensive experiments on real stock market data demonstrate that FactorGCL not only outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods but also mines effective hidden factors for predicting stock returns.
中摘: 作为经济学和金融学的基本方法，因子模型在量化投资中得到了广泛的应用。近年来，因子模型从传统的由专家设计的线性模型转向了更具灵活性的数据驱动的非线性机器学习模型，旨在增强这些因子模型的有效性。然而，由于市场数据中的低信噪比，挖掘有效的数据驱动因子仍然具有挑战性。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种基于超图的时间残差对比学习因子模型（FactorGCL），利用超图结构更好地捕捉股票回报与因子之间的高阶非线性关系。为了挖掘补充人类设计先验因子以预测股票回报的隐藏因子，我们设计了一个级联残差超图架构，在该架构中，隐藏因子是从移除先验因子影响后的残差信息中提取出来的。此外，我们提出了时间残差对比学习方法，通过对比不同时间段的股票特定残差信息来指导有效且全面的隐藏因子的提取。我们在实际股票市场数据上的广泛实验表明，FactorGCL 不仅优于现有的先进方法，还能挖掘出有效的隐藏因子以预测股票回报。

[No.310] GoBERT: Gene Ontology Graph Informed BERT for Universal Gene Function Prediction

英摘: Exploring the functions of genes and gene products is crucial to a wide range of fields, including medical research, evolutionary biology, and environmental science. Existing methods of automatic function annotation or prediction mainly focus on protein function prediction with sequence, 3D-structures or protein family information. In this study, we propose to tackle the gene function prediction problem by exploring Gene Ontology graph and annotation with BERT (GoBERT) to decipher the underlying relationships among gene functions. 中摘: 探索基因和基因产物的功能对于医学研究、进化生物学和环境科学等多个领域至关重要。然而，发现新的功能主要依赖于昂贵且耗时的湿实验。现有的自动功能注释或预测方法主要集中在基于序列、三维结构或蛋白质家族信息的蛋白质功能预测上。在本研究中，我们提出通过探索Gene Ontology图和注释来利用BERT（GoBERT）来解析基因功能之间的潜在关系。我们提出的新型功能预测任务将现有功能作为输入，并将功能预测扩展到基因和基因产物。具体来说，设计了两个预训练任务以联合训练GoBERT，捕捉功能的显性和隐性关系。邻域预测是一个自我监督的多标签分类任务，用于捕获显性功能关系。指定的掩码和恢复任务帮助GoBERT找到功能之间的隐性模式。预训练的GoBERT能够根据已知的功能注释预测各种基因和基因产物的新功能。进行了广泛的实验、生物案例研究和消融研究，以证明我们提出的GoBERT的优势。

来源: AAAI 2025

[No.321] M3Net: Multimodal Multi-task Learning for 3D Detection, Segmentation, and Occupancy Prediction in Autonomous Driving
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: M3Net：自动驾驶中三维检测、分割和占用预测的多模态多任务学习
类别: computer vision i
作者: Xuesong Chen;Shaoshuai Shi;Tao Ma;Jingqiu Zhou;Simon See;Ka Chun Cheung;Hongsheng Li;
机构: Chinese University of Hong Kong;NVIDIA;Centre for Perceptual and Interactive Intelligence;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32227
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18100
英摘: The perception system for autonomous driving generally requires to handle multiple diverse sub-tasks. However, current algorithms typically tackle individual sub-tasks separately, which leads to low efficiency when aiming at obtaining full-perception results. Some multi-task learning methods try to unify multiple tasks with one model, but do not solve the conflicts in multi-task learning. In this paper, we introduce M3Net, a novel multimodal and multi-task network that simultaneously tackles detection, segmentation, and 3D occupancy prediction for autonomous driving and achieves superior performance than single task model. M3Net takes multimodal data as input and multiple tasks via query-token interactions. To enhance the integration of multi-modal features for multi-task learning, we first propose the Modality-Adaptive Feature Integration (MAFI) module, which enables single-modality features to predict channel-wise attention weights for their high-performing tasks, respectively. Based on integrated features, we then develop task-specific query initialization strategies to accommodate the needs of detection/segmentation and 3D occupancy prediction. Leveraging the properly initialized queries, a shared decoder transforms queries and BEV features layer-wise, facilitating multi-task learning. Furthermore, we propose a Task-oriented Channel Scaling (TCS) module in the decoder to mitigate conflicts between optimizing for different tasks. Additionally, our proposed multi-task querying and TCS module support both Transformer-based decoder and Mamba-based decoder, demonstrating its flexibility to different architectures. M3Net achieves state-of-the-art multi-task learning performance on the nuScenes benchmarks.
中摘: 自动驾驶感知系统通常需要处理多个多样化的子任务。然而，当前的算法通常分别处理这些子任务，这在追求全面感知结果时导致效率低下。一些多任务学习方法试图用一个模型统一处理多个任务，但并未解决多任务学习中的冲突问题。在本文中，我们介绍了一种名为M3Net的新颖的多模态和多任务网络，它可以同时处理自动驾驶中的检测、分割和3D占用率预测，并且其性能优于单一任务模型。M3Net以多模态数据作为输入，并通过查询-令牌交互机制处理多种任务。为了增强多模态特征在多任务学习中的整合，我们首先提出了模态自适应特征整合（MAFI）模块，该模块使单模态特征能够为各自的高性能任务预测通道级别的注意力权重。基于整合后的特征，我们随后开发了针对特定任务的查询初始化策略，以满足检测/分割和3D占用率预测的需求。利用正确初始化的查询，共享解码器逐层转换查询和BEV特征，促进多任务学习。此外，我们在解码器中提出了一种面向任务的通道缩放（TCS）模块，以缓解优化不同任务之间的冲突。我们的多任务查询和TCS模块支持基于Transformer的解码器和Mamba基于的解码器，展示了其对不同架构的灵活性。M3Net在nuScenes基准测试中实现了最先进的多任务学习性能。

[No.326] ViPOcc: Leveraging Visual Priors from Vision Foundation Models for Single-View 3D Occupancy Prediction
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: ViPOcc：利用视觉先验从视觉基础模型进行单视图三维占用预测
类别: computer vision ii
作者: Yi Feng;Yu Han;Xijing Zhang;Tanghui Li;Yanting Zhang;Rui Fan;
机构: Tongji University;Donghua University;Xi'an Jiao Tong University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32308
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11210
英摘: Inferring the 3D structure of a scene from a single image is an ill-posed and challenging problem in the field of vision-centric autonomous driving. Existing methods usually employ neural radiance fields to produce voxelized 3D occupancy, lacking instance-level semantic reasoning and temporal photometric consistency. In this paper, we propose ViPOcc, which leverages the visual priors from vision foundation models (VFMs) for fine-grained 3D occupancy prediction. Unlike previous works that solely employ volume rendering for RGB and depth image reconstruction, we introduce a metric depth estimation branch, in which an inverse depth alignment module is proposed to bridge the domain gap in depth distribution between VFM predictions and the ground truth. The recovered metric depth is then utilized in temporal photometric alignment and spatial geometric alignment to ensure accurate and consistent 3D occupancy prediction. Additionally, we also propose a semantic-guided non-overlapping Gaussian mixture sampler for efficient, instance-aware ray sampling, which addresses the redundant and imbalanced sampling issue that still exists in previous state-of-the-art methods. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of ViPOcc in both 3D occupancy prediction and depth estimation tasks on the KITTI-360 and KITTI Raw datasets. Our code is available at: \url{https://mias.group/ViPOcc}.
中摘: 从单张图像推断场景的三维结构在基于视觉的自主驾驶领域是一个病态且具有挑战性的问题。现有的方法通常使用神经辐射场来生成体素化的三维占用信息，但缺乏实例级别的语义推理和时间光度一致性。在本文中，我们提出了一种ViPOcc方法，该方法利用视觉基础模型（VFMs）的视觉先验进行精细的三维占用预测。与以往仅依赖体渲染重建RGB和深度图像的方法不同，我们引入了一个度量深度估计分支，在其中提出了一个逆深度对齐模块，以弥合VFMs预测和真实值在深度分布上的域差距。恢复的度量深度随后用于时间光度对齐和空间几何对齐，以确保准确且一致的三维占用预测。此外，我们还提出了一种语义引导的非重叠高斯混合采样器，用于高效的、实例感知的光线采样，解决了之前最先进的方法中存在的冗余和样本不平衡问题。广泛的实验表明，ViPOcc在KITTI-360和KITTI Raw数据集的三维占用预测和深度估计任务上均表现出优越的性能。我们的代码可在以下链接获取：https://mias.group/ViPOcc。

[No.327] Mind the Uncertainty in Human Disagreement: Evaluating Discrepancies Between Model Predictions and Human Responses in VQA
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 注意人类分歧中的不确定性：评估VQA中模型预测与人类响应之间的差异
类别: computer vision iii
作者: Jian Lan;Diego Frassinelli;Barbara Plank;
机构: Ludwig Maximilian University of Munich;Munich Center for Machine Learning;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32468
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02773
英摘: Large vision-language models frequently struggle to accurately predict responses provided by multiple human annotators, particularly when those responses exhibit human uncertainty. In this study, we focus on the Visual Question Answering (VQA) task, and we comprehensively evaluate how well the state-of-the-art vision-language models correlate with the distribution of human responses. To do so, we categorize our samples based on their levels (low, medium, high) of human uncertainty in disagreement (HUD) and employ not only accuracy but also three new human-correlated metrics in VQA, to investigate the impact of HUD. To better align models with humans, we also verify the effect of common calibration and human calibration. Our results show that even BEiT3, currently the best model for this task, struggles to capture the multi-label distribution inherent in diverse human responses. Additionally, we observe that the commonly used accuracy-oriented calibration technique adversely affects BEiT3's ability to capture HUD, further widening the gap between model predictions and human distributions. In contrast, we show the benefits of calibrating models towards human distributions for VQA, better aligning model confidence with human uncertainty. Our findings highlight that for VQA, the consistent alignment between human responses and model predictions is understudied and should become the next crucial target of future studies.
中摘: 大型的视觉-语言模型经常难以准确预测多名人类标注者的响应，尤其是在这些响应表现出人类不确定性时。在本研究中，我们专注于视觉问答（VQA）任务，并全面评估最先进的视觉-语言模型与人类响应分布的相关性。为此，我们将样本按照人类不确定性在分歧中的不同水平（低、中、高）进行分类，并不仅使用准确性，还采用三个新的与人类相关的VQA指标来探究人类不确定性分布（Human Uncertainty in Disagreement, HUD）的影响。为了更好地使模型与人类一致，我们还验证了常用校准技术和人类校准的效果。结果显示，即使目前BEiT3是该任务表现最好的模型，它也难以捕捉到多样化人类响应所固有的多标签分布。此外，我们观察到，常用的以准确性为导向的校准技术会进一步影响BEiT3捕捉HUD的能力，从而加大了模型预测与人类分布之间的差距。相比之下，我们展示了将模型校准向人类分布方向对于VQA的好处，这有助于更好地使模型的信心与人类的不确定性相匹配。我们的发现强调，在VQA任务中，人类响应和模型预测之间的一致对齐尚未得到充分研究，应成为未来研究的关键目标。

[No.328] Prediction-Feedback DETR for Temporal Action Detection
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 预测反馈DETR在时间动作检测中的应用
类别: computer vision iii
作者: Jihwan Kim;Miso Lee;Cheol-Ho Cho;Jihyun Lee;Jae-Pil Heo;
机构: Sungkyunkwan University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32448
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.16729
英摘: Temporal Action Detection (TAD) is fundamental yet challenging for real-world video applications. Leveraging the unique benefits of transformers, various DETR-based approaches have been adopted in TAD. However, it has recently been identified that the attention collapse in self-attention causes the performance degradation of DETR for TAD. Building upon previous research, this paper newly addresses the attention collapse problem in cross-attention within DETR-based TAD methods. Moreover, our findings reveal that cross-attention exhibits patterns distinct from predictions, indicating a short-cut phenomenon. To resolve this, we propose a new framework, Prediction-Feedback DETR (Pred-DETR), which utilizes predictions to restore the collapse and align the cross- and self-attention with predictions. Specifically, we devise novel prediction-feedback objectives using guidance from the relations of the predictions. As a result, Pred-DETR significantly alleviates the collapse and achieves state-of-the-art performance among DETR-based methods on various challenging benchmarks including THUMOS14, ActivityNet-v1.3, HACS, and FineAction.
中摘: 时空动作检测（TAD）对于实际视频应用来说既是基础的又是具有挑战性的。利用变压器的独特优势，各种DETR基于的方法已被应用于TAD。然而，最近的研究发现，自注意力中的注意力坍塌导致了DETR在TAD上的性能下降。在此基础上，本文新地解决了DETR基于的时空动作检测方法中交叉注意力的注意力坍塌问题。此外，我们的研究发现交叉注意力表现出与预测不同的模式，暗示了一种捷径现象。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种新的框架——预测反馈DETR（Pred-DETR），该框架利用预测来恢复坍塌，并使交叉注意力和自注意力与预测对齐。具体而言，我们设计了新颖的预测反馈目标，这些目标得到了预测关系的指导。因此，Pred-DETR显著缓解了注意力坍塌的问题，并在THUMOS14、ActivityNet-v1.3、HACS和FineAction等多个具有挑战性的基准测试上实现了DETR基于方法的最佳性能。

[No.329] ProtoOcc: Accurate, Efficient 3D Occupancy Prediction Using Dual Branch Encoder-Prototype Query Decoder
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: ProtoOcc: 基于双支编码器-原型查询解码器的准确高效三维占用预测
类别: computer vision iii
作者: Jungho Kim;Changwon Kang;Dongyoung Lee;Sehwan Choi;Jun Won Choi;
机构: Seoul National University;Hanyang University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32450
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08774
英摘: In this paper, we introduce ProtoOcc, a novel 3D occupancy prediction model designed to predict the occupancy states and semantic classes of 3D voxels through a deep semantic understanding of scenes. ProtoOcc consists of two main components: the Dual Branch Encoder (DBE) and the Prototype Query Decoder (PQD). The DBE produces a new 3D voxel representation by combining 3D voxel and BEV representations across multiple scales through a dual branch structure. This design enhances both performance and computational efficiency by providing a large receptive field for the BEV representation while maintaining a smaller receptive field for the voxel representation. The PQD introduces Prototype Queries to accelerate the decoding process. Scene-Adaptive Prototypes are derived from the 3D voxel features of input sample, while Scene-Agnostic Prototypes are computed by applying Scene-Adaptive Prototypes to an Exponential Moving Average during the training phase. By using these prototype-based queries for decoding, we can directly predict 3D occupancy in a single step, eliminating the need for iterative Transformer decoding. Additionally, we propose the Robust Prototype Learning, which injects noise into prototype generation process and trains the model to denoise during the training phase. ProtoOcc achieves state-of-the-art performance with 45.02% mIoU on the Occ3D-nuScenes benchmark. For single-frame method, it reaches 39.56% mIoU with an inference speed of 12.83 FPS on an NVIDIA RTX 3090. Our code can be found at https://github.com/SPA-junghokim/ProtoOcc.
中摘: 在本文中，我们介绍了ProtoOcc，这是一种新颖的3D占用状态预测模型，旨在通过深度场景语义理解来预测3D体素的占用状态和语义类别。ProtoOcc由两个主要组件组成：双分支编码器（DBE）和原型查询解码器（PQD）。DBE通过双分支结构结合多尺度的3D体素和BEV表示，产生一种新的3D体素表示形式。这种设计通过为BEV表示提供较大的感受野，同时保持较小的感受野来增强性能和计算效率。PQD引入了原型查询以加速解码过程。场景自适应原型是从输入样本的3D体素特征中派生出来的，而场景无关原型则是在训练阶段通过将场景自适应原型应用于指数移动平均值来计算得出的。通过使用这些基于原型的查询进行解码，我们可以直接在单一步骤中预测3D占用状态，从而消除迭代Transformer解码的需要。此外，我们提出了鲁棒的原型学习方法，在原型生成过程中注入噪声，并在训练阶段训练模型进行去噪。在Occ3D-nuScenes基准测试中，ProtoOcc实现了最先进的性能，mIoU为45.02%。对于单帧方法，它在NVIDIA RTX 3090上达到39.56%的mIoU，推理速度为12.83 FPS。我们的代码可以在https://github.com/SPA-junghokim/ProtoOcc找到。

[No.331] HVIS: A Human-like Vision and Inference System for Human Motion Prediction
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: HVIS：一个人类like的视觉和推理系统用于人类运动预测
类别: computer vision v
作者: Kedi Lyu;Haipeng Chen;Zhenguang Liu;Yifang Yin;Yukang Lin;Yingying Jiao;
机构: Jilin University;Zhejiang University;Hangzhou High-Tech Zone Institute of Blockchain and Data Security;Institute for Infocomm Research;Tsinghua University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32633
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16913
英摘: Grasping the intricacies of human motion, which involve perceiving spatio-temporal dependence and multi-scale effects, is essential for predicting human motion. While humans inherently possess the requisite skills to navigate this issue, it proves to be markedly more challenging for machines to emulate. To bridge the gap, we propose the Human-like Vision and Inference System (HVIS) for human motion prediction, which is designed to emulate human observation and forecast future movements. HVIS comprises two components: the human-like vision encode (HVE) module and the human-like motion inference (HMI) module. The HVE module mimics and refines the human visual process, incorporating a retina-analog component that captures spatiotemporal information separately to avoid unnecessary crosstalk. Additionally, a visual cortex-analogy component is designed to hierarchically extract and treat complex motion features, focusing on both global and local features of human poses. The HMI is employed to simulate the multi-stage learning model of the human brain. The spontaneous learning network simulates the neuronal fracture generation process for the adversarial generation of future motions. Subsequently, the deliberate learning network is optimized for hard-to-train joints to prevent misleading learning. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves new state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming existing methods by 19.8% on Human3.6M, 15.7% on CMU Mocap, and 11.1% on G3D.
中摘: 掌握人类运动的复杂性，包括感知时空依赖性和多尺度效应，对于预测人类运动至关重要。尽管人类天生具备应对这一问题所需的技能，但对于机器来说，模仿这些技能却显得更加困难。为了解决这一差距，我们提出了一种名为“类人视觉与推理系统”（HVIS）的人类运动预测方案，旨在模拟和重现人类观察并预测未来动作的过程。HVIS 包含两个组件：类人视觉编码（HVE）模块和类人运动推理（HMI）模块。HVE 模块模仿并细化了人类的视觉过程，其中包含一个视网膜模拟组件，专门用于分别捕获时空信息，以避免不必要的干扰。此外，还设计了一个类似视觉皮层的组件，用于分层次提取和处理复杂的运动特征，重点关注人类姿态的全局和局部特征。HMI 被用来模拟人类大脑的多阶段学习模型。自发学习网络模拟神经元断裂生成过程，以对抗生成未来动作。随后，刻意学习网络则针对难以训练的关键关节进行优化，以防止误导性的学习。实验结果表明，我们的方法在 Human3.6M、CMU Mocap 和 G3D 上均取得了新的最佳性能，分别比现有方法提高了 19.8%、15.7% 和 11.1%。

[No.332] CaRDiff: Video Salient Object Ranking Chain of Thought Reasoning for Saliency Prediction with Diffusion
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 卡迪夫：视频显著对象排名扩散链推理在显著性预测中的应用
类别: computer vision vi
作者: Yunlong Tang;Gen Zhan;Li Yang;Yiting Liao;Chenliang Xu;
机构: ByteDance;University of Rochester;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32785
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12009
英摘: Video saliency prediction aims to identify the regions in a video that attract human attention and gaze, driven by bottom-up features from the video and top-down processes like memory and cognition. Among these top-down influences, language plays a crucial role in guiding attention by shaping how visual information is interpreted. Existing methods primarily focus on modeling perceptual information while neglecting the reasoning process facilitated by language, where ranking cues are crucial outcomes of this process and practical guidance for saliency prediction. In this paper, we propose CaRDiff (Caption, Rank, and generate with Diffusion), a framework that imitates the process by integrating a multimodal large language model (MLLM), a grounding module, and a diffusion model, to enhance video saliency prediction. Specifically, we introduce a novel prompting method VSOR-CoT (Video Salient Object Ranking Chain of Thought), which utilizes an MLLM with a grounding module to caption video content and infer salient objects along with their rankings and positions. This process derives ranking maps that can be sufficiently leveraged by the diffusion model to decode the saliency maps for the given video accurately. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness of VSOR-CoT in improving the performance of video saliency prediction. The proposed CaRDiff performs better than state-of-the-art models on the MVS dataset and demonstrates cross-dataset capabilities on the DHF1k dataset through zero-shot evaluation.
中摘: 视频显著性预测旨在识别视频中吸引人类注意力和注视的区域，这由视频中的底上特征以及记忆和认知等顶下过程驱动。在这其中，语言在通过塑造视觉信息的解释方式来引导注意力方面扮演着至关重要的角色。现有方法主要集中在建模感知信息，而忽视了语言促进的推理过程，其中排名线索是这一过程的关键成果，并为显著性预测提供实际指导。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为CaRDiff（Caption、Rank和生成扩散模型）的框架，该框架通过整合多模态大语言模型（MLLM）、定位模块和扩散模型来增强视频显著性预测。具体来说，我们引入了一种新颖的提示方法VSOR-CoT（视频显著对象排名链式思维），该方法利用带有定位模块的大语言模型对视频内容进行标注，并推断显著对象及其排名和位置。这个过程会产生排名图，这些图可以充分被扩散模型利用，以准确解码给定视频的显著性图。广泛的实验表明，VSOR-CoT 在提高视频显著性预测性能方面非常有效。所提出的CaRDiff在MVS数据集上优于最先进的模型，并且通过零样本评估在DHF1k数据集上展示了跨数据集的能力。

[No.334] Discrete Prior-Based Temporal-Coherent Content Prediction for Blind Face Video Restoration
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于离散先验的时域相干内容预测盲脸视频恢复
类别: computer vision vii
作者: Lianxin Xie;Bingbing Zheng;Wen Xue;Yunfei Zhang;Le Jiang;Ruotao Xu;Si Wu;Hau-San Wong;
机构: South China University of Technology;Institute of Super Robotics;City University of Hong Kong;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32944
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.09960
英摘: Blind face video restoration aims to restore high-fidelity details from videos subjected to complex and unknown degradations. This task poses a significant challenge of managing temporal heterogeneity while at the same time maintaining stable face attributes. In this paper, we introduce a Discrete Prior-based Temporal-Coherent content prediction transformer to address the challenge, and our model is referred to as DP-TempCoh. Specifically, we incorporate a spatial-temporal-aware content prediction module to synthesize high-quality content from discrete visual priors, conditioned on degraded video tokens. To further enhance the temporal coherence of the predicted content, a motion statistics modulation module is designed to adjust the content, based on discrete motion priors in terms of cross-frame mean and variance. As a result, the statistics of the predicted content can match with that of real videos over time. By performing extensive experiments, we verify the effectiveness of the design elements and demonstrate the superior performance of our DP-TempCoh in both synthetically and naturally degraded video restoration.
中摘: 盲脸视频恢复旨在从受到复杂且未知退化影响的视频中恢复高保真细节。这一任务面临着管理时间异质性的同时保持面部属性稳定性的显著挑战。在本文中，我们介绍了一种基于离散先验的时间一致内容预测变换器（Discrete Prior-based Temporal-Coherent content prediction transformer）来应对这一挑战，并将我们的模型称为DP-TempCoh。具体而言，我们引入了一个时空感知的内容预测模块，该模块根据退化视频标记合成了高质量的内容，同时利用离散视觉先验。为了进一步增强预测内容的时间一致性，我们设计了一个运动统计调节模块，根据跨帧均值和方差的离散运动先验调整内容。因此，预测内容的统计特性可以与真实视频的时间统计特性相匹配。通过进行广泛的实验，我们验证了设计元素的有效性，并展示了在合成退化和自然退化视频恢复方面的优越性能。

[No.337] CasFT: Future Trend Modeling for Information Popularity Prediction with Dynamic Cues-Driven Diffusion Models
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: CasFT：基于动态线索驱动扩散模型的信息流行度预测未来趋势建模
类别: data mining knowledge management i
作者: Xin Jing;Yichen Jing;Yuhuan Lu;Bangchao Deng;Xueqin Chen;Dingqi Yang;
机构: University of Macau;Kash Institute of Electronics and Information Industry;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33296
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16619
英摘: The rapid spread of diverse information on online social platforms has prompted both academia and industry to realize the importance of predicting content popularity, which could benefit a wide range of applications, such as recommendation systems and strategic decision-making. Recent works mainly focused on extracting spatiotemporal patterns inherent in the information diffusion process within a given observation period so as to predict its popularity over a future period of time. However, these works often overlook the future popularity trend, as future popularity could either increase exponentially or stagnate, introducing uncertainties to the prediction performance. Additionally, how to transfer the preceding-term dynamics learned from the observed diffusion process into future-term trends remains an unexplored challenge. Against this background, we propose CasFT, which leverages observed information Cascades and dynamic cues extracted via neural ODEs as conditions to guide the generation of Future popularity-increasing Trends through a diffusion model. These generated trends are then combined with the spatiotemporal patterns in the observed information cascade to make the final popularity prediction. Extensive experiments conducted on three real-world datasets demonstrate that CasFT significantly improves the prediction accuracy, compared to state-of-the-art approaches, yielding 2.2%-19.3% improvement across different datasets.
中摘: 在线社交平台上多样化信息的快速传播促使学术界和工业界意识到预测内容流行度的重要性，这可以惠及推荐系统、战略决策等多种应用。最近的研究主要集中在提取给定观察期内信息扩散过程中的时空模式，以便预测未来时期的流行度。然而，这些研究往往忽视了未来的流行度趋势，因为未来的流行度可能呈指数增长或停滞不前，从而引入了预测性能的不确定性。此外，如何将观察到的扩散过程中学到的前期动态转移应用于未来的趋势仍是一个未解决的挑战。在此背景下，我们提出了一种名为CasFT的方法，该方法利用观察到的信息级联和通过神经ODE提取的动力学线索作为条件，通过扩散模型指导未来流行度增加趋势的生成。生成的趋势随后与观察到的信息级联中的时空模式结合，以进行最终的流行度预测。在三个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，CasFT显著提高了预测准确性，相比于最先进的方法，在不同数据集上分别获得了2.2%-19.3%的改进。

[No.340] Entire-Space Variational Information Exploitation for Post-Click Conversion Rate Prediction
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 全空间变分信息利用以预测点击后转换率
类别: data mining knowledge management i
作者: Ke Fei;Xinyue Zhang;Jingjing Li;
机构: University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33268
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15687
英摘: In recommender systems, post-click conversion rate (CVR) estimation is an essential task to model user preferences for items and estimate the value of recommendations. Sample selection bias (SSB) and data sparsity (DS) are two persistent challenges for post-click conversion rate (CVR) estimation. Currently, entire-space approaches that exploit unclicked samples through knowledge distillation are promising to mitigate SSB and DS simultaneously. Existing methods use non-conversion, conversion, or adaptive conversion predictors to generate pseudo labels for unclicked samples. However, they fail to consider the unbiasedness and information limitations of these pseudo labels. Motivated by such analysis, we propose an entire-space variational information exploitation framework (EVI) for CVR prediction. First, EVI uses a conditional entire-space CVR teacher to generate unbiased pseudo labels. Then, it applies variational information exploitation and logit distillation to transfer non-click space information to the target CVR estimator. We conduct extensive offline experiments on six large-scale datasets. EVI demonstrated a 2.25\% average improvement compared to the state-of-the-art baselines.
中摘: 在推荐系统中，点击后转化率（CVR）估计是建模用户项目偏好的一个关键任务，同时也是评估推荐价值的重要手段。点击后转化率估计面临着两个持久性的挑战：样本选择偏差（SSB）和数据稀疏性（DS）。目前，通过知识蒸馏利用未点击样本的全部空间方法对于同时缓解SSB和DS显示出有前景的效果。现有方法使用非转化、转化或自适应转化预测器为未点击样本生成伪标签。然而，这些方法未能考虑到伪标签的无偏差性和信息限制。受此分析的启发，我们提出了一种用于CVR预测的全部空间变分信息利用框架（EVI）。首先，EVI使用条件下的全部空间CVR教师来生成无偏的伪标签。然后，它应用变分信息利用和logit蒸馏将非点击空间的信息转移到目标CVR估算器上。我们在六个大规模数据集上进行了广泛的离线实验。与最先进的基线方法相比，EVI展示了2.25%的平均改进效果。

[No.341] GeoPro-Net: Learning Interpretable Spatiotemporal Prediction Models Through Statistically-Guided Geo-Prototyping
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: GeoPro-Net：通过统计指导的地理事物原型化学习可解释的空间时间预测模型
类别: data mining knowledge management i
作者: Bang An;Xun Zhou;Zirui Zhou;Ronilo Ragodos;Zenglin Xu;Jun Luo;
机构: Georgia Institute of Technology;Harbin Institute of Technology;University of Iowa;Fudan University;Logistics and Supply Chain MultiTech R&D Centre;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15353
英摘: The problem of forecasting spatiotemporal events such as crimes and accidents is crucial to public safety and city management. Besides accuracy, interpretability is also a key requirement for spatiotemporal forecasting models to justify the decisions. Interpretation of the spatiotemporal forecasting mechanism is, however, challenging due to the complexity of multi-source spatiotemporal features, the non-intuitive nature of spatiotemporal patterns for non-expert users, and the presence of spatial heterogeneity in the data. Currently, no existing deep learning model intrinsically interprets the complex predictive process learned from multi-source spatiotemporal features. To bridge the gap, we propose GeoPro-Net, an intrinsically interpretable spatiotemporal model for spatiotemporal event forecasting problems. GeoPro-Net introduces a novel Geo-concept convolution operation, which employs statistical tests to extract predictive patterns in the input as Geo-concepts, and condenses the Geo-concept-encoded input through interpretable channel fusion and geographic-based pooling. In addition, GeoPro-Net learns different sets of prototypes of concepts inherently, and projects them to real-world cases for interpretation. Comprehensive experiments and case studies on four real-world datasets demonstrate that GeoPro-Net provides better interpretability while still achieving competitive prediction performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines.
中摘: 时空事件（如犯罪和事故）的预测问题对于公共安全和城市管理至关重要。除了准确性之外，解释性也是时空预测模型的关键要求，以便为决策提供依据。然而，由于多源时空特征的复杂性、非直观的时空模式对非专家用户的挑战性以及数据中的时空异质性，解释时空预测机制是具有挑战性的。目前，没有现有的深度学习模型能够内在地解释从多源时空特征中学到的复杂预测过程。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了GeoPro-Net，这是一种用于时空事件预测问题的内在可解释时空模型。GeoPro-Net 引入了一种新颖的Geo概念卷积操作，该操作利用统计测试从输入中提取预测模式作为Geo概念，并通过可解释的通道融合和基于地理的聚合来压缩Geo概念编码的输入。此外，GeoPro-Net 内在地学习不同概念的不同原型集，并将它们投影到现实世界案例中以进行解释。在四个真实世界的数据集上的全面实验和案例研究表明，GeoPro-Net 在提供更好的解释性的同时，仍然能够实现与最先进的基线模型相当的预测性能。

[No.342] Identifying Predictions That Influence the Future: Detecting Performative Concept Drift in Data Streams
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 识别影响未来的预测：检测数据流中的表演性概念漂移
类别: data mining knowledge management i
作者: Brandon Gower-Winter;Georg Krempl;Sergey Dragomiretskiy;Tineke Jelsma;Arno Siebes;
机构: Utrecht University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33276
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10545
英摘: Concept Drift has been extensively studied within the context of Stream Learning. However, it is often assumed that the deployed model's predictions play no role in the concept drift the system experiences. Closer inspection reveals that this is not always the case. Automated trading might be prone to self-fulfilling feedback loops. Likewise, malicious entities might adapt to evade detectors in the adversarial setting resulting in a self-negating feedback loop that requires the deployed models to constantly retrain. Such settings where a model may induce concept drift are called performative. In this work, we investigate this phenomenon. Our contributions are as follows: First, we define performative drift within a stream learning setting and distinguish it from other causes of drift. We introduce a novel type of drift detection task, aimed at identifying potential performative concept drift in data streams. We propose a first such performative drift detection approach, called CheckerBoard Performative Drift Detection (CB-PDD). We apply CB-PDD to both synthetic and semi-synthetic datasets that exhibit varying degrees of self-fulfilling feedback loops. Results are positive with CB-PDD showing high efficacy, low false detection rates, resilience to intrinsic drift, comparability to other drift detection techniques, and an ability to effectively detect performative drift in semi-synthetic datasets. Secondly, we highlight the role intrinsic (traditional) drift plays in obfuscating performative drift and discuss the implications of these findings as well as the limitations of CB-PDD.
中摘: 概念漂移在流学习的背景下已经被广泛研究。然而，通常假设部署模型的预测对系统经历的概念漂移不起作用。更仔细地检查后发现，这并不总是正确的。自动化交易可能容易受到自我实现反馈循环的影响。同样，在对抗性设置中，恶意实体可能会适应以规避检测，从而导致一种自我否定的反馈循环，需要部署的模型不断重新训练。在这种模型可能引起概念漂移的设置中，被称为“表现性”的情况。在本项工作中，我们探讨了这一现象。我们的贡献如下：首先，我们在流学习环境中定义了表现性漂移，并将其与其他漂移原因区分开来。我们引入了一种新的漂移检测任务，旨在识别数据流中潜在的表现性概念漂移。我们提出了第一个此类表现性漂移检测方法，称为“棋盘表现性漂移检测”（CB-PDD）。我们将CB-PDD应用于展示不同程度自我实现反馈循环的合成和半合成数据集。结果显示，CB-PDD具有很高的有效性、较低的误检率、对固有漂移的抗干扰性、与其他漂移检测技术的可比性以及在半合成数据集中有效检测表现性漂移的能力。其次，我们强调了固有（传统）漂移在掩盖表现性漂移方面的作用，并讨论了这些发现的意义以及CB-PDD的局限性。

[No.346] Self-Explainable Graph Transformer for Link Sign Prediction
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 可自我解释的图变换器用于链接符号预测
类别: data mining knowledge management i
作者: Lu Li;Jiale Liu;Xingyu Ji;Maojun Wang;Zeyu Zhang;
机构: Huazhong Agricultural University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33316
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08754
英摘: Signed Graph Neural Networks (SGNNs) have been shown to be effective in analyzing complex patterns in real-world situations where positive and negative links coexist. However, SGNN models suffer from poor explainability, which limit their adoptions in critical scenarios that require understanding the rationale behind predictions. To the best of our knowledge, there is currently no research work on the explainability of the SGNN models. Our goal is to address the explainability of decision-making for the downstream task of link sign prediction specific to signed graph neural networks. Since post-hoc explanations are not derived directly from the models, they may be biased and misrepresent the true explanations. Therefore, in this paper we introduce a Self-Explainable Signed Graph transformer (SE-SGformer) framework, which can not only outputs explainable information while ensuring high prediction accuracy. Specifically, We propose a new Transformer architecture for signed graphs and theoretically demonstrate that using positional encoding based on signed random walks has greater expressive power than current SGNN methods and other positional encoding graph Transformer-based approaches. We constructs a novel explainable decision process by discovering the $K$-nearest (farthest) positive (negative) neighbors of a node to replace the neural network-based decoder for predicting edge signs. These $K$ positive (negative) neighbors represent crucial information about the formation of positive (negative) edges between nodes and thus can serve as important explanatory information in the decision-making process. We conducted experiments on several real-world datasets to validate the effectiveness of SE-SGformer, which outperforms the state-of-the-art methods by improving 2.2\% prediction accuracy and 73.1\% explainablity accuracy in the best-case scenario.
中摘: Signed Graph Neural Networks (SGNNs) 在分析现实世界中正负链接共存的情况下的复杂模式方面已被证明是有效的。然而，SGNN模型在解释性方面表现较差，这限制了它们在需要理解预测背后理由的关键场景中的应用。据我们所知，目前还没有关于SGNN模型解释性的研究工作。我们的目标是解决针对带符号图神经网络的链路符号预测下游任务的决策解释性问题。由于事后解释不是直接从模型中得出的，它们可能会带有偏见并歪曲真实的解释。因此，在本文中，我们引入了一个自解释带符号图变换器（SE-SGformer）框架，该框架不仅可以在保证高预测准确性的前提下输出可解释的信息。具体而言，我们提出了一种新的带符号图的Transformer架构，并理论上证明了基于带符号随机游走的位置编码比当前的SGNN方法和其他基于图形变换器的位置编码方法具有更强的表达能力。我们通过发现一个节点的最近（最远）正（负）邻居来构建一个新的可解释决策过程，以替代基于神经网络的解码器来预测边的符号。这些K个正（负）邻居包含了节点之间形成正（负）边的关键信息，因此可以作为决策过程中重要的解释性信息。我们在多个真实世界数据集上进行了实验，验证了SE-SGformer的有效性，在最佳情况下，其预测准确率提高了2.2%，解释性准确率提高了73.1%。

[No.349] Addressing Cold-Start Problem in Click-Through Rate Prediction via Supervised Diffusion Modeling
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 通过监督扩散建模解决点击率预测中的冷启动问题

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06270
英摘: Predicting Click-Through Rates is a crucial function within recommendation and advertising platforms, as the output of CTR prediction determines the order of items shown to users. The Embedding \& MLP paradigm has become a standard approach for industrial recommendation systems and has been widely deployed. However, this paradigm suffers from cold-start problems, where there is either no or only limited user action data available, leading to poorly learned ID embeddings. The cold-start problem hampers the performance of new items. To address this problem, we designed a novel diffusion model to generate a warmed-up embedding for new items. Specifically, we define a novel diffusion process between the ID embedding space and the side information space. In addition, we can derive a sub-sequence from the diffusion steps to expedite training, given that our diffusion model is non-Markovian. Our diffusion model is supervised by both the variational inference and binary cross-entropy objectives, enabling it to generate warmed-up embeddings for items in both the cold-start and warm-up phases. Additionally, we have conducted extensive experiments on three recommendation datasets. The results confirmed the effectiveness of our approach.

英摘: Accurately predicting the trajectory of vehicles is critically important for ensuring safety and reliability in autonomous driving. Although considerable research efforts have been made recently, the inherent trajectory uncertainty caused by various factors including the dynamic driving intends and the diverse driving scenarios still poses significant challenges to accurate trajectory prediction. To address this issue, we propose C2F-TP, a coarse-to-fine denoising framework for uncertainty-aware vehicle trajectory prediction. C2F-TP features an innovative two-stage coarse-to-fine prediction process. Specifically, in the spatial-temporal interaction stage, we propose a spatial-temporal interaction module to capture the inter-vehicle interactions and learn a multimodal trajectory distribution, from which a certain number of noisy trajectories are sampled. [No.351] GDiffRetro: Retrosynthesis Prediction with Dual Graph Enhanced Molecular Representation and Diffusion Generation
来源: AAAI 2025

标题: GDiffRetro：基于双图增强分子表示和扩散生成的逆合成预测
类别: data mining knowledge management ii
作者: Shengyin Sun;Wenhao Yu;Yuxiang Ren;Weitao Du;Liwei Liu;Xuecang Zhang;Ying Hu;Chen Ma;

英摘: Retrosynthesis prediction focuses on identifying reactants capable of synthesizing a target product. Typically, the retrosynthesis prediction involves two phases: Reaction Center Identification and Reactant Generation. However, we argue that most existing methods suffer from two limitations in the two phases: (i) Existing models do not adequately capture the ``face'' information in molecular graphs for the reaction center identification. (ii) Current approaches for the reactant generation predominantly use sequence generation in a 2D space, which lacks versatility in generating reasonable distributions for completed reactive groups and overlooks molecules' inherent 3D properties. To overcome the above limitations, we propose GDiffRetro. For the reaction center identification, GDiffRetro uniquely integrates the original graph with its corresponding dual graph to represent molecular structures, which helps guide the model to focus more on the faces in the graph. For the reactant generation, GDiffRetro employs a conditional diffusion model in 3D to further transform the obtained synthon into a complete reactant. Our experimental findings reveal that GDiffRetro outperforms state-of-the-art semi-template models across various evaluative metrics.
中摘: 逆合成分析预测专注于识别能够合成目标产物的反应物。通常，逆合成分析预测涉及两个阶段：反应中心识别和反应物生成。然而，我们认为大多数现有方法在这两个阶段中存在两个局限性：（i）现有的模型未能充分捕捉分子图中的“面”信息，以进行反应中心识别。（ii）当前的反应物生成方法主要在二维空间中使用序列生成方法，这在生成合理的完成反应基团分布时缺乏灵活性，并且忽略了分子固有的三维性质。为了克服上述局限性，我们提出了GDiffRetro。在反应中心识别方面，GDiffRetro独特地将原始图与其对应的双图结合起来表示分子结构，有助于引导模型更多地关注图中的面。在反应物生成方面，GDiffRetro采用三维条件扩散模型进一步将获得的合成子转化为完整的反应物。我们的实验结果表明，GDiffRetro在各种评估指标上优于最先进的半模板模型。

[No.354] Int2Planner: An Intention-based Multi-modal Motion Planner for Integrated Prediction and Planning
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于意图的多模态运动规划者：集成预测与规划
类别: intelligent robots
作者: Xiaolei Chen;Junchi Yan;Wenlong Liao;Tao He;Pai Peng;
机构: Shanghai Jiao Tong University;COWAROBOT Co. Ltd.;University of South China;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33595
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12799
英摘: Motion planning is a critical module in autonomous driving, with the primary challenge of uncertainty caused by interactions with other participants. As most previous methods treat prediction and planning as separate tasks, it is difficult to model these interactions. Furthermore, since the route path navigates ego vehicles to a predefined destination, it provides relatively stable intentions for ego vehicles and helps constrain uncertainty. On this basis, we construct Int2Planner, an \textbf{Int}ention-based \textbf{Int}egrated motion \textbf{Planner} achieves multi-modal planning and prediction. Instead of static intention points, Int2Planner utilizes route intention points for ego vehicles and generates corresponding planning trajectories for each intention point to facilitate multi-modal planning. The experiments on the private dataset and the public nuPlan benchmark show the effectiveness of route intention points, and Int2Planner achieves state-of-the-art performance. We also deploy it in real-world vehicles and have conducted autonomous driving for hundreds of kilometers in urban areas. It further verifies that Int2Planner can continuously interact with the traffic environment. Code will be avaliable at https://github.com/cxlz/Int2Planner.
中摘: 自主驾驶中的运动规划是一个关键模块，主要挑战来自于与其他参与者的交互不确定性。由于大多数先前的方法将预测和规划视为独立的任务，因此很难建模这些交互。此外，因为路线路径引导ego车辆到达预定义的目的地，它为ego车辆提供了相对稳定的意图，并有助于限制不确定性。在此基础上，我们构建了Int2Planner，这是一种基于意图的集成运动规划器，实现了多模式的规划和预测。不同于静态意图点，Int2Planner利用了路线意图点来生成ego车辆的相应规划轨迹，以促进多模式规划。在私有数据集和公开的nuPlan基准测试上的实验显示了路线意图点的有效性，且Int2Planner达到了最先进的性能。我们也将其部署在实际车辆上，并在城市地区进行了数百公里的自动驾驶测试，进一步验证了Int2Planner能够持续与交通环境互动。代码将在https://github.com/cxlz/Int2Planner上提供。

[No.357] CUQDS: Conformal Uncertainty Quantification Under Distribution Shift for Trajectory Prediction
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 同分布转移下的 conformal 确信区间量化及其在轨迹预测中的应用
类别: machine learning ii
作者: Huiqun Huang;Sihong He;Fei Miao;
机构: University of Connecticut;University of Texas at Arlington;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33915
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12100
英摘: Trajectory prediction models that can infer both finite future trajectories and their associated uncertainties of the target vehicles in an online setting (e.g., real-world application scenarios) is crucial for ensuring the safe and robust navigation and path planning of autonomous vehicle motion. However, the majority of existing trajectory prediction models have neither considered reducing the uncertainty as one objective during the training stage nor provided reliable uncertainty quantification during inference stage under potential distribution shift. Therefore, in this paper, we propose the Conformal Uncertainty Quantification under Distribution Shift framework, CUQDS, to quantify the uncertainty of the predicted trajectories of existing trajectory prediction models under potential data distribution shift, while considering improving the prediction accuracy of the models and reducing the estimated uncertainty during the training stage. Specifically, CUQDS includes 1) a learning-based Gaussian process regression module that models the output distribution of the base model (any existing trajectory prediction or time series forecasting neural networks) and reduces the estimated uncertainty by additional loss term, and 2) a statistical-based Conformal P control module to calibrate the estimated uncertainty from the Gaussian process regression module in an online setting under potential distribution shift between training and testing data.
中摘: 在线设置（例如现实世界的应用场景）下能够推断目标车辆的有限未来轨迹及其相关不确定性的时间预测模型对于确保自主车辆运动的安全和稳健导航至关重要。然而，现有的大多数时间预测模型在训练阶段并未将减少不确定性作为目标，同时在潜在数据分布偏移的情况下，在推理阶段也无法提供可靠的不确定性量化。因此，在本文中，我们提出了一种称为Conformal Uncertainty Quantification under Distribution Shift（CUQDS）的框架，以在潜在数据分布偏移的情况下量化现有时间预测模型预测轨迹的不确定性，同时考虑提高模型的预测精度并减少估计的不确定性。具体而言，CUQDS包括：1）基于学习的高斯过程回归模块，该模块建模基模型（任何现有的轨迹预测或时间序列预测神经网络）的输出分布，并通过附加的损失项减少估计的不确定性；2）基于统计的Conformal P控制模块，在训练数据和测试数据之间可能存在潜在分布偏移的情况下，在线校准来自高斯过程回归模块的估计不确定性。

[No.359] Accurate Link Prediction for Edge-Incomplete Graphs via PU Learning
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于PU学习的边缘不完全图准确链接预测
类别: machine learning iii
作者: Junghun Kim;Ka Hyun Park;Hoyoung Yoon;U Kang;
机构: Seoul National University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33966
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11911
英摘: Given an edge-incomplete graph, how can we accurately find the missing links? The link prediction in edge-incomplete graphs aims to discover the missing relations between entities when their relationships are represented as a graph. Edge-incomplete graphs are prevalent in real-world due to practical limitations, such as not checking all users when adding friends in a social network. Addressing the problem is crucial for various tasks, including recommending friends in social networks and finding references in citation networks. However, previous approaches rely heavily on the given edge-incomplete (observed) graph, making it challenging to consider the missing (unobserved) links during training. In this paper, we propose PULL (PU-Learning-based Link predictor), an accurate link prediction method based on the positive-unlabeled (PU) learning. PULL treats the observed edges in the training graph as positive examples, and the unconnected node pairs as unlabeled ones. PULL effectively prevents the link predictor from overfitting to the observed graph by proposing latent variables for every edge, and leveraging the expected graph structure with respect to the variables. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets show that PULL consistently outperforms the baselines for predicting links in edge-incomplete graphs.
中摘: 给定一个边不完整的图，我们如何准确找到缺失的链接？边不完整图中的链接预测旨在发现社交网络等场景中实体之间缺失的关系。由于实际限制，如社交网络中添加好友时并不检查所有用户，边不完整图在现实世界中非常普遍。解决这个问题对于推荐朋友、寻找引用网络中的参考等多种任务至关重要。然而，之前的方法主要依赖于给定的边不完整（观测到的）图，在训练过程中很难考虑缺失（未观测到的）链接。在本文中，我们提出了基于正未标记（PU）学习的PULL（PU-Learning-based Link predictor）方法，这是一种准确的链接预测方法。PULL将训练图中的观测到的边视为正例，将未连接的节点对视为未标记数据。通过为每条边引入潜在变量，并利用这些变量期望的图结构，PULL有效防止了链接预测器过度拟合到观测到的图。在五个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，PULL在预测边不完整图中的链接方面始终优于基线方法。

[No.360] Adaptive Sampling to Reduce Epistemic Uncertainty Using Prediction Interval-Generation Neural Networks
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 使用预测区间生成神经网络来适应性采样以减少认知不确定性
类别: machine learning iv
作者: Giorgio Morales;John W. Sheppard;
机构: Montana State University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34152
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10570
英摘: Obtaining high certainty in predictive models is crucial for making informed and trustworthy decisions in many scientific and engineering domains. However, extensive experimentation required for model accuracy can be both costly and time-consuming. This paper presents an adaptive sampling approach designed to reduce epistemic uncertainty in predictive models. Our primary contribution is the development of a metric that estimates potential epistemic uncertainty leveraging prediction interval-generation neural networks. This estimation relies on the distance between the predicted upper and lower bounds and the observed data at the tested positions and their neighboring points. Our second contribution is the proposal of a batch sampling strategy based on Gaussian processes (GPs). A GP is used as a surrogate model of the networks trained at each iteration of the adaptive sampling process. Using this GP, we design an acquisition function that selects a combination of sampling locations to maximize the reduction of epistemic uncertainty across the domain. We test our approach on three unidimensional synthetic problems and a multi-dimensional dataset based on an agricultural field for selecting experimental fertilizer rates. The results demonstrate that our method consistently converges faster to minimum epistemic uncertainty levels compared to Normalizing Flows Ensembles, MC-Dropout, and simple GPs.
中摘: 获得高确定性的预测模型对于许多科学和工程领域中的知情且可靠的决策至关重要。然而，为了提高模型精度所需的大量实验既昂贵又耗时。本文提出了一种自适应采样方法，旨在减少预测模型中的认识不确定性。我们的主要贡献是开发了一个利用预测区间生成神经网络来估算潜在认识不确定性的度量标准。这一估计依赖于测试位置及其邻近点的预测上限和下限与观测数据之间的距离。我们的第二个贡献是基于高斯过程（GPs）提出的批量采样策略。在每次自适应采样过程中，使用一个GPs作为训练网络的代理模型。通过这个GPs，我们设计了一个获取函数，选择一组采样位置以最大化整个领域内认识不确定性水平的降低。我们在三个一维合成问题和基于农业田地的多维数据集上测试了该方法，用于选择实验肥料率。结果显示，我们的方法在将认识不确定性水平降至最低方面比归一化流集成、MC-Dropout以及简单的GPs更快地收敛。

[No.361] Early Concept Drift Detection via Prediction Uncertainty
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 早期概念漂移检测通过预测不确定性
类别: machine learning iv
作者: Pengqian Lu;Jie Lu;Anjin Liu;Guangquan Zhang;
机构: University of Technology Sydney;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34105
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11158
英摘: Concept drift, characterized by unpredictable changes in data distribution over time, poses significant challenges to machine learning models in streaming data scenarios. Although error rate-based concept drift detectors are widely used, they often fail to identify drift in the early stages when the data distribution changes but error rates remain constant. This paper introduces the Prediction Uncertainty Index (PU-index), derived from the prediction uncertainty of the classifier, as a superior alternative to the error rate for drift detection. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that: (1) The PU-index can detect drift even when error rates remain stable. (2) Any change in the error rate will lead to a corresponding change in the PU-index. These properties make the PU-index a more sensitive and robust indicator for drift detection compared to existing methods. We also propose a PU-index-based Drift Detector (PUDD) that employs a novel Adaptive PU-index Bucketing algorithm for detecting drift. Empirical evaluations on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate PUDD's efficacy in detecting drift in structured and image data.
中摘: 概念漂移，表现为数据分布随时间出现的不可预测变化，在流式数据场景中对机器学习模型构成了重大挑战。尽管基于错误率的概念漂移检测器被广泛使用，但在数据分布发生变化而错误率保持恒定时，它们往往无法在早期阶段识别出漂移。本文引入了预测不确定性指数（PU指数），该指数源自分类器的预测不确定性，作为比错误率更优越的概念漂移检测替代方案。我们的理论分析表明：(1) PU指数即使在错误率保持稳定的情况下也能检测出漂移。(2) 任何错误率的变化都会导致PU指数相应地变化。这些特性使得PU指数相较于现有方法，成为一种更为敏感和稳健的概念漂移检测指标。我们还提出了一种基于PU指数的概念漂移检测器（PUDD），采用了一种新颖的自适应PU指数桶化算法进行漂移检测。在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实证评估证明了PUDD在检测结构化数据和图像数据中的漂移方面的有效性。

[No.362] Harnessing Event Sensory Data for Error Pattern Prediction in Vehicles: A Language Model Approach
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 利用事件感应数据进行车辆错误模式预测：一种语言模型方法
类别: machine learning iv
作者: Hugo Math;Rainer Lienhart;Robin Schön;
机构: Augsburg University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34138
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13041
英摘: In this paper, we draw an analogy between processing natural languages and processing multivariate event streams from vehicles in order to predict $\textit{when}$ and $\textit{what}$ error pattern is most likely to occur in the future for a given car. Our approach leverages the temporal dynamics and contextual relationships of our event data from a fleet of cars. Event data is composed of discrete values of error codes as well as continuous values such as time and mileage. Modelled by two causal Transformers, we can anticipate vehicle failures and malfunctions before they happen. Thus, we introduce $\textit{CarFormer}$, a Transformer model trained via a new self-supervised learning strategy, and $\textit{EPredictor}$, an autoregressive Transformer decoder model capable of predicting $\textit{when}$ and $\textit{what}$ error pattern will most likely occur after some error code apparition. Despite the challenges of high cardinality of event types, their unbalanced frequency of appearance and limited labelled data, our experimental results demonstrate the excellent predictive ability of our novel model. Specifically, with sequences of $160$ error codes on average, our model is able with only half of the error codes to achieve $80\%$ F1 score for predicting $\textit{what}$ error pattern will occur and achieves an average absolute error of $58.4 \pm 13.2$h $\textit{when}$ forecasting the time of occurrence, thus enabling confident predictive maintenance and enhancing vehicle safety.
中摘: 在本文中，我们将处理自然语言的过程与处理车辆多变量事件流的过程进行了类比，以便预测给定汽车在未来最有可能出现的错误模式及其发生时间。我们的方法利用了来自汽车车队的事件数据的时间动态和上下文关系。事件数据由错误代码的离散值以及时间、里程等连续值组成。通过两个因果Transformer模型，我们可以在问题发生之前预见车辆故障和功能异常。因此，我们引入了名为“CarFormer”的Transformer模型，该模型是通过一种新的自监督学习策略训练的；以及“EPredictor”自回归Transformer解码器模型，能够预测某个错误代码出现后，最有可能发生的错误模式及其发生时间。尽管面临着事件类型数量庞大、频率不均衡以及标注数据有限的挑战，我们的实验结果证明了新型模型的出色预测能力。具体而言，通过平均160个错误代码的序列，仅使用一半的错误代码，我们的模型就能实现80%的F1分数来预测将要发生的错误模式，并且在预测错误模式发生时间上实现了平均绝对误差为58.4±13.2小时，从而实现可靠的预测性维护并提升车辆安全性。

[No.365] Towards Precise Prediction Uncertainty in GNNs: Refining GNNs with Topology-grouping Strategy
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于图神经网络（GNN）的精确预测不确定性：通过拓扑分组策略优化GNN
类别: machine learning v
作者: Hyunjin Seo;Kyusung Seo;Joonhyung Park;Eunho Yang;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Polymerize;AITRICS;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34239
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14223
英摘: Recent advancements in graph neural networks (GNNs) have highlighted the critical need of calibrating model predictions, with neighborhood prediction similarity recognized as a pivotal component. Existing studies suggest that nodes with analogous neighborhood prediction similarity often exhibit similar calibration characteristics. Building on this insight, recent approaches incorporate neighborhood similarity into node-wise temperature scaling techniques. However, our analysis reveals that this assumption does not hold universally. Calibration errors can differ significantly even among nodes with comparable neighborhood similarity, depending on their confidence levels. This necessitates a re-evaluation of existing GNN calibration methods, as a single, unified approach may lead to sub-optimal calibration. In response, we introduce **Simi-Mailbox**, a novel approach that categorizes nodes by both neighborhood similarity and their own confidence, irrespective of proximity or connectivity. Our method allows fine-grained calibration by employing *group-specific* temperature scaling, with each temperature tailored to address the specific miscalibration level of affiliated nodes, rather than adhering to a uniform trend based on neighborhood similarity. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our **Simi-Mailbox** across diverse datasets on different GNN architectures, achieving up to 13.79\% error reduction compared to uncalibrated GNN predictions.
中摘: 近年来，图神经网络（GNNs）的发展突显了校准模型预测的重要性，邻域预测相似性被认作关键因素。现有研究指出，具有类似邻域预测相似性的节点通常表现出相似的校准特性。基于这一洞察，最近的方法将邻域相似性纳入节点级别的温度调整技术中。然而，我们的分析表明，这一假设并不普遍适用。即使邻域相似性相当的节点，其校准误差也可能因置信水平的不同而显著不同。这需要重新评估现有的GNN校准方法，因为单一统一的方法可能会导致校准效果不佳。为此，我们提出了一种名为**Simi-Mailbox**的新方法，该方法根据节点自身的置信度和邻域相似性对节点进行分类，而不考虑它们之间的距离或连接性。我们的方法通过采用*组特定的*温度调整来进行细粒度校准，为每个温度调整专门针对相关节点的具体校准偏差进行设计，而不是遵循基于邻域相似性的统一趋势。广泛的实验表明，**Simi-Mailbox**在不同数据集和各种GNN架构上均有效，与未校准的GNN预测相比，能够实现高达13.79%的误差减少。

[No.366] Beyond Accuracy: On the Effects of Fine-Tuning Towards Vision-Language Model’s Prediction Rationality

[No.367] Blend the Separated: Mixture of Synergistic Experts for Data-Scarcity Drug-Target Interaction Prediction
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 融合分离者：协同专家混合预测数据稀缺性药物-靶标相互作用
类别: machine learning vii
作者: Xinlong Zhai;Chunchen Wang;Ruijia Wang;Jiazheng Kang;Shujie Li;Boyu Chen;Tengfei Ma;Zikai Zhou;Cheng Yang;Chuan Shi;
机构: Beijing University of Posts and Telecommunications;China Telecom;Hunan University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34389
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15796
英摘: Drug-target interaction prediction (DTI) is essential in various applications including drug discovery and clinical application. There are two perspectives of input data widely used in DTI prediction: Intrinsic data represents how drugs or targets are constructed, and extrinsic data represents how drugs or targets are related to other biological entities. However, any of the two perspectives of input data can be scarce for some drugs or targets, especially for those unpopular or newly discovered. Furthermore, ground-truth labels for specific interaction types can also be scarce. Therefore, we propose the first method to tackle DTI prediction under input data and/or label scarcity. To make our model functional when only one perspective of input data is available, we design two separate experts to process intrinsic and extrinsic data respectively and fuse them adaptively according to different samples. Furthermore, to make the two perspectives complement each other and remedy label scarcity, two experts synergize with each other in a mutually supervised way to exploit the enormous unlabeled data. Extensive experiments on 3 real-world datasets under different extents of input data scarcity and/or label scarcity demonstrate our model outperforms states of the art significantly and steadily, with a maximum improvement of 53.53%. We also test our model without any data scarcity and it still outperforms current methods.
中摘: 药物-靶标相互作用预测（DTI）在药物发现和临床应用等多个领域中至关重要。输入数据在DTI预测中有两种广泛使用的视角：固有数据表示药物或靶标是如何构建的，而外在数据则表示药物或靶标与其他生物实体的关系。然而，对于某些药物或靶标，这两种视角中的任何一种输入数据都可能稀缺，尤其是那些不常用或新发现的药物或靶标。此外，特定相互作用类型的ground-truth标签也可能稀缺。因此，我们提出了第一个针对输入数据和/或标签稀缺性进行DTI预测的方法。为了使我们的模型能够在仅有一种视角的输入数据可用时发挥作用，我们设计了两个独立的专家分别处理固有数据和外在数据，并根据不同样本适应性地融合它们。此外，为了使两种视角相互补充并弥补标签稀缺性，在互为监督的方式下，这两个专家协同工作以利用庞大的未标记数据。在不同程度的输入数据稀缺性和/或标签稀缺性的3个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们的模型在各方面显著且稳定地优于现有方法，最大改进幅度达到53.53%。即使在没有任何数据稀缺的情况下，我们的模型也仍然优于当前的方法。

[No.368] GraSP: Simple Yet Effective Graph Similarity Predictions
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: GraSP: 简单而有效的图相似性预测
类别: machine learning vii
