英摘: Graph similarity computation (GSC) is to calculate the similarity between one pair of graphs, which is a fundamental problem with fruitful applications in the graph community. In GSC, graph edit distance (GED) and maximum common subgraph (MCS) are two important similarity metrics, both of which are NP-hard to compute. Instead of calculating the exact values, recent solutions resort to leveraging graph neural networks (GNNs) to learn data-driven models for the estimation of GED and MCS. Most of them are built on components involving node-level interactions crossing graphs, which engender vast computation overhead but are of little avail in effectiveness. In the paper, we present GraSP, a simple yet effective GSC approach for GED and MCS prediction. GraSP achieves high result efficacy through several key instruments: enhanced node features via positional encoding and a GNN model augmented by a gating mechanism, residual connections, as well as multi-scale pooling. Theoretically, GraSP can surpass the 1-WL test, indicating its high expressiveness. Empirically, extensive experiments comparing GraSP against 10 competitors on multiple widely adopted benchmark datasets showcase the superiority of GraSP over prior arts in terms of both effectiveness and efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/HaoranZ99/GraSP.
中摘: 图相似性计算（GSC）是指计算一对图之间的相似度，这是一个在图社区中有广泛应用的基本问题。在GSC中，图编辑距离（GED）和最大公共子图（MCS）是两种重要的相似度度量标准，两者都难以精确计算，属于NP-hard问题。近年来，解决方案转向利用图神经网络（GNNs）来学习数据驱动模型，以估计GED和MCS。大多数方法依赖于涉及节点级别交互跨越图的组件，但这些方法在效果上却收效甚微，带来了巨大的计算开销。在本文中，我们提出了GraSP，这是一种简单而有效的GSC方法，用于预测GED和MCS。GraSP通过几个关键工具实现了高效的结果：通过位置编码增强节点特征，并结合门控机制、残差连接以及多尺度池化来扩展GNN模型。理论上，GraSP可以超越1-WL测试，表明其高度表达能力。实验上，在多个广泛采用的基准数据集上将GraSP与10种竞争对手进行对比的大量实验展示了GraSP在效果和效率方面均优于现有方法。代码可在https://github.com/HaoranZ99/GraSP获取。

[No.373] Multi-Grained Query-Guided Set Prediction Network for Grounded Multimodal Named Entity Recognition
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 面向 grounded 多模态命名实体识别的多粒度查询引导集合预测网络
类别: natural language processing iii
作者: Jielong Tang;Zhenxing Wang;ZiYang Gong;Jianxing Yu;Xiangwei Zhu;Jian Yin;
机构: Sun Yat-sen University;Chinese Academy of Sciences;Pazhou Lab;
类型: Technical

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21033
英摘: Grounded Multimodal Named Entity Recognition (GMNER) is an emerging information extraction (IE) task, aiming to simultaneously extract entity spans, types, and corresponding visual regions of entities from given sentence-image pairs data. Recent unified methods employing machine reading comprehension or sequence generation-based frameworks show limitations in this difficult task. The former, utilizing human-designed type queries, struggles to differentiate ambiguous entities, such as Jordan (Person) and off-White x Jordan (Shoes). The latter, following the one-by-one decoding order, suffers from exposure bias issues. We maintain that these works misunderstand the relationships of multimodal entities. To tackle these, we propose a novel unified framework named Multi-grained Query-guided Set Prediction Network (MQSPN) to learn appropriate relationships at intra-entity and inter-entity levels. Specifically, MQSPN explicitly aligns textual entities with visual regions by employing a set of learnable queries to strengthen intra-entity connections. Based on distinct intra-entity modeling, MQSPN reformulates GMNER as a set prediction, guiding models to establish appropriate inter-entity relationships from a optimal global matching perspective. Additionally, we incorporate a query-guided Fusion Net (QFNet) as a glue network to boost better alignment of two-level relationships. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performances in widely used benchmarks.
中摘: 基于多粒度查询引导集合预测网络（MQSPN）是一种新兴的多模态命名实体识别（GMNER）任务，旨在同时从给定的句子-图像对数据中提取实体跨度、类型以及相应的视觉区域。最近采用机器阅读理解和序列生成框架的统一方法在这项困难的任务中显示出局限性。前者利用人工设计的类型查询难以区分具有歧义性的实体，例如“Jordan”（人名）和“off-White x Jordan”（鞋类）。后者遵循逐个解码的顺序，受到曝光偏差问题的影响。我们认为这些工作误解了多模态实体之间的关系。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新颖的统一框架——多粒度查询引导集合预测网络（MQSPN），以在实体内部和实体之间学习适当的关系。具体而言，MQSPN通过一组可学习的查询显式地将文本实体与视觉区域对齐，从而加强实体内部的连接。基于不同的实体内部建模，MQSPN将GMNER重新表述为集合预测问题，指导模型从全局最优匹配的角度建立适当的实体间关系。此外，我们还引入了一种查询引导融合网络（QFNet）作为粘合网络，以增强两层关系的对齐效果。广泛的实验表明，我们的方法在广泛使用的基准测试中达到了最先进的性能。

[No.374] RLPF: Reinforcement Learning from Prediction Feedback for User Summarization with LLMs
来源: AAAI 2025

标题: RLPF：基于预测反馈的强化学习用户总结方法（使用大语言模型）

英摘: LLM-powered personalization agent systems employ Large Language Models (LLMs) to predict users' behavior from their past activities. Existing pretrained LLMs may generate summaries that are concise but lack the necessary context for downstream tasks, hindering their utility in personalization systems. To address these challenges, we introduce Reinforcement Learning from Prediction Feedback (RLPF). RLPF fine-tunes LLMs to generate concise, human-readable user summaries that are optimized for downstream task performance. 中摘: 基于LLM的强大个性化代理系统利用大型语言模型（LLMs）从用户的过去活动预测用户行为。然而，它们的有效性往往依赖于有效地利用大量且长期的用户历史数据，因为这些数据本身就具有内在的噪声和长度问题。现有的预训练LLM可能生成简洁但缺乏下游任务所需上下文的摘要，阻碍了其在个性化系统中的实用性。为了解决这些挑战，我们引入了预测反馈强化学习（RLPF）。RLPF对LLM进行微调，以生成既简洁又易于理解的用户摘要，并优化了下游任务的性能。通过最大化生成摘要的有用性，RLPF有效地浓缩了广泛的用户历史数据，同时保留了下游任务所需的关键信息。我们的实证评估显示，在提高下游任务外部实用性和摘要内部质量方面取得了显著改进，与基线方法相比，在下游任务性能上提高了高达22%，并在事实性、抽象性和可读性方面分别实现了最高84.59%的胜率。此外，RLPF还实现了令人瞩目的74%的上下文长度减少，同时在19个未见过的任务或数据集中有16个任务上提升了性能，展示了其泛化能力。这种方法为通过有效转化长且嘈杂的用户历史数据为信息丰富且易于理解的表现形式，增强LLM个性化提供了有前景的解决方案。

[No.375] FairTP: A Prolonged Fairness Framework for Traffic Prediction
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: FairTP：一种延长公平性框架的交通预测方法
类别: philosophy and ethics of ai
作者: Jiangnan Xia;Yu Yang;Jiaxing Shen;Senzhang Wang;Jiannong Cao;
机构: Central South University;Education University of Hong Kong;Lingnan University;Hong Kong Polytechnic University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34838
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16214
英摘: Traffic prediction plays a crucial role in intelligent transportation systems. Existing approaches primarily focus on improving overall accuracy, often neglecting a critical issue: whether predictive models lead to biased decisions by transportation authorities. In practice, the uneven deployment of traffic sensors across urban areas results in imbalanced data, causing prediction models to perform poorly in certain regions and leading to unfair decision-making. This imbalance ultimately harms the equity and quality of life for residents. Moreover, current fairness-aware machine learning models only ensure fairness at specific time points, failing to maintain fairness over extended periods. As traffic conditions change, such static fairness approaches become ineffective. To address this gap, we propose FairTP, a framework for prolonged fair traffic prediction. We introduce two new fairness definitions tailored for dynamic traffic scenarios. Fairness in traffic prediction is not static; it varies over time and across regions. Each sensor or urban area can alternate between two states: "sacrifice" (low prediction accuracy) and "benefit" (high prediction accuracy). Prolonged fairness is achieved when the overall states of sensors remain similar over a given period. We define two types of fairness: region-based static fairness and sensor-based dynamic fairness. To implement this, FairTP incorporates a state identification module to classify sensors' states as either "sacrifice" or "benefit," enabling prolonged fairness-aware predictions. Additionally, we introduce a state-guided balanced sampling strategy to further enhance fairness, addressing performance disparities among regions with uneven sensor distributions. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets demonstrate that FairTP significantly improves prediction fairness while minimizing accuracy degradation.
中摘: 智能交通系统中的交通预测起着至关重要的作用。现有的方法主要集中在提高整体准确性上，往往忽视了一个关键问题：预测模型是否会导致交通管理部门作出有偏见的决策。实际上，在城市区域中不均匀部署交通传感器导致数据失衡，使得预测模型在某些地区表现不佳，从而引发不公平的决策。这种不平衡最终损害了居民的公平性和生活质量。此外，当前的公平感知机器学习模型只能确保特定时间点的公平性，而无法在长时间内维持公平性。随着交通状况的变化，这样的静态公平方法变得无效。为了解决这一差距，我们提出了FairTP，一种用于长期公平交通预测的框架。我们引入了两种新的公平性定义，专门针对动态交通场景。交通预测中的公平性不是静态的；它会随时间和地区变化。每个传感器或城市区域可以在“牺牲”（低预测精度）和“受益”（高预测精度）两种状态之间交替。当给定时间段内所有传感器的整体状态保持相似时，便实现了长期的公平性。我们定义了两种类型的公平性：基于地区的静态公平性和基于传感器的动态公平性。为了实现这一点，FairTP整合了一个状态识别模块，能够将传感器的状态分类为“牺牲”或“受益”，从而实现长期的公平感知预测。此外，我们还提出了一种基于状态的平衡采样策略，进一步增强公平性，解决传感器分布不均导致的性能差异问题。在两个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，FairTP在显著提高预测公平性的同时，还能最小化准确性的下降。

[No.377] Practicable Black-Box Evasion Attacks on Link Prediction in Dynamic Graphs—a Graph Sequential Embedding Method
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 面向链接预测的动态图实用黑盒规避攻击——一种图序列嵌入方法
类别: philosophy and ethics of ai
作者: Jiate Li;Meng Pang;Binghui Wang;
机构: Nanchang University;Illinois Institute of Technology;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34824
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13134
英摘: Link prediction in dynamic graphs (LPDG) has been widely applied to real-world applications such as website recommendation, traffic flow prediction, organizational studies, etc. These models are usually kept local and secure, with only the interactive interface restrictively available to the public. Thus, the problem of the black-box evasion attack on the LPDG model, where model interactions and data perturbations are restricted, seems to be essential and meaningful in practice. In this paper, we propose the first practicable black-box evasion attack method that achieves effective attacks against the target LPDG model, within a limited amount of interactions and perturbations. To perform effective attacks under limited perturbations, we develop a graph sequential embedding model to find the desired state embedding of the dynamic graph sequences, under a deep reinforcement learning framework. To overcome the scarcity of interactions, we design a multi-environment training pipeline and train our agent for multiple instances, by sharing an aggregate interaction buffer. Finally, we evaluate our attack against three advanced LPDG models on three real-world graph datasets of different scales and compare its performance with related methods under the interaction and perturbation constraints. Experimental results show that our attack is both effective and practicable.
中摘: 动态图中的链接预测（LPDG）模型已被广泛应用于实际应用中，如网站推荐、交通流量预测、组织研究等。这些模型通常保持局部和安全状态，只有交互界面在有限范围内对公众开放。因此，在模型交互和数据扰动受限的情况下，针对目标LPDG模型进行有效的黑盒逃避攻击问题显得尤为重要且有意义。在本文中，我们提出了首个能够在有限交互和扰动下有效攻击目标LPDG模型的可实践黑盒逃避攻击方法。为了在有限扰动下执行有效的攻击，我们开发了一种图序列嵌入模型，在深度强化学习框架下找到动态图序列的期望状态嵌入。为克服交互稀少的问题，我们设计了一个多环境训练管道，并通过共享聚合交互缓冲区对多个实例进行训练。最后，我们在三个不同规模的实际图数据集上评估了我们的攻击方法，并在交互和扰动约束条件下将其性能与相关方法进行了比较。实验结果表明，我们的攻击方法既有效又可实践。

[No.378] Hierarchical Gradient-Based Genetic Sampling for Accurate Prediction of Biological Oscillations
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于分层梯度遗传采样的生物振荡准确预测方法
类别: search and optimization
作者: Heng Rao;Yu Gu;Jason Zipeng Zhang;Ge Yu;Yang Cao;Minghan Chen;
机构: Northeastern University;Wake Forest University;Virginia Polytechnic Institute and State University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34920
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12816
英摘: Biological oscillations are periodic changes in various signaling processes crucial for the proper functioning of living organisms. These oscillations are modeled by ordinary differential equations, with coefficient variations leading to diverse periodic behaviors, typically measured by oscillatory frequencies. This paper explores sampling techniques for neural networks to model the relationship between system coefficients and oscillatory frequency. However, the scarcity of oscillations in the vast coefficient space results in many samples exhibiting non-periodic behaviors, and small coefficient changes near oscillation boundaries can significantly alter oscillatory properties. This leads to non-oscillatory bias and boundary sensitivity, making accurate predictions difficult. While existing importance and uncertainty sampling approaches partially mitigate these challenges, they either fail to resolve the sensitivity problem or result in redundant sampling. To address these limitations, we propose the Hierarchical Gradient-based Genetic Sampling (HGGS) framework, which improves the accuracy of neural network predictions for biological oscillations. The first layer, Gradient-based Filtering, extracts sensitive oscillation boundaries and removes redundant non-oscillatory samples, creating a balanced coarse dataset. The second layer, Multigrid Genetic Sampling, utilizes residual information to refine these boundaries and explore new high-residual regions, increasing data diversity for model training. Experimental results demonstrate that HGGS outperforms seven comparative sampling methods across four biological systems, highlighting its effectiveness in enhancing sampling and prediction accuracy.
中摘: 生物振荡是指各种对于生物体正常功能至关重要的信号过程中的周期性变化。这些振荡可以通过常微分方程进行建模，系数的变化会导致不同的周期性行为，通常通过振荡频率来衡量。本文探讨了用于神经网络建模系统系数与振荡频率之间关系的采样技术。然而，在庞大的系数空间中，振荡现象相对稀缺，导致许多样本表现出非周期性行为；而在振荡边界附近的微小系数变化也会显著改变振荡特性，这造成了非振荡偏差和边界敏感性，使得准确预测变得困难。虽然现有的重要性和不确定性采样方法在一定程度上缓解了这些问题，但它们要么未能解决敏感性问题，要么导致冗余采样。为了解决这些限制，我们提出了基于层次梯度的遗传采样（Hierarchical Gradient-based Genetic Sampling, HGGS）框架，该框架可以提高神经网络对生物振荡预测的准确性。第一层，基于梯度过滤，提取敏感的振荡边界并移除冗余的非振荡样本，创建一个平衡的粗略数据集。第二层，多网格遗传采样，利用残差信息细化这些边界，并探索新的高残差区域，增加模型训练的数据多样性。实验结果显示，HGGS 在四个生物系统中优于七种比较采样方法，突显了其在增强采样和预测准确性方面的有效性。

[No.379] Improved Bounds for Online Facility Location with Predictions
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于预测的在线设施定位问题改进边界
类别: search and optimization
作者: Dimitris Fotakis;Evangelia Gergatsouli;Themistoklis Gouleakis;Nikolas Patris;Thanos Tolias;
机构: National Technical University of Athens;Archimedes Research Center;University of Wisconsin-Madison;Georgia Institute of Technology;Nanyang Technological University;University of California, Irvine;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/34903
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2107.08277
英摘: We consider Online Facility Location in the framework of learning-augmented online algorithms. In Online Facility Location (OFL), demands arrive one-by-one in a metric space and must be (irrevocably) assigned to an open facility upon arrival, without any knowledge about future demands. We focus on uniform facility opening costs and present an online algorithm for OFL that exploits potentially imperfect predictions on the locations of the optimal facilities. We prove that the competitive ratio decreases from sublogarithmic in the number of demands $n$ to constant as the so-called $η_1$ error, i.e., the sum of distances of the predicted locations to the optimal facility locations, decreases. E.g., our analysis implies that if for some $\varepsilon > 0$, $η_1 = \mathrm{OPT} / n^\varepsilon$, where $\mathrm{OPT}$ is the cost of the optimal solution, the competitive ratio becomes $O(1/\varepsilon)$. We complement our analysis with a matching lower bound establishing that the dependence of the algorithm's competitive ratio on the $η_1$ error is optimal, up to constant factors. Finally, we evaluate our algorithm on real world data and compare the performance of our learning-augmented approach against the performance of the best known algorithm for OFL without predictions.
中摘: 我们在学习增强型在线算法的框架下考虑在线设施定位问题。在在线设施定位（OFL）中，需求一个接一个地在度量空间中到达，并且必须在到达时不可撤销地分配给已开放的设施，而没有任何对未来需求的了解。我们关注均匀设施开设成本，并提出了一种利用潜在不完美的最优设施位置预测的在线算法。我们证明了随着所谓的$η_1$误差（即预测位置与最优设施位置之间的距离总和）减少，竞争比从需求数量$n$的次对数减少到常数。例如，我们的分析表明，如果对于某个$\varepsilon>0$，有$η_1 = \mathrm{OPT} / n^\varepsilon$（其中$\mathrm{OPT}$是最优解的成本），那么竞争比将变为$O(1/\varepsilon)$。我们还通过提供一个匹配的下界来补充我们的分析，证明了该算法的竞争比对$η_1$误差的依赖性在常数因子内是最佳的。最后，我们在实际数据上评估了该算法，并将其学习增强的方法与已知的最佳无预测在线算法进行了性能对比。

[No.384] G2LTraj: A Global-to-Local Generation Approach for Trajectory Prediction
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: G2LTraj：一种全局到局部生成方法用于轨迹预测
类别: Data Mining
作者: Zhanwei Zhang;Zishuo Hua;Minghao Chen;Wei Lu;Binbin Lin;Deng Cai;Wenxiao Wang;
机构: Zhejiang University;Hangzhou Dianzi University;Ningbo Beilun Third Container Terminal Co., Ltd;Zhiyuan Research Institute;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/285
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19330
英摘: Predicting future trajectories of traffic agents accurately holds substantial importance in various applications such as autonomous driving. Previous methods commonly infer all future steps of an agent either recursively or simultaneously. However, the recursive strategy suffers from the accumulated error, while the simultaneous strategy overlooks the constraints among future steps, resulting in kinematically infeasible predictions. To address these issues, in this paper, we propose G2LTraj, a plug-and-play global-to-local generation approach for trajectory prediction. Specifically, we generate a series of global key steps that uniformly cover the entire future time range. Subsequently, the local intermediate steps between the adjacent key steps are recursively filled in. In this way, we prevent the accumulated error from propagating beyond the adjacent key steps. Moreover, to boost the kinematical feasibility, we not only introduce the spatial constraints among key steps but also strengthen the temporal constraints among the intermediate steps. Finally, to ensure the optimal granularity of key steps, we design a selectable granularity strategy that caters to each predicted trajectory. Our G2LTraj significantly improves the performance of seven existing trajectory predictors across the ETH, UCY and nuScenes datasets. Experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness. Code will be available at https://github.com/Zhanwei-Z/G2LTraj.
中摘: 准确预测交通代理未来的轨迹在各种应用中（如自动驾驶）具有重要意义。以往的方法通常会递归地或同时地推断所有未来步骤。然而，递归策略会导致累积误差，而同时策略则忽视了未来步骤之间的约束，从而产生动力学上不可行的预测。为了解决这些问题，本文提出了一种名为G2LTraj的插件式全局到局部生成方法，用于轨迹预测。具体来说，我们生成一系列均匀覆盖整个未来时间范围的全局关键步骤。随后，在相邻的关键步骤之间递归填充局部中间步骤。这样可以防止累积误差传播到相邻的关键步骤之外。此外，为了提高动力学可行性，我们不仅引入了关键步骤之间的空间约束，还加强了中间步骤之间的时间约束。最后，为了确保关键步骤的最佳粒度，我们设计了一种可选粒度策略，以适应每个预测的轨迹。我们的G2LTraj在ETH、UCY和nuScenes数据集中显著提高了七个现有轨迹预测器的性能，并且实验结果证明了其有效性。代码将在https://github.com/Zhanwei-Z/G2LTraj获取。

[No.385] Make Graph Neural Networks Great Again: A Generic Integration Paradigm of Topology-Free Patterns for Traffic Speed Prediction
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 重新焕发图神经网络的 brilliance：一种拓扑无关模式的一般性集成范式用于交通速度预测

作者: Yicheng Zhou;Pengfei Wang;Hao Dong;Denghui Zhang;Dingqi Yang;Yanjie Fu;Pengyang Wang;

英摘: Urban traffic speed prediction aims to estimate the future traffic speed for improving urban transportation services. Enormous efforts have been made to exploit Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) for modeling spatial correlations and temporal dependencies of traffic speed evolving patterns, regularized by graph topology.While achieving promising results, current traffic speed prediction methods still suffer from ignoring topology-free patterns, which cannot be captured by GNNs. To tackle this challenge, we propose a generic model for enabling the current GNN-based methods to preserve topology-free patterns. Specifically, we first develop a Dual Cross-Scale Transformer (DCST) architecture, including a Spatial Transformer and a Temporal Transformer, to preserve the cross-scale topology-free patterns and associated dynamics, respectively. Then, to further integrate both topology-regularized/-free patterns, we propose a distillation-style learning framework, in which the existing GNN-based methods are considered as the teacher model, and the proposed DCST architecture is considered as the student model. The teacher model would inject the learned topology-regularized patterns into the student model for integrating topology-free patterns. The extensive experimental results demonstrated the effectiveness of our methods.
中摘: 城市交通速度预测旨在估计未来的交通速度以改善城市交通运输服务。人们已经投入了大量努力，利用图神经网络（GNNs）来建模交通速度演变模式的空间相关性和时间依赖性，并通过图形拓扑进行正则化。尽管取得了令人鼓舞的结果，当前的交通速度预测方法仍然忽视了一些无拓扑结构的模式，这些模式无法被GNNs捕捉。为了解决这一挑战，我们提出了一种通用模型，使现有的基于GNN的方法能够保留无拓扑结构的模式。具体而言，我们首先开发了一个双尺度交叉变换器（DCST）架构，包括一个空间变换器和一个时间变换器，分别用于保留跨尺度的无拓扑结构模式及其相关动态。然后，为了进一步整合拓扑正则化/无拓扑结构模式，我们提出了一种类似蒸馏的学习框架，在该框架中，现有的基于GNN的方法被视为教师模型，而提出的DCST架构被视为学生模型。教师模型将注入学习到的拓扑正则化模式到学生模型中，以整合无拓扑结构的模式。广泛的实验结果证明了我们方法的有效性。

英摘: Molecular property prediction (MPP) plays a crucial role in biomedical applications, but it often encounters challenges due to a scarcity of labeled data. [No.393] Visual Attention Prompted Prediction and Learning
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 视觉注意力引导预测与学习
类别: Machine Learning
作者: Yifei Zhang;Bo Pan;Siyi Gu;Guangji Bai;Meikang Qiu;Xiaofeng Yang;Liang Zhao;
机构: Emory University;Stanford University;Augusta University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/610
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.08420
英摘: Visual explanation (attention)-guided learning uses not only labels but also explanations to guide model reasoning process. While visual attention-guided learning has shown promising results, it requires a large number of explanation annotations that are time-consuming to prepare. However, in many real-world situations, it is usually desired to prompt the model with visual attention without model retraining. For example, when doing AI-assisted cancer classification on a medical image, users (e.g., clinicians) can provide the AI model with visual attention prompt on which areas are indispensable and which are precluded. Despite its promising objectives, achieving visual attention-prompted prediction presents several major challenges: 1) How can the visual prompt be effectively integrated into the model's reasoning process? 2) How should the model handle samples that lack visual prompts? 3) What is the impact on the model's performance when a visual prompt is imperfect? This paper introduces a novel framework for attention-prompted prediction and learning, utilizing visual prompts to steer the model's reasoning process. To improve performance in non-prompted situations and align it with prompted scenarios, we propose a co-training approach for both non-prompted and prompted models, ensuring they share similar parameters and activations. Additionally, for instances where the visual prompt does not encompass the entire input image, we have developed innovative attention prompt refinement methods. These methods interpolate the incomplete prompts while maintaining alignment with the model's explanations. Extensive experiments on four datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed framework in enhancing predictions for samples both with and without prompt.
中摘: 视觉解释（注意力）引导学习不仅使用标签，还使用解释来指导模型的推理过程。虽然视觉注意力引导学习已经显示出有前景的结果，但它需要大量的解释注释，这些注释的准备耗时较长。然而，在许多现实世界的情境下，通常希望在不重新训练模型的情况下，用视觉注意力提示模型。例如，在使用AI辅助进行医学图像中的癌症分类时，用户（如临床医生）可以向AI模型提供视觉注意力提示，指出哪些区域是必不可少的，哪些区域是被排除的。尽管其目标具有很大的潜力，实现基于视觉注意力提示的预测仍面临几个主要挑战：1）如何有效地将视觉提示整合进模型的推理过程中？2）当样本缺乏视觉提示时，模型应该如何处理？3）当视觉提示不完整时，对模型性能会产生什么影响？本文介绍了一种新的注意力提示预测和学习框架，利用视觉提示来引导模型的推理过程。为了提高非提示情况下的性能并使其与提示情景保持一致，我们提出了一种针对非提示和提示模型的协同训练方法，确保它们共享相似的参数和激活。此外，对于视觉提示未能涵盖整个输入图像的情况，我们开发了创新的注意力提示精炼方法。这些方法通过插值不完整的提示同时保持与模型解释的一致性。在四个数据集上的广泛实验表明，我们提出的框架能够有效提升带有和不带提示的样本的预测效果。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.394] A Cognitive-Driven Trajectory Prediction Model for Autonomous Driving in Mixed Autonomy Environments
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 一种面向混合自动化环境的认知驱动轨迹预测模型
类别: Multidisciplinary Topics and Applications
作者: Haicheng Liao;Zhenning Li;Chengyue Wang;Bonan Wang;Hanlin Kong;Yanchen Guan;Guofa Li;Zhiyong Cui;
机构: University of Macau;University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;Chongqing University;Beihang University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/656
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17520
英摘: As autonomous driving technology progresses, the need for precise trajectory prediction models becomes paramount. This paper introduces an innovative model that infuses cognitive insights into trajectory prediction, focusing on perceived safety and dynamic decision-making. Distinct from traditional approaches, our model excels in analyzing interactions and behavior patterns in mixed autonomy traffic scenarios. It represents a significant leap forward, achieving marked performance improvements on several key datasets. Specifically, it surpasses existing benchmarks with gains of 16.2% on the Next Generation Simulation (NGSIM), 27.4% on the Highway Drone (HighD), and 19.8% on the Macao Connected Autonomous Driving (MoCAD) dataset. Our proposed model shows exceptional proficiency in handling corner cases, essential for real-world applications. Moreover, its robustness is evident in scenarios with missing or limited data, outperforming most of the state-of-the-art baselines. This adaptability and resilience position our model as a viable tool for real-world autonomous driving systems, heralding a new standard in vehicle trajectory prediction for enhanced safety and efficiency.
中摘: 随着自动驾驶技术的发展，精确的轨迹预测模型变得至关重要。本文介绍了一种创新模型，该模型将认知洞察融入轨迹预测，专注于感知安全性和动态决策制定。与传统方法不同，我们的模型在分析混合自主交通场景中的交互和行为模式方面表现出色。它代表了显著的进步，在几个关键数据集上实现了明显的性能提升。具体而言，它在下一代仿真（NGSIM）数据集上的表现优于现有基准16.2%，在高速公路无人机（HighD）数据集上优于现有基准27.4%，在澳门连接式自动驾驶（MoCAD）数据集上优于现有基准19.8%。我们提出的模型在处理边缘案例方面表现出色，这对于实际应用至关重要。此外，在缺少或数据有限的情况下，其鲁棒性尤为明显，超越了大多数最先进的基线模型。这种适应性和韧性使我们的模型成为现实世界自动驾驶系统的一个可行工具，标志着车辆轨迹预测的新标准，提高了安全性与效率。

[No.396] Heterogeneous Causal Metapath Graph Neural Network for Gene-Microbe-Disease Association Prediction
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 异质因果元路径图神经网络在基因-微生物-疾病关联预测中的应用
类别: Multidisciplinary Topics and Applications
作者: Kexin Zhang;Feng Huang;Luotao Liu;Zhankun Xiong;Hongyu Zhang;Yuan Quan;Wen Zhang;
机构: Huazhong Agricultural University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/681
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19156
英摘: The recent focus on microbes in human medicine highlights their potential role in the genetic framework of diseases. To decode the complex interactions among genes, microbes, and diseases, computational predictions of gene-microbe-disease (GMD) associations are crucial. Existing methods primarily address gene-disease and microbe-disease associations, but the more intricate triple-wise GMD associations remain less explored. In this paper, we propose a Heterogeneous Causal Metapath Graph Neural Network (HCMGNN) to predict GMD associations. HCMGNN constructs a heterogeneous graph linking genes, microbes, and diseases through their pairwise associations, and utilizes six predefined causal metapaths to extract directed causal subgraphs, which facilitate the multi-view analysis of causal relations among three entity types. Within each subgraph, we employ a causal semantic sharing message passing network for node representation learning, coupled with an attentive fusion method to integrate these representations for predicting GMD associations. Our extensive experiments show that HCMGNN effectively predicts GMD associations and addresses association sparsity issue by enhancing the graph's semantics and structure.
中摘: 近期医学领域对微生物的关注突显了它们在疾病遗传框架中的潜在作用。为了破解基因、微生物和疾病之间的复杂相互作用，计算预测基因-微生物-疾病（GMD）关联至关重要。现有方法主要处理基因-疾病和微生物-疾病关联，但三者之间的更为复杂的三元组关联则较少被探索。本文提出了一种异质因果元路径图神经网络（HCMGNN），用于预测GMD关联。HCMGNN通过构建一个异质图来连接基因、微生物和疾病，利用六个预定义的因果元路径提取定向因果子图，这有助于三种实体类型之间因果关系的多视图分析。在每个子图内，我们采用因果语义共享消息传递网络进行节点表示学习，并结合注意融合方法将这些表示综合起来以预测GMD关联。我们的大量实验表明，HCMGNN能够有效预测GMD关联，并通过增强图的语义和结构来解决关联稀疏性问题。

[No.397] Improving Paratope and Epitope Prediction by Multi-Modal Contrastive Learning and Interaction Informativeness Estimation
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 通过多模态对比学习和相互作用信息估计改进配体和表位预测
类别: Multidisciplinary Topics and Applications
作者: Zhiwei Wang;Yongkang Wang;Wen Zhang;
机构: Huazhong Agricultural University;Engineering Research Center of Intelligent Technology for Agriculture;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/669
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20668
英摘: Accurately predicting antibody-antigen binding residues, i.e., paratopes and epitopes, is crucial in antibody design. However, existing methods solely focus on uni-modal data (either sequence or structure), disregarding the complementary information present in multi-modal data, and most methods predict paratopes and epitopes separately, overlooking their specific spatial interactions. In this paper, we propose a novel Multi-modal contrastive learning and Interaction informativeness estimation-based method for Paratope and Epitope prediction, named MIPE, by using both sequence and structure data of antibodies and antigens. MIPE implements a multi-modal contrastive learning strategy, which maximizes representations of binding and non-binding residues within each modality and meanwhile aligns uni-modal representations towards effective modal representations. To exploit the spatial interaction information, MIPE also incorporates an interaction informativeness estimation that computes the estimated interaction matrices between antibodies and antigens, thereby approximating them to the actual ones. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our method compared to baselines. Additionally, the ablation studies and visualizations demonstrate the superiority of MIPE owing to the better representations acquired through multi-modal contrastive learning and the interaction patterns comprehended by the interaction informativeness estimation.
中摘: 准确预测抗体-抗原结合位点，即互补决定区（paratopes）和表位（epitopes），在抗体设计中至关重要。然而，现有方法仅专注于单模数据（序列或结构），忽视了多模数据中存在的互补信息，并且大多数方法分别预测互补决定区和表位，忽略了它们的具体空间相互作用。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为MIPE的新颖的多模态对比学习和相互作用信息量估计方法，通过同时使用抗体和抗原的序列和结构数据来进行预测。MIPE 实施了一种多模态对比学习策略，该策略在每个模态内最大化结合和非结合残基的表示，并同时将单模态表示对齐到有效的模态表示。为了利用空间相互作用信息，MIPE 还整合了一种相互作用信息量估计方法，计算抗体与抗原之间的估计相互作用矩阵，从而近似这些实际矩阵。广泛的实验表明，我们的方法在基准方法上具有明显的优势。此外，消融研究和可视化结果进一步证明了 MIPE 的优越性，这得益于多模态对比学习获得的更好表示以及相互作用信息量估计理解的相互作用模式。

[No.398] MFTraj: Map-Free, Behavior-Driven Trajectory Prediction for Autonomous Driving
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: MFTraj：无地图、行为驱动的自动驾驶轨迹预测
类别: Multidisciplinary Topics and Applications
作者: Haicheng Liao;Zhenning Li;Chengyue Wang;Huanming Shen;Dongping Liao;Bonan Wang;Guofa Li;Chengzhong Xu;
机构: University of Macau;University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;Chongqing University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/657
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.01266
英摘: This paper introduces a trajectory prediction model tailored for autonomous driving, focusing on capturing complex interactions in dynamic traffic scenarios without reliance on high-definition maps. The model, termed MFTraj, harnesses historical trajectory data combined with a novel dynamic geometric graph-based behavior-aware module. At its core, an adaptive structure-aware interactive graph convolutional network captures both positional and behavioral features of road users, preserving spatial-temporal intricacies. Enhanced by a linear attention mechanism, the model achieves computational efficiency and reduced parameter overhead. Evaluations on the Argoverse, NGSIM, HighD, and MoCAD datasets underscore MFTraj's robustness and adaptability, outperforming numerous benchmarks even in data-challenged scenarios without the need for additional information such as HD maps or vectorized maps. Importantly, it maintains competitive performance even in scenarios with substantial missing data, on par with most existing state-of-the-art models. The results and methodology suggest a significant advancement in autonomous driving trajectory prediction, paving the way for safer and more efficient autonomous systems.
中摘: 本文介绍了一种针对自动驾驶的轨迹预测模型，专注于在无需依赖高精度地图的情况下捕捉动态交通场景中的复杂交互。该模型被称为MFTraj，结合了历史轨迹数据和一种新颖的行为感知动态几何图模块。其核心在于自适应结构感知交互图卷积网络，能够同时捕获道路使用者的位置和行为特征，并保留空间-时间的复杂细节。通过线性注意力机制的增强，该模型实现了计算效率并减少了参数开销。在Argoverse、NGSIM、HighD和MoCAD数据集上的评估证明了MFTraj的鲁棒性和适应性，在缺乏高精度地图或矢量化地图等额外信息的数据挑战场景中，其性能甚至优于众多基准模型。更重要的是，即使在大量数据缺失的情景下，它也能保持与大多数现有先进模型相当的竞争性能。研究结果和方法表明，在自动驾驶轨迹预测方面取得了显著进展，为更安全、更高效的自主系统铺平了道路。

[No.402] ZeroDDI: A Zero-Shot Drug-Drug Interaction Event Prediction Method with Semantic Enhanced Learning and Dual-modal Uniform Alignment
来源: IJCAI 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 零DDI：一种基于语义增强学习和双模态统一对齐的零样本药物-药物相互作用事件预测方法
类别: Multidisciplinary Topics and Applications
作者: Ziyan Wang;Zhankun Xiong;Feng Huang;Xuan Liu;Wen Zhang;
机构: Huazhong Agricultural University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://www.ijcai.org/proceedings/2024/671
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00891
英摘: Drug-drug interactions (DDIs) can result in various pharmacological changes, which can be categorized into different classes known as DDI events (DDIEs). In recent years, previously unobserved/unseen DDIEs have been emerging, posing a new classification task when unseen classes have no labelled instances in the training stage, which is formulated as a zero-shot DDIE prediction (ZS-DDIE) task. However, existing computational methods are not directly applicable to ZS-DDIE, which has two primary challenges: obtaining suitable DDIE representations and handling the class imbalance issue. To overcome these challenges, we propose a novel method named ZeroDDI for the ZS-DDIE task. Specifically, we design a biological semantic enhanced DDIE representation learning module, which emphasizes the key biological semantics and distills discriminative molecular substructure-related semantics for DDIE representation learning. Furthermore, we propose a dual-modal uniform alignment strategy to distribute drug pair representations and DDIE semantic representations uniformly in a unit sphere and align the matched ones, which can mitigate the issue of class imbalance. Extensive experiments showed that ZeroDDI surpasses the baselines and indicate that it is a promising tool for detecting unseen DDIEs. Our code has been released in https://github.com/wzy-Sarah/ZeroDDI.
中摘: 药物-药物相互作用（DDIs）可以导致各种药理学变化，这些变化可以归类为不同的类别，即DDI事件（DDIEs）。近年来，一些未观察到或未见过的DDIEs正在出现，在训练阶段没有标注实例的情况下，这提出了一个新的分类任务，即零样本DDIE预测（ZS-DDIE）任务。然而，现有的计算方法并不直接适用于ZS-DDIE，它有两个主要挑战：获取合适的DDIE表示和处理类别不平衡问题。为了克服这些挑战，我们提出了一种名为ZeroDDI的新方法，专门用于ZS-DDIE任务。具体而言，我们设计了一个生物语义增强的DDIE表示学习模块，该模块强调关键的生物语义，并提炼出区分性的小分子亚结构相关的语义，以促进DDIE表示学习。此外，我们还提出了一种双模态统一对齐策略，将药物对表示和DDIE语义表示均匀分布在单位球中，并对匹配项进行对齐，这可以缓解类别不平衡的问题。广泛实验表明，ZeroDDI超越了基线方法，并表明它是一个检测未见过的DDIEs的有前景工具。我们的代码已发布在https://github.com/wzy-Sarah/ZeroDDI。

[No.404] Accurate and Data-Efficient Toxicity Prediction when Annotators Disagree
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 当注释者意见不一致时的准确且数据高效的毒性预测
类别: 
作者: Harbani Jaggi;Kashyap Coimbatore Murali;Eve Fleisig;Erdem Biyik;
机构: 
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.1221/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12217
英摘: When annotators disagree, predicting the labels given by individual annotators can capture nuances overlooked by traditional label aggregation. We introduce three approaches to predicting individual annotator ratings on the toxicity of text by incorporating individual annotator-specific information: a neural collaborative filtering (NCF) approach, an in-context learning (ICL) approach, and an intermediate embedding-based architecture. We also study the utility of demographic information for rating prediction. NCF showed limited utility; however, integrating annotator history, demographics, and survey information permits both the embedding-based architecture and ICL to substantially improve prediction accuracy, with the embedding-based architecture outperforming the other methods. We also find that, if demographics are predicted from survey information, using these imputed demographics as features performs comparably to using true demographic data. This suggests that demographics may not provide substantial information for modeling ratings beyond what is captured in survey responses. Our findings raise considerations about the relative utility of different types of annotator information and provide new approaches for modeling annotators in subjective NLP tasks.
中摘: 当注释者存在分歧时，预测个体注释者的标签可以捕捉传统标签聚合所忽略的细微差别。我们引入了三种方法来通过结合个体注释者特定的信息来预测文本毒性的评分：神经协同过滤（NCF）方法、上下文学习（ICL）方法以及一种中间嵌入式架构。我们还研究了人口统计信息在评分预测中的应用价值。NCF显示出有限的应用价值；然而，将注释者的历史记录、人口统计信息和调查数据结合起来，允许嵌入式架构和ICL显著提高预测准确性，其中嵌入式架构的表现优于其他方法。我们还发现，如果从调查信息中预测人口统计信息，并使用这些推断的人口统计信息作为特征，其性能与使用真实的人口统计数据相当。这表明人口统计信息可能并不提供超出调查响应所捕获信息之外的实质性建模帮助。我们的研究结果引发了关于不同类型注释者信息相对价值的考虑，并为建模主观自然语言处理任务中的注释者提供了新的方法。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.405] An Instruction Tuning-Based Contrastive Learning Framework for Aspect Sentiment Quad Prediction with Implicit Aspects and Opinions
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于指令调整的对比学习框架：用于隐含方面和观点的方面情感四元预测

[No.407] API Is Enough: Conformal Prediction for Large Language Models Without Logit-Access
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: API即可：无需访问 logits 的大型语言模型的自校准预测
类别: 
作者: Jiayuan Su;Jing Luo;Hongwei Wang;Lu Cheng;
机构: Zhejiang University;Shandong University;University of Illinois Chicago;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.54/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01216
英摘: This study aims to address the pervasive challenge of quantifying uncertainty in large language models (LLMs) without logit-access. Conformal Prediction (CP), known for its model-agnostic and distribution-free features, is a desired approach for various LLMs and data distributions. However, existing CP methods for LLMs typically assume access to the logits, which are unavailable for some API-only LLMs. In addition, logits are known to be miscalibrated, potentially leading to degraded CP performance. To tackle these challenges, we introduce a novel CP method that (1) is tailored for API-only LLMs without logit-access; (2) minimizes the size of prediction sets; and (3) ensures a statistical guarantee of the user-defined coverage. The core idea of this approach is to formulate nonconformity measures using both coarse-grained (i.e., sample frequency) and fine-grained uncertainty notions (e.g., semantic similarity). Experimental results on both close-ended and open-ended Question Answering tasks show our approach can mostly outperform the logit-based CP baselines.
中摘: 本研究旨在解决大型语言模型（LLM）在没有logit访问的情况下量化不确定性这一普遍挑战。Conformal预测（CP），以其模型无关性和无分布假设的特点，是处理各种LLM和数据分布的理想方法。然而，现有的针对LLM的CP方法通常假设可以访问logit，而一些仅通过API的LLM则无法提供logit访问。此外，logit本身可能校准不当，这可能会导致CP性能下降。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的CP方法，该方法具有以下特点：（1）专门针对没有logit访问的API-only LLM；（2）最小化预测集的大小；（3）确保用户定义覆盖范围的统计保证。这种方法的核心思想是使用粗粒度（即样本频率）和细粒度的不确定性概念（例如，语义相似性）来制定非一致性度量。实验结果表明，在封闭式和开放式问答任务上，我们的方法大多能优于基于logit的CP基准方法。

[No.410] Collaborative Performance Prediction for Large Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 大型语言模型协作性能预测
类别: 
作者: Qiyuan Zhang;Fuyuan Lyu;Xue Liu;Chen Ma;
机构: City University of Hong Kong;McGill University;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.150/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01300
英摘: Comprehensively understanding and accurately predicting the performance of large language models across diverse downstream tasks has emerged as a pivotal challenge in NLP research. The pioneering scaling law on downstream works demonstrated intrinsic similarities within model families and utilized such similarities for performance prediction. However, they tend to overlook the similarities between model families and only consider design factors listed in the original scaling law. To overcome these limitations, we introduce a novel framework, Collaborative Performance Prediction (CPP), which significantly enhances prediction accuracy by leveraging the historical performance of various models on downstream tasks and other design factors for both model and task. We also collect a collaborative data sourced from online platforms containing both historical performance and additional design factors. With the support of the collaborative data, CPP not only surpasses traditional scaling laws in predicting the performance of scaled LLMs but also facilitates a detailed analysis of factor importance, an area previously overlooked.
中摘: 全面理解和准确预测大型语言模型在多样化的下游任务中的性能已成为自然语言处理（NLP）研究中的一个关键挑战。最初的下游工作扩展律展示了模型家族内的固有相似性，并利用这些相似性进行性能预测。然而，它们往往忽视了不同模型家族之间的相似性，仅考虑原始扩展律中列出的设计因素。为了克服这些局限，我们引入了一种新的框架——协作性能预测（CPP），该框架通过利用各种模型在下游任务上的历史性能以及其他针对模型和任务的设计因素，显著提高了预测准确性。我们还收集了来自在线平台的协作数据，其中包括历史性能和其他设计因素。借助协作数据的支持，CPP 不仅超越了传统的扩展律，在预测缩放后的大型语言模型的性能方面表现更佳，而且还促进了对因素重要性的详细分析，这是以前被忽视的一个领域。

[No.412] Consolidating Ranking and Relevance Predictions of Large Language Models through Post-Processing
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 通过后处理 consolidating 大型语言模型的排名和相关性预测
类别: 
作者: Le Yan;Zhen Qin;Honglei Zhuang;Rolf Jagerman;Xuanhui Wang;Michael Bendersky;Harrie Oosterhuis;
机构: Google;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.25/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11791
英摘: The powerful generative abilities of large language models (LLMs) show potential in generating relevance labels for search applications. Previous work has found that directly asking about relevancy, such as ``How relevant is document A to query Q?", results in sub-optimal ranking. Instead, the pairwise ranking prompting (PRP) approach produces promising ranking performance through asking about pairwise comparisons, e.g., ``Is document A more relevant than document B to query Q?". Thus, while LLMs are effective at their ranking ability, this is not reflected in their relevance label generation. In this work, we propose a post-processing method to consolidate the relevance labels generated by an LLM with its powerful ranking abilities. Our method takes both LLM generated relevance labels and pairwise preferences. The labels are then altered to satisfy the pairwise preferences of the LLM, while staying as close to the original values as possible. Our experimental results indicate that our approach effectively balances label accuracy and ranking performance. Thereby, our work shows it is possible to combine both the ranking and labeling abilities of LLMs through post-processing.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLM）的强大生成能力在为搜索应用生成相关标签方面显示出潜力。以往的研究发现，直接询问关于相关性的内容，例如“文档A与查询Q的相关性如何？”，会导致排名不佳。相反，通过询问成对比较，如“文档A与文档B相比，哪一个更相关于查询Q？”的方式来使用成对排名提示（PRP）方法，能够产生令人满意的排名性能。因此，虽然LLM在其排名能力方面表现出有效性，但这种能力并未体现在其生成的相关标签中。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种后处理方法，将LLM生成的相关标签与其强大的排名能力结合起来。该方法同时考虑了LLM生成的相关标签和成对偏好。然后，这些标签被调整以满足LLM的成对偏好，同时尽可能保持原始值的准确性。我们的实验结果表明，我们的方法有效地平衡了标签准确性和排名性能。因此，我们的工作展示了可以通过后处理将LLM的排名能力和标签能力结合起来的可能性。

[No.413] Debate as Optimization: Adaptive Conformal Prediction and Diverse Retrieval for Event Extraction
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 辩论作为优化：自适应校准预测与多样化检索在事件提取中的应用
类别: 
作者: Sijia Wang;Lifu Huang;
机构: Virginia Tech;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.958/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12197
英摘: We propose a multi-agent debate as optimization (DAO) system for event extraction, where the primary objective is to iteratively refine the large language models (LLMs) outputs through debating without parameter tuning. In DAO, we introduce two novel modules: the Diverse-RAG (DRAG) module and the Adaptive Conformal Prediction (AdaCP) module. DRAG systematically retrieves supporting information that best fits the debate discussion, while AdaCP enhances the accuracy and reliability of event extraction by effectively rejecting less promising answers. Experimental results demonstrate a significant reduction in the performance gap between supervised approaches and tuning-free LLM-based methods by 18.1% and 17.8% on ACE05 and 17.9% and 15.2% on CASIE for event detection and argument extraction respectively.
中摘: 我们提出了一种多智能体辩论作为优化（DAO）系统用于事件提取，其主要目标是通过辩论逐步细化大型语言模型（LLMs）的输出，而不进行参数调整。在DAO中，我们引入了两个新型模块：Diverse-RAG（DRAG）模块和自适应符合预测（AdaCP）模块。DRAG系统性地检索出最符合辩论讨论的支持信息，而AdaCP则通过有效排除不太有希望的答案来增强事件提取的准确性和可靠性。实验结果表明，DAO显著缩小了监督方法与无需调参的LLM基方法之间的性能差距，在ACE05数据集上分别在事件检测和论据提取方面减少了18.1%和17.8%，在CASIE数据集上分别减少了17.9%和15.2%。

[No.414] DKEC: Domain Knowledge Enhanced Multi-Label Classification for Diagnosis Prediction
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: DKEC：基于领域知识增强的多标签分类诊断预测
类别: 
作者: Xueren Ge;Abhishek Satpathy;Ronald Dean Williams;John Stankovic;Homa Alemzadeh;
机构: University of Virginia;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.712/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.07059
英摘: Multi-label text classification (MLTC) tasks in the medical domain often face the long-tail label distribution problem. Prior works have explored hierarchical label structures to find relevant information for few-shot classes, but mostly neglected to incorporate external knowledge from medical guidelines. This paper presents DKEC, Domain Knowledge Enhanced Classification for diagnosis prediction with two innovations: (1) automated construction of heterogeneous knowledge graphs from external sources to capture semantic relations among diverse medical entities, (2) incorporating the heterogeneous knowledge graphs in few-shot classification using a label-wise attention mechanism. We construct DKEC using three online medical knowledge sources and evaluate it on a real-world Emergency Medical Services (EMS) dataset and a public electronic health record (EHR) dataset. Results show that DKEC outperforms the state-of-the-art label-wise attention networks and transformer models of different sizes, particularly for the few-shot classes. More importantly, it helps the smaller language models achieve comparable performance to large language models.
中摘: 医疗领域的多标签文本分类（MLTC）任务经常面临长尾标签分布问题。先前的工作探索了层次标签结构以找到少量样本类别的相关信息，但大多忽略了从医学指南中融入外部知识。本文提出了DKEC（领域知识增强分类），这是一种用于诊断预测的创新方法，包含两项革新：（1）从外部来源自动构建异构知识图谱，以捕捉不同医疗实体之间的语义关系；（2）通过标签级别的注意力机制将异构知识图谱融入少量样本分类中。我们使用三个在线医学知识源构建了DKEC，并在真实的紧急医疗服务（EMS）数据集和一个公开的电子健康记录（EHR）数据集上进行了评估。结果显示，DKEC在多标签文本分类和不同大小的变压器模型方面表现优于最先进的标签级别注意力网络，尤其是在少量样本类别上。更重要的是，它帮助较小的语言模型达到与大型语言模型相当的性能。

[No.415] Enabling Discriminative Reasoning in LLMs for Legal Judgment Prediction
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 在LLMs中启用区分性推理以预测法律判决
类别: 
作者: Chenlong Deng;Kelong Mao;Yuyao Zhang;Zhicheng Dou;
机构: Renmin University of China;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.43/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01964
英摘: Legal judgment prediction is essential for enhancing judicial efficiency. In this work, we identify that existing large language models (LLMs) underperform in this domain due to challenges in understanding case complexities and distinguishing between similar charges. To adapt LLMs for effective legal judgment prediction, we introduce the Ask-Discriminate-Predict (ADAPT) reasoning framework inspired by human judicial reasoning. ADAPT involves decomposing case facts, discriminating among potential charges, and predicting the final judgment. [No.416] Fine-Grained Prediction of Reading Comprehension from Eye Movements
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 基于眼动的细粒度阅读理解预测
类别: 
作者: Omer Shubi;Yoav Meiri;Cfir Avraham Hadar;Yevgeni Berzak;

英摘: Can human reading comprehension be assessed from eye movements in reading? In this work, we address this longstanding question using large-scale eyetracking data over textual materials that are geared towards behavioral analyses of reading comprehension. We focus on a fine-grained and largely unaddressed task of predicting reading comprehension from eye movements at the level of a single question over a passage. [No.417] Fine-Tuning Large Language Models for Stock Return Prediction Using Newsflow
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 使用新闻流 fine-tuning 大型语言模型进行股票回报预测
类别: 
作者: Tian Guo;Emmanuel Hauptmann;
机构: RAM Active Investments;
类型: Industry
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-industry.77/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.18103
英摘: Large language models (LLMs) and their fine-tuning techniques have demonstrated superior performance in various language understanding and generation tasks. This paper explores fine-tuning LLMs for stock return forecasting with financial newsflow. In quantitative investing, return forecasting is fundamental for subsequent tasks like stock picking, portfolio optimization, etc. We formulate the model to include text representation and forecasting modules. We propose to compare the encoder-only and decoder-only LLMs, considering they generate text representations in distinct ways. The impact of these different representations on forecasting performance remains an open question. Meanwhile, we compare two simple methods of integrating LLMs' token-level representations into the forecasting module. The experiments on real news and investment universes reveal that: (1) aggregated representations from LLMs' token-level embeddings generally produce return predictions that enhance the performance of long-only and long-short portfolios; (2) in the relatively large investment universe, the decoder LLMs-based prediction model leads to stronger portfolios, whereas in the small universes, there are no consistent winners. Among the three LLMs studied (DeBERTa, Mistral, Llama), Mistral performs more robustly across different universes; (3) return predictions derived from LLMs' text representations are a strong signal for portfolio construction, outperforming conventional sentiment scores.
中摘: 大型语言模型（LLMs）及其微调技术在各种语言理解和生成任务中表现出卓越的性能。本文探讨了使用金融新闻流对LLMs进行微调以进行股票回报预测的方法。在量化投资中，回报预测是后续任务如股票选择、组合优化等的基础。我们构建了包含文本表示和预测模块的模型，并提出比较仅编码器和仅解码器的LLMs，因为它们以不同的方式生成文本表示。这些不同表示形式对预测性能的影响仍是一个开放问题。同时，我们比较了两种简单的将LLMs的标记级表示集成到预测模块中的方法。实验结果显示：（1）LLMs标记级嵌入的聚合表示通常能够提高长仓和长仓短仓组合的表现；（2）在相对较大的投资组合中，基于解码器LLMs的预测模型能产生更强的组合表现，而在较小的投资组合中，没有一致的胜者；（3）在研究的三种LLMs（DeBERTa、Mistral、Llama）中，Mistral在不同规模的投资组合中表现更为稳健；（4）来自LLMs文本表示的回报预测是构建投资组合的强大信号，优于传统的 sentiment 分数。

[No.418] Hybrid-RACA: Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Composition Assistance for Real-time Text Prediction
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: Hybrid-RACA：混合检索增强组成辅助的实时文本预测
类别: 
作者: Menglin Xia;Xuchao Zhang;Camille Couturier;Guoqing Zheng;Saravan Rajmohan;Victor Rühle;

链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-industry.11/

英摘: Large language models (LLMs) enhanced with retrieval augmentation has shown great performance in many applications. However, the computational demands for these models pose a challenge when applying them to real-time tasks, such as composition assistance. To address this, we propose Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Composition Assistance (Hybrid-RACA), a novel system for real-time text prediction that efficiently combines a cloud-based LLM with a smaller client-side model through retrieval augmented memory. This integration enables the client model to generate better responses, benefiting from the LLM's capabilities and cloud-based data. 中摘: 增强检索辅助的大语言模型在许多应用中表现出色。然而，这些模型的计算需求在应用于实时任务（如文本辅助创作）时构成了挑战。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Composition Assistance (Hybrid-RACA)的新颖系统，该系统通过检索增强的记忆高效地结合了基于云的大语言模型和较小的客户端模型。这种集成使客户端模型能够生成更好的回应，从而受益于大语言模型的能力和基于云的数据。同时，通过一种新颖的异步记忆更新机制，客户端模型可以在无需等待云响应的情况下，实时提供完成内容给用户输入。我们在五个数据集上的实验表明，Hybrid-RACA在保持低延迟的同时提供了强大的性能。

[No.419] Incorporating Precedents for Legal Judgement Prediction on European Court of Human Rights Cases

链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.214/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18644
英摘: Inspired by the legal doctrine of stare decisis, which leverages precedents (prior cases) for informed decision-making, we explore methods to integrate them into LJP models. To facilitate precedent retrieval, we train a retriever with a fine-grained relevance signal based on the overlap ratio of alleged articles between cases. We investigate two strategies to integrate precedents: direct incorporation at inference via label interpolation based on case proximity and during training via a precedent fusion module using a stacked-cross attention model. We employ joint training of the retriever and LJP models to address latent space divergence between them. Our experiments on LJP tasks from the ECHR jurisdiction reveal that integrating precedents during training coupled with joint training of the retriever and LJP model, outperforms models without precedents or with precedents incorporated only at inference, particularly benefiting sparser articles.
中摘: 受制于法理学中的“遵循先例”（stare decisis）原则，该原则通过利用先前案例来支持明智的决策，我们探讨了如何将这些先例整合到LJP模型中。为了促进先例检索，我们训练了一个基于案件间涉嫌文章重叠比例的细粒度相关性信号的检索器。我们研究了两种整合先例的方法：一种是在推理时通过基于案件接近度的标签插值直接纳入先例；另一种是在训练过程中通过一个先例融合模块使用堆叠交叉注意力模型进行整合。我们采用检索器和LJP模型的联合训练来解决它们之间的潜在空间偏差问题。我们在欧洲人权法院（ECHR）管辖范围内的LJP任务实验中发现，结合训练过程中纳入先例与检索器和LJP模型的联合训练相比，没有纳入先例的模型或仅在推理时纳入先例的模型表现更优，特别是在处理稀疏文章方面。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.420] Language models and brains align due to more than next-word prediction and word-level information
来源: EMNLP 2025
命中: prediction
标题: 语言模型和大脑在预测下一个词和单词级信息之外存在更多的对齐之处
类别: 
作者: Gabriele Merlin;Mariya Toneva;
机构: Max Planck Institute for Software Systems;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.1024/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2212.00596
英摘: Pretrained language models have been shown to significantly predict brain recordings of people comprehending language. Recent work suggests that the prediction of the next word is a key mechanism that contributes to this alignment. What is not yet understood is whether prediction of the next word is necessary for this observed alignment or simply sufficient, and whether there are other shared mechanisms or information that are similarly important. In this work, we take a step towards understanding the reasons for brain alignment via two simple perturbations in popular pretrained language models. These perturbations help us design contrasts that can control for different types of information. By contrasting the brain alignment of these differently perturbed models, we show that improvements in alignment with brain recordings are due to more than improvements in next-word prediction and word-level information.
中摘: 预训练语言模型已被证明能够显著预测人们理解语言时的大脑记录。最近的研究表明，预测下一个单词可能是这种对齐的关键机制之一。但目前尚不清楚的是，这种下一个单词的预测是否是大脑对齐所必需的，还是仅仅足够，以及是否存在其他同样重要的共享机制或信息。在这项工作中，我们通过在流行的预训练语言模型中进行两种简单的扰动，朝着理解大脑对齐的原因迈出了一步。这些扰动帮助我们设计对比实验，以控制不同类型的信息。通过对比这些不同扰动模型的大脑对齐情况，我们发现，与大脑记录相比，对齐的改善不仅仅来自于下一个单词预测能力的提高和词级信息的优化。
